[p. b625]

bṛhatī

(śābarabhāṣyavyākhyā)

tṛtīyādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ

athātaśśeṣalakṣaṇam || 1 ||
1nānākarmalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamiti
 | karmānubandhabhedācchāstrabhedaḥ, ataḥ śāstra-
bhedalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamityarthaḥ | nanu ca śāstrabhede satyaidamarthyaṃ na
siddhayati aṅgapradhānānām | viniyogataḥ karmabhedaḥ uktaḥ |

[p. b626]

nanu cedānīmeva viniyogaścintyate | kathaṃ viniyogato bhedaḥ, bhede
sati viniyogaḥ | ata eva na bhedalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamiti hetvarthatayaiva
bhedalakṣaṇopanyāsaḥ śeṣalakṣaṇasya | satyaṃ, hetvarthatayaiva upanyāsaḥ
satyaṃ ca bhedādviniyogaḥ | tathāpi śāstrabhedo’pi viniyogata
eva | kintvasau viniyogaḥ padanimittakaḥ |

kimidaṃ padanimittaka iti ? pratipattau hetuḥ padanimittakaḥ
padārthānāṃ saṃbandhapratipattinimittaka ityarthaḥ | yastvayaṃ tṛtīye
cintyate sa kāryanimittakaḥ | ato vākyārthabhedena śāstrabhede
sati vākyārthānāṃ parasparaṃ śeṣaśeṣicintā śeṣalakṣaṇamityucyate
lakṣyate hi śeṣaśeṣipratipattau na pratipādyate1 | nanu vrīhīn

[p. b627]

prokṣatīti padārthānāṃ śeṣaśiṣicintā | aśravaṇamatrāparādhyate
bhavataḥ | vrīhibhiryajetetyanena sahāsya śeṣaśeṣicintā na puna-
rvrīhipadārthamātratayaiva | kimiti tarhi śeṣalakṣaṇaṃ ? viniyojyo
hi śeṣaḥ tadviṣayāṇi śrutyādīni tasmāccheṣalakṣaṇamityucyate |
śeṣiparijñānaṃ tarhi kinnimittaṃ ? pramāṇāntaranimittakaṃ tat |

[p. b628]

lakṣyate hi śeṣī śeṣapratipattau, na pratipādyate | ato yuktaṃ
śeṣalakṣaṇam |

yadyevaṃ viniyojyaḥ śeṣa iti vaktavyam | kimidaṃ śeṣaḥ parārtha-
tvāditi ? uktamasmābhiḥ padanimittako’pi viniyogo vidyata iti |
ataḥ kārye viniyojyaḥ śeṣaḥ kathaṃ syāditi śeṣaḥ parārthatvādityu-
ktam | yasya kārye viniyogaḥ sa tadartha eva bhavati | ataḥ
parārthatvāditi lakṣitam | na ca kāryaṃ parārthaṃ bhavitumarhati kāryameva
hi tanna syāt yadyanyārtham |

[p. b629]

ata eva garbhadāsadṛṣṭānto bhāṣye | nanu ca parārthatāmātraṃ parārtha-
śabdenocyate | kimidaṃ atyantaparārtha iti | ucyate | ata evā-
tyantaparārthaḥ yasmāt parārthatāmātrataivocyate | ataḥ sādhu vivṛtam |

[p. b630]

kaḥ śeṣaḥ kena hetunā śeṣaḥ1 iti
 | kimidaṃ praśnadvayaṃ ? kena hetunā śeṣa
ityetāvadvaktavyam | siddhayati hi parārthatvāccheṣa iti | ucyate,
syādetadevaṃ yadi siddhavadabhidhānaṃ hetoḥ syāt | heturapyatra kathanīya
evetyadoṣaḥ |

ata eva ko dharmaḥ kathaṃlakṣaṇaṃ ityuktam | kiṃ punaḥ siddhasya
kathane prayojanaṃ ? upayuktādanyaḥ śeṣa ityevamādinivṛttyartham |
kathaṃ ca viniyujyata 2iti ?
 ko’yaṃ praśnaḥ ? kena viniyujyata iti
vaktavyam, kimidaṃ kathaṃ ca viniyujyata iti |

[p. b631]

atrābhidhīyate | kena viniyujyata ityucyamāne śrutyādivyā-
pāra eva kevalaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ kathitaśca bhavati | na punaḥ saviniyogānāṃ
śrutyādīnāṃ, na ca niyogaśūnyāni śrutyādīni śeṣatāmāpādayituṃ
śaknuvanti | kāryābhāvāt | kāryanimittaṃ ca parārthamityuktam | ataḥ
kathaṃ ca viniyujyata ityuktaṃ sahetukaviniyogasiddhayartham | ata eva
ca śrutyādīni viniyoge kāraṇānītyuktam | na punaḥ śrutyādibhiriti |
kathamityanuṣaṅgaḥ | teṣāṃ balavadabalavattetyanenāpi kathaṃ cetyanuṣaktavyam |

[p. b632]

eṣa tāvat lakṣaṇārthaḥ | naitāvatā vinā śeṣaparijñānasiddhiḥ | ata
evānyadapi sādhanasiddhayarthaṃ kiñcidupoddhātādi्nā śeṣaḥ parārtha-
tvāditi sūtraṃ sabhāṣyaṃ vyākhyātam |

1atha tatra kiṃ bṛttam ? yaistu dravyaṃ cikīrṣyate guṇastatra pratīyeteti
 |
uktaṃ taccheṣalakṣaṇaṃ manvānasyaitaccodyam | uttaraṃ tu niyoga-
viṣayatā tatrāpanītā śeṣatvaṃ tu ihaiva sarveṣāṃ pratipādyate |
ko’yaṃ viniyogaviṣayatvāpanhavaḥ ? na ca viniyogaśūnyāni
śrūtyādīni śeṣatayā viniyoktuṃ śaknuvantītyuktam |

[p. b633]

tasmāccheṣatvaṃ viniyogāpahnavaśceti vipratiṣiddham | ataḥ śeṣatvaṃ
vā nāstyapūrvaviṣayatā vā nāpahnotuṃ śakyate | ucyate---anabhijño
bhavān viṣayaniyojyānām | na brūmo guṇabhūteṣu pusāṃ niyojyatā
nāstīti | kiṃ tarhi ? viṣayastu na bhavatīti| kāryāntarasya darśanāt|
yasya punarniyogasiddhireva kāryaḥ sa viṣayaḥ ‘teṣāmarthena’
 ityatna prakaṭa-
tarameva vakṣyāmaḥ | ato yuktamuktaṃ tatrāpūrvārthatā vyāvartiteti |1

[p. b634]

dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣu bādariḥ || 3 ||1
kaḥ punaratrāsyābhiprāyo dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣveva pārārthyaṃ vadataḥ ?
ayamabhiprāyaḥ---vibhaktibhiḥ tādarthyaṃ prātipadikārthasya pratipādyate |
tacca dravyaguṇayorupalabhyate ekahāyanyā krīṇātīti aruṇayeti
ca | saṃskārasya tu kathaṃ ? tatrāpi vibhaktyaiva vrīhin prokṣati
vrīhīnavahanti iti | nanu ca prātipadikārthasya prādhānyaṃ
dvitīyā pratipādayati na karmaṇo guṇabhāvam | akārakajño bhavān |
kārakavibhaktayo hi kathaṃ kriyānirapekṣāḥ | tasmāt kriyā-
nirapekṣameva prātipadikārthasya prādhānyaṃ guṇatvaṃ vā | ata
eva ca vibhaktividhāne bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ kārakoddeśaḥ |

[p. b635]

kathaṃ tarhi taddhitādiṣu tādarthyāvagatiḥ kathaṃ copapadavibhaktayaḥ |
ato’nyadapi vibhaktivyatiriktaṃ pārārthye pramāṇaṃ dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣu
vaktavyam |

nanu copakāradarśanameva dravyaguṇasaṃskārāṇāṃ pārārthye heturiti
bādarermatam | kimatra vibhaktyādibhi kriyate | ucyate---yadyu-
pakāradarśanaṃ pārārthye hetuḥ brīhayo’pi prokṣaṇasyopakārakāḥ syuḥ | atha
prayojanaṃ prati upakāradarśanaṃ hetuḥ, prayojanavattājñānamidānīṃ kutaḥ ?

[p. b636]

niyogata iti cet | evaṃ tarhi niyoganirvṛtireva pārārthye
hetuḥ kimidaṃ upakāradarśanaṃ kariṣyati | ata eva ca
vibhaktivyatyayo’pi prayājaśeṣeṇetyevamādiṣu siddho bhavati |
kṛttaddhitasamāsāśca tadvaśenaiva aidamarthyapratipādakā bhavanti | eva-
mapyupapadavibhakterasaṅkaraḥ siddho bhavati |

kimiti tarhi bādariḥ dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣveva śeṣabhāvaṃ
manyate yadi niyoganirvṛttiḥ pārārthye hetuḥ |

[p. b637]

na niyogaṃ phale puruṣe ca manyate yāga eva niyogaḥ
tannirvṛttyā ca niyoganirvṛttiḥ | dravyaguṇasaṃskāraiśca yāgo
nirvartyate | ataḥ teṣāmeva pārārthyaṃ evaṃ manyate bādarirā-
cāryaḥ | nanu svargaṃ yāgena kuryāṃdātmārthaṃ iti niyogaḥ |
ātmanepadaprayogāt | naivaṃ śabdāt pratipattuṃ śakyate dvitīyā hi
kārakasya prādhānyaṃ pratipādayati | yasyāḥ kriyāyāḥ tatkārakam |

[p. b638]

na ca svarge yāgasya kārakamiti siddham | na ca
vedāt kriyākārakasaṃbandhāvagatiḥ | siddhasya hi anuṣṭhānaṃ
codyate | kathaṃ tarhi yadāhavanīye juhvatīti ? yuktaṃ
pramāṇāntareṇa saṃbhavadarśanāt | svarge punarnaitadupapadyate vedasyā-
prāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt | kiñca kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ cātra svarga(kāma)śabdaḥ
na kartṛviśeṣaṇamīpsitatamaṃ bhavati rājapuruṣaṃ paśyetivat |

[p. b639]

ato na svargaṃ kuryāditi niyogaviṣayatā śakyate pratipattum |
kathaṃ tarhi svargakāmapadasaṃbandhāt svargaṃ bhāvayediti bhāṣyam | eva-
metadbhāṣyaṃ anyathā hi svargamiti śravaṇāt svargaṃ bhāvayediti syāt |

[p. b640]

ata eva svayameva tadbhavatītyetadabhiprāyaṃ tat | nimittamātra-
kathanaṃ hi tat karmaṇaḥ | ataḥ prayojanaṃ svargo yāgasya na
karma pākasyeva grāsaḥ | ato dravyaguṇasaṃskārāṇāmeva sādhya-
sādhanabhāvāvagateḥ prayojanavattayā śeṣabhāvaḥ | na punaḥ karmatve-
nāpratīyamānayoḥ phalapuruṣayorapi syāt | tasmāt sūktaṃ
dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣu bādariḥ iti | 1na cet phalayāgau guṇabhāvena codyete
kasya puruṣaḥ pradhānabhūto bhaviṣyatīti
 | ko’yaṃ prādhānyapratiṣedhaḥ
śeṣatā hi puruṣasya pratiṣedhyā “na yāgaphalapuruṣeṣu”
 iti
pratijñānāt | ucyate | śeṣatānivṛttyarthameva prādhānyaṃ
pratiṣidhyate | kathaṃ ? sati hi prādhānye puruṣaḥ śeṣa iti

[p. b641]

śakyate vaktum | kimiti ? puruṣaśabdo hyayaṃ svargakāme prayuktaḥ
yadi tadarthaḥ svargaṃ syāt tadā tadviśiṣṭasya śeṣatā syāt |
svadravyaviśiṣṭasyeva prayājādeḥ | atādarthye punaḥ svargasya kevalaṃ
kartṛtayaiva śeṣabhāvaḥ dravyamātratayetyarthaḥ na puruṣaśeṣatayā | na
svargakāmapadavācyatayetyabhiprāyaḥ | tasmādanavadyam |

karmāṇyapi jaiminiḥ phalārthatvāt1 || 4 ||
kathaṃ phalārthatā karmaṇaḥ ? kāmino hi adhikāro yāge | sa
ca phalasādhanatāṃ yāgasyānāpādayan na siddhayatīti ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ |

[p. b642]

yadi punarayaṃ yāgaḥ phale sādhanabhāvaṃ na pratipadyate tatra ko doṣaḥ |
na khalu kaścit | pratīyate tu tathā | nanu śeṣalakṣaṇe śeṣatā
pratipādayituṃ nyāyyā, kimidamucyate 1enamevārthaṃ ṣaṣṭhe sūtraireva sādhayiṣyati

iti, kiṃ vā śrutyādivyatiriktaṃ kiñcidanyat śeṣatve pramāṇamasti |
atha śrutyādīnāmanyatamaṃ tadihaivopanyasanīyaṃ kimiti sānyāsikaṃ
kriyate | atrocyate---adhikārāpekṣiṇī hi sā na śrutyapekṣo’dhikāra
iti sānyāsikaṃ yāgaphalaśeṣatvaṃ yuktam |

[p. b643]

phalaṃ ca puruṣārthatvāt || 5 ||1
phalamapi puruṣaṃ pratyupadiśyate yaḥ svargo me syādityevaṃ
kāmayate tasya, niyogaḥ na svargaḥ ātmānaṃ labheteti | kutaḥ
ātmanepadaprayogāt |2

nanu ca svargakāmaśabdādevedamavagamyate svargo me bhavediti |
kimidamātmanepadaprayogāditi | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ parasya duḥkhaṃ
kāmayata ityatrāpi kameḥ prayogenānātmasaṃbandhitvenāpi dṛṣṭa iti
tadayuktam, tatra paraśabdaprayogāt duḥ khasya ca parasambandhitve-
nābhīṣṭatvāt | yatra punaḥ paraśabdo na śrūyate nāpyaniṣṭaṃ duḥ khaṃ tatra
kenānyena kāmyamānasya saṃbandho’nyatra kāmayituḥ |

[p. b644]

atha manyase kāmyamānenaiva kāmaḥ saṃbadhyate svargo bhavatviti |
na hi; tannibandhanasukhāsmaraṇāt | na kasyacit (kāmamātreṇa)
sadbhāvamātre kasyacitkāmo bhavati | tasmādātmanepadopanyāso
liṅgatayā pratipattavyaḥ na punaḥ prāpte pramāṇatayā |

puruṣaśca karmārthatvāt1 || 6 ||
svargaścetpuruṣārthaḥ svargakāmapadavācyatayaiva ‘yajamānena saṃmitau-
dumbarī bhavati’
 ityādiṣu kartuḥ śeṣatvaṃ kathaṃ ? adhikṛtasya kartṛtvāt |

[p. b645]

kartuśca yajamānaśabdavācyatvāt | tasmāt sūktaṃ sūtratrayaṃ
jaimineḥ vṛttikāramatopanyāsaḥ pūrvapakṣāparījñānārthaḥ | kimiti
kalpāntaraṃ na bhavati | na hi āpekṣikaṃ śeṣatvamasti | viniyo-
jyanibandhanaṃ hi tat | viniyoga evāpekṣika iti | satyam | śeṣaḥ
viniyojyaṃ śeṣiṇaṃ cāpekṣite na punarekasya śeṣaśeṣitāmāpādayati |

[p. b646]

vrīhyādīnāṃ ca svaśeṣāpekṣatayā tulyatvāt | tasmāt svakauśala-
pradarśanārthaṃ paramatamupanyastaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa |

teṣāmarthena saṃbandhaḥ1 || 7 ||
uktaṃ niyogasavyapekṣāṇi śrutyādīni viniyojakāni
śāstrāntaravihitasaṃbandhitayā ca kāryalābha ityuktam | śāstrā-
ntaravihitaṃ(yuktaṃ) hi śeṣi bhavati na śeṣaśāstrādeva prādhānyamava-
gamyata iti saktuṣu darśitam | śāstrāntaravihitāni ca vrīhyādīni

[p. b647]

kathaṃ tatsuṃyukteṣu saṃśayaḥ | kāryaśūnye hi śeṣaśāstraṃ kalpyaṃ kārya-
pamekṣate | pratyakṣaśrutakāryaṃ tu sandihyata ityayuktam | ucyate |
niyogādanyat viniyogagamyaṃ kāryamastīti na śakyate vaktum |

nanu ca ‘yaistu dravyaṃ cikīrṣyate’
 iti nāpūrvaṃ
kāryamityuktam | satyam | sthite etasmin śeṣatve tat | śeṣa-
tvamevedānīṃ niyoganirvṛttyā vinā nāvagamyate ityākṣepaḥ |
kimiti vā na niyogoniṣpādyate vrīhīn prokṣatīti |
yadyatra niyogārthaniṣpattiḥ, darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityanena na

[p. b648]

saṃbadhyeta | atha ‘phalavatsannidhau’
 iti saṃbandhaḥ sa tu niyogaikya-
lakṣaṇa iti pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ | atrāpicet sa eva saṃbandhaḥ
na tarhi vrīhīn prokṣatītyetāvataiva niyogasiddhiḥ | ataḥ
tuṣakaṇavipramocanaṃ na svargāvāptivat nāntarīyako niyogasya |

evaṃ tarhi ko’yamupanyāsaḥ ‘auṣadhe sānnāyye ca karmavyā’1

iti | naiva hi padārthānāṃ prādhānyamasmin pakṣe | ucyate---naiva hi

[p. b649]

padārthānāṃ prādhānyaparo’yaṃ granthaḥ kiṃ tarhi sarveśeṣatābhiprāyaparaḥ |
nanu cāsminnapi pakṣe śeṣaśeṣitaiva nopapadyate aṅgapradhānām |
sarvaśeṣāṇāṃ niyogārthaśeṣatvāt | prakaraṇapramāṇasvarūpanirūpaṇe’sya
codyasya parihāraṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | iha tu kāryāntarāsaṃbhavāta
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmityanena asaṃbandhādityuktam |
tasmāt prayājādīnāmeva niyogo hyetallakṣaṇaḥ sarveṣā-
mabhisaṃbandhaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi vrīhīn ājyaṃ iti ca | asmin
pakṣe saktuvadetadbhaviṣyati | gāṃ dadāti itivadvā |

[p. b650]

ata idamuktam | 1avadhātādayastu padārthāḥ śrutyā vidhīyanta
 iti |
bhāvārthānāṃ śrutyā niyogasaṃbandhaścetyarthaḥ | vākyenaiṣāṃ vrīhyādi-
saṃbandha iti bhāvārthānupraveśena dravyādīnāṃ tu niyogasaṃbandha
ityarthaḥ na pradhānabhūtānāṃ, na hi dravyaparyanto niyogaḥ
svaya2mevasādhyatvāt tasmāt sūktaṃ sarve sarvārthā iti |

[p. b651]

kimidamadṛṣṭaṃ bhavatīti | adṛṣṭamapūrvaṃ niyoga ityekamida-
mityuktaṃ ‘ato’pūrvaṃ kṛtvā’
iti |

phalaṃ tu saha ceṣṭayā śabdārtho’bhāvādviprayoge syāt || 10 ||
atrocyate | syāt sarvārthatā yadi hi kāryārthe virodho
niyogasya syāt | na tvatra virodho vidyate | kathaṃ na vidyate
virodhaḥ ? kathaṃ vā vidyate virodhaḥ | uktaṃ kāryāntaratvāt tuṣakaṇa-
vipramokasyeti | na ca kāryaṃ kāryāntare saṃbadhyate yathā svargaḥ
paśubhiḥ evaṃ niyogaḥ kāryaṃ tuṣakaṇavipramokaśceti viprati-
ṣiddham | anabhijño bhavān viṣayaniyojyānāmityuktam |

[p. b652]

kathaṃ anabhijñaḥ ? viṣayo hi 1nāmādhikārasaṃbandhī svargakāmo
yajeta iti | tasya ca niyogasiddhireva prayojanam | dṛṣṭe’rthe
saṃbhavatyapi yathā godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayediti |

nanu cātrāpi phalasiddhiḥ; niyogasiddhayarthā phalasiddhiḥ
iti ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | ata eva ca ‘karmāṇyapi jaiminiḥ phalārthatvāt’

[p. b653]

‘phalaṃ ca puruṣārthatvāt’
 ‘puruṣaśca karmārthatvāt’
 iti viṣaya-
niyoga1 eva upasaṃhṛtaṃ sakalasya padārthajātasya śeṣatvam |
niyojyaḥ idānīṃ kaḥ ? akārakabhūtapadārthaviśiṣṭa iti ṣaṣṭhe
vakṣyāmaḥ | kaḥ punarasau ? adhikṛtaḥ karmaṇi ? kimidaṃ adhikṛtaṃ
iti ? nanu ca kartaivādhikṛtaḥ | yathā na karturadhikāro’dhi-
kṛtasya kartṛtvamiti ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | yadyevaṃ kimāyātaṃ vrīhīn
prokṣatītyatra | kāryāntarepi niyogāvirodha itīdamevāyātam |

[p. b654]

vrīhīnprokṣatīti pradhānabhūtaviṣayasaṃsparśānniyogasya na niyogāntarā-
vagatiḥ samidādīnāmiva | nanu kāryāntaramityuktam | kāryā-
ntaratve ca niyogāntarāvagatiḥ someṣṭīnāmiva | satyaṃ
yadi kāryāntaraṃ syāt | kathaṃ na kāryāntaraṃ ? adhikāra-
niyogākṣepāt taṇḍulaniṣpatteḥ svargasiddhivanna kāryāntaram |

[p. b655]

tasmādadhikāraniyoga evāyaṃ vrīhīn prokṣati iti prayājādī-
nāmiva | iyān viśeṣaḥ dṛṣṭakāryadvārakastvayaṃ sākṣānniyoga-
kārya eva prayājādiḥ na ca dvārameva pratibandhakaṃ bhavitumarhati |
viśeṣato dṛṣṭasākṣānniyogasaṃbandhānāmapi dvāraṃ kalpyam | idaṃ ca
klṛptamiti viśeṣaḥ | tathā ca dvārakalpanāmantareṇa pradhāneti-
kartavyatāsiddhirna bhavati tathā prakaraṇapramāṇa eva vakṣyāmaḥ | tadida-
muktaṃ pramāṇābhāvānna sarvārthateti | na hi dṛṣṭe saṃbhavati
saṃbandhahetau kalpyaṃ hetvantaramātmānaṃ labhate ityarthaḥ |
nanu ca viniyogataḥ kāryaṃ, na kāryato viniyogaḥ

[p. b656]

parihṛtametat śāstrāntaradṛṣṭaviṣayatvāditi | ata eva bhedā-
bhedalakṣaṇaṃ heturiti | tasmāt sūktaṃ phalaṃ tu saha ceṣṭayeti |
kāryānurodhī niyoga ityarthaḥ | ato ye yatra śrūyanta
iti siddham |

dravyaṃ cotpattisaṃyogāt tadarthameva codyeta || 11 || a. 5 ||
idaṃ cintyate sphayaśca kapālāni ceti sannidhāpanavidhayaḥ
saṃyogato’viśeṣāt prakaraṇāviśeṣācceti kiṃ sarvārthatayā’vagamyante
uta ‘sphayenoddhanti’
 ityevamādibhiḥ upasaṃhriyanta iti | kiṃ
tāvada्yuktaṃ ? nopasaṃhriyanta iti | kutaḥ ? aśakteḥ | kathamaśaktiḥ ?

[p. b657]

sannidhāne hi viniyogaikyāt dvāramātrānujñānācca dṛṣṭadvāra-
katvameva śakyate vaktum | kalpyapratipakṣatvāt | na punaḥ
dṛṣṭaviśeṣadvāramapi | tasmāt śaktito vyavasthāḥ | atrābhidhīyate |
syādetadevaṃ yadi dvāramātramevānurudhyate niyogena (tataḥ) saṃbandhānu-
sārī hi niyogaḥ | (kathaṃ tarhi aruṇādyupanyāsena nirākaraṇaṃ
āsthite guṇavacanatve aruṇādivadbhaviṣyati vākye niyoga
iti |) uktaṃ śāstrāntaravihitaviṣayatvānniyogasyeti | ataḥ
sannidhāpanavidhayo na saṃbandhavidheḥ pratipakṣāḥ bhavitumarhanti tanmūla-
tvāt iti | ataḥ siddhamatrāpi ye yatra śrūyanta iti |

[p. b658]

arthaikatve dravyaguṇayoraikakarmyānniyamaḥ syāta1 || 12 || a. 6 ||
saṃskāradravyavidhayo ye yatra śrūyante te tatraivetyuktam | guṇe
idānīṃ cintyate---kiṃ ye yatra śrūyante tatraiva uta kṛtsne prakaraṇe
niveśa iti | nanu ca anvitapadārthakatvāt padānāṃ naiva kevalaguṇa-
vacanatā saṃbhavati yathā na kevalākṛtivacanatā ‘ālabheta’
 iti na
kevalaguṇavacanatā śakyate vaktuṃ yathā ‘vāyavyaṃ śvetamālabheta’
 iti |
satyaṃ aparityaktadravyasyaivānvayo yatra dṛśyate amūrtasya naiva
tadudāharaṇam | yatra punaḥ parityaktadravyābhiprāye kevalaguṇavacana-
syānvayaḥ yathā paṭasya śukla iti bhavati tatra saṃśayaḥ | kāraka-
vibhaktau satyāmapi kiṃ kriyānvayī guṇavacana uta kevaladravyā-
nvayyeveti |

[p. b659]

nanu ca kevalaguṇavacanatā asyāsmin vākye naiva pratīyate
paṭasya śukla iti(vat) ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśāt dravyasya tena saṃbandhāvagateśca
śuklaśabdasya yuktaṃ kevalaguṇavacanasyānvayitvam | iha punaḥ
aruṇayeti kārakanirdeśāt kriyāsaṃbandhāvagatiḥ | tadava-
gateśca guṇamātrasyāśakteḥ aparityaktadravyasyaivānvayo yuktaḥ
śvetamālabheteti vat | ucyate | na dravyasambandhā-
vagatireva tatra guṇavacanatve hetuḥ dravyāntarānavagatirapi
dravyāntarāvagatau hi paṭasya śuklo bhāga itivat guṇaguṇinoḥ
saṃbandho na syāt | yadyevaṃ kimāyātaṃ aruṇayeti ?

[p. b660]

idamāyātam | atrāpi ekahāyanyā na dravyāntaraṃ pratīyate |
ataḥ ekahāyanīśabdopāttatvāt dravyasya nāruṇāśabdaḥ dravyā-
ntaropaniviṣṭaṃ guṇamabhidhātuṃ śaknoti | ekahāyanyupaniveśe ca
na krayasambandha ityupariṣṭādvakṣyate | tasmāt padāntaropāttadravyake
vākye kevalaguṇavacanānvayitaiveti sthitam | ata upapadyate
saṃśayaḥ | kiṃ vākyaṃ bhitvā prakaraṇe niveśo guṇasya dravyayogitvāt
uta karaṇanirdeśāt krayaṇa eveti | kathamevaṃ sthite guṇavacanatve “1nanu
naivāyaṃ guṇavacanaḥ, kiṃ tarhi ? dravyavacanaḥ
 ityākṣepaḥ upapadyate | ucyate---strī-
liṅganirdeśāt, dravyāśritatvāt | prātipadikārthagatatvācca liṅgasya |

[p. b661]

kimaruṇayetyevamādidarśanaṃ nāstyākṣepavādinaḥ | na nāstidarśanaṃ,
kintu aruṇayetyevamādiṣu netyabhiprāyaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi aruṇāda्yu-
panyāsena nirākaraṇaṇam ? aruṇādiṣu prayogasādhuteti | kathamaruṇā-
valliṅgamiti dravyavacanatānirākaraṇam | sthite guṇavacanatve
dṛḍhatareṇa kāraṇena aruṇāvalliṅgaṃ bhaviṣyatītyabhiprāyaḥ “atha krīṇātinā
na sambadhyeta tasminneva vākye ekahāyanīśabdena sambhantsyate na bhaviṣyati vākyabheda”

iti vadataḥ ko’bhiprāyaḥ | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---yadā dvayoḥ kriyā-
sambandhaḥ tadā dravyāntaranirodhāt kevalaguṇavacanatā aruṇayeti |
yadā punaḥ kriyāsambandho nāsti tadā āśritatvāda्guṇānāṃ 
dravyāṇāṃ ca tadāśrayatvena guṇānāṃ ko nāma dravyasaṃbandhaṃ
niruṇāddhi yathā nīlotpalamiti | atrottaram---prātipadikāttā-

[p. b662]
 
vata guṇa eva pratīyate padāntarasaṃbandhāttu aparityaktadravya-
ko’nvayaḥ | liṅgaṃ ca vākyādbalīyaḥ | kimidaṃ vākyālliṅgaṃ
balīya iti | aidamarthye hi balābalamuktam na punaranvayayo-
ge’pi | ucyate | anvayayoge ca anvayāvagatau satyāṃ kena
balābalaṃ nivāryate | uktaṃ cāsmābhiḥ | padāntareṇa dravyopādānāt
kriyāsaṃbandhācca padadvayasya kevalaguṇānvayitā na śakyā vākyena
bādhitum | atredānīmuttaram---kriyāyogitve’nanvayamālocya
ananvayādvaraṃ dravyānvayaḥ dravyānvayitvepi kriyānvayo nātidūraṃ
parityakto bhavati |

[p. b663]

avaśyaṃ caitadevābhyupagantavyam | anyathā hi prakaraṇasaṃbandho-
’pi na syāt prakaraṇe hi kriyayā dravyeṇa vā anvayaḥ | kriyayā
tāvannasaṃbandha ityuktam | atha liṅgāt dravyeṇāpi na saṃbadhyate
hanta ananvayitā’bhyupagatābhavati | na ca ananvayitā abhi-
dhānasya dharmo loke dṛṣṭaḥ | tasmādvaramasminneva vākye dravya-
saṃbandhābhyupagamaḥ | ata eva nīlotpalādiṣvapyanvayo(na)
dṛśyate | anyathā hi saṃbandhābhidhānavibhaktiśūnyānāmapi kathaṃ
vānvayaḥ syāt 1“naitadevamiti”

 | satyameva vaibhaktiko’pi
saṃbandhaḥ abhidhānalakṣaṇo bhavati yadyapratipakṣo bhavet
ayaṃ tu sapratipakṣaḥ | ataḥ tṛtīyānuguṇamevāsyānvayitvaṃ

[p. b664]

kalpayitavyam, na punaḥ kevalābhidhānānuguṇam | tasmāt na
nīlotpalavat saṃbandha upapadyate | ucyate | yadi tṛtīyārthaḥ
prātipadikārthānvayavirodhī syāt syādetadevam | sa punaḥ prāptatvānnaiva
virudhyate | ato yāvadaprāptamekahāyanyā saṃbadhyata iti na doṣaḥ |
bhavedetadevaṃ yadyekahāyanī nānyārthā, sāpi tvanyārthaiva | na ca
dvayoranyārthayoḥ mithaḥ saṃbandhaḥ saṃbhavati | kimarthā punarekāhayanī ?
krayārtheti brūmaḥ | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ tulye samabhivyāhāre krayārthā
ekahāyanī na punaḥ guṇasaṃbandhārthā | atra bhāṣyaṃ “abhihitamapi
punaḥ paryanuyujyata”
 iti | yadyabhihitaṃ kimiti paryanuyujyate |
anyārthasyānyeva saṃbandhaḥ virodhapradarśanārthaṃ tadarthamevottaraṃ tṛtīyā-

[p. b665]

saṃbandhāt krayārthateti | anyārthamapyanyena saṃbadhyata iti na
doṣaḥ | śaknoti hi ākāṅkṣāvataḥ anyārthamapi sannihitamā-
kāṅkṣāṃ pūrayitum sannidhānāviśeṣāt | satyaṃ, śaknoti na puna
ryadarthaṃ tatsaṃbandhitayaiva | nanu ca tatsaṃbandhinyapi sannidhānama-
viśiṣṭam itarasya cākāṅkṣā | bhavedetadevaṃ yadi sannidhānamaviśiṣṭaṃ
iha tu sannidhānamapyanyārtham | na cānyārthaṃ sannidhānamākāṅkṣati
svārthasiddhaye | satyamevam | tasyāvaśyānvayabhāvitvāt |
abhidhānasya cānyasyāsannidhānāt anyārthatāmavakṛṣya
sannidhānamātraṃ parigṛhyate | tathā ca rātrisatrādiṣu dṛṣṭam |

[p. b666]

yuktamuktam, kintvatra prakaraṇāt sannidhānamananyārthaṃ vidyate |
tasmādvākyabhedādevānvayo yuktaḥ ananyārthasya saṃbandhino’vagamāt
ataḥ prakaraṇe sarvasminniveśa ityevaṃ prāpte brūmaḥ |

arthaikatve dravyaguṇayoraikakarmyānniyamaḥ syāt || 12 ||
kraya evāruṇimā niviśate | kathaṃ ? tatsaṃbandhapratipatteḥ tṛtī-
yātaḥ | nanu kevalaguṇavacanatvāt na kriyayā saṃbadhyata ityuktam |
duruktaṃ tat | kathaṃ duruktam ? yasya hi yādṛśaṃ kriyāsādhanatvaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ

[p. b667]

tasya tathāvidhasyaiva vibhaktayaḥ saṃbandhamāpādayanti | dravyaviśeṣaṇa-
tayā ca guṇaḥ kriyāṃ niṣpādayati | aviśiṣṭasya kāraṇatvānu-
papatteḥ | nanu cānupāttadravyaka evāyaṃ prayogaḥ paṭasya śukla
iti vadityuktam | tatsatyamuktaṃ na punaranupāttadravyatayā kriyā-
saṃbandho’pahnotuṃ śakyate | svavyāpārāpekṣitvāt kriyāyogasya |
na cānupāttadravyatayā vyāpārahāniḥ śakyate vaktum | svavyā-
pārāpekṣāṇi kārakāṇi kriyayā saṃbadhyante iti na kārakāntara-
vyāpāramapekṣante | nāpyarthāntaragataṃ hānamupādānaṃ vā’bhidhānasya |

[p. b668]

tasmādbhinnavyāpārakatvādekahāyanīdvāraka eva krayasaṃbandha upapannaḥ |
ata eva ca na vikalpaḥ prayojanavada्dravyāntara---
saṃbandhārthaṃ kriyāyāḥ prādhānyam | tatprādhānye tu na pratyekaṃ
vākyaparisamāptiḥ | tasmādekavākyatvātsiddhaṃ dravyaguṇayoraikakarmyā-
nniyama iti |

ekatvayuktamekasya śrutisaṃyogāt1 || 3-4-13 || (a. 7)
ukto dravyaguṇasaṃskārāṇāṃ viniyogaḥ | idānīṃ viniyojya-
viṣayagatā cintā kriyate | evaṃ śrūyate ‘grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭi’
 iti |

[p. b669]

tatra saṃśayaḥ---kiṃ śoṣiṇo viśeṣaṇaṃ śoṣitve ādriyate śeṣasyeva śeṣa-
tve uta neti | kathaṃ punaratra saṃśayaḥ ? yuktaṃ nāvaccheṣaḥ upādīyamāna-
tvāt tadupayogiviśeṣaṇam | śeṣiṇaḥ punaḥ kāryāntare nirjñātaśeṣa-
bhāvasyaiva svaśeṣayogo yukta iti na saṃśayaṃ manyāmahe | ucyate |
śrutitaḥ sarvaṃ kalpate nopādeyatvāt paśorekatvam, kiṃ tarhi ?
śruteḥ | śrutiścennādriyate dvitvaṃ bahutvaṃ vā kimiti śoṣitayā nopā-
dīyate | ataḥ śeṣiṇyapi yathaiva grahaśrutirādriyate evamekatvaśrutirapi
aviśeṣāt | tadidamuktaṃ---‘ekatvayuktamekasya śrutisaṃyogādi’
ti |

[p. b670]

ata eva ca ‘agnīnādadhīta’
 iti bahutvasiddhiḥ yūpaṃ chinatti’
iti ca ekatvam |

sarveṣāṃ vā lakṣaṇatvādaviśiṣṭaṃ hi lakṣaṇam || 14 ||
atrocyate---naitadevaṃ yadekasya saṃskāra iti | saṃskāro hi nāma
vyāpārāntarayogyatā | yasya saṃskāro vyāpārāntarayogyatālakṣaṇaḥ
saṃbhavati sa eva saṃbandhitāmāpadyate | nanu ca sasaṅkhyāka
eva saṃskāreṇa vyāpārāntarayogyaḥ kriyate | kutaḥ ? vyāpārāntaraṃ
upādānaśeṣatvāt saṅakhyāyā liṅgasya ca | yathābhūtaścāsau śeṣaḥ
tathābhūtasyaiva saṃskārasaṃbandhaḥ kāryārthatvāt | aindravāyavādi-

[p. b671]

vākyeṣu ca ekatvayuktā eva grahāḥ śeṣatvena śrūyante | ato-
’vivakṣitamityayuktam | na ca grahamātraṃ śeṣaḥ ekaikasya
viniyogāt |

nanu viniyuktasyopādānaśeṣatvaṃ saṅkhyādayaḥ pratipadyante yathā
ca śeṣabhāve prādhānyaṃ tathā śeṣibhāvepi | bhavatu prādhānyaṃ na
kaścidvirodhaḥ saṅkhyādyaparityāgena tu yathaiva śeṣatāṃ pratipadyate tathaiva
saṃskāryatāmapīti vadamāḥ | atrocyate---satyaṃ śeṣabhūtasyaiva saṃskāraḥ
tathāpi saṅkhyādayo na saṃbadhyante | kutaḥ ? sarvatra hi padārthāntara-
saṃbandhaḥ pradhānasyaiva rājapuruṣamānayetivat | tatra hi rājārthaḥ

[p. b672]

kevalamupalakṣaṇatayaiva bhāti | evamatrāpi dravyameva pradhānaṃ, yadanyatta-
dupalakṣaṇaṃ jātyādi | na ca upalakṣaṇasya kāryasaṃbandhaḥ | ata
idamuktaṃ aviśiṣṭaṃ hi lakṣaṇamiti | nahyekatvadīnāṃ vilakṣaṇatve
grahāntaranivṛttau sāmarthyamasti saṃbandhī hi saṃbandhyantaraṃ nivartayati |
nanu coktaṃ na saṃbandhimātraṃ(tā) saṃbandhī hetuḥ kastarhi ? śrutyādi-
pratipādyateti | atra caikatvaviśiṣṭaḥ śrutyā pratipādyate śeṣī |
kathaṃ ca śakyate vaktum śrutipramāṇake vastuni ekatvamaviva-
kṣitamiti | na coddiśyamānatvaṃ vidhīyamānatvaṃ vā śrutiṃ vihanti |
śrutividhāte hi grahepyaprāptiḥ syāt | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ uddiśya-
mānasya viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamiti | asyaivārthasyāsminnadhikaraṇe
heturvaktavyaḥ na cāsāvastītyuktamiti | ucyeta---ayamasti hetuḥ

[p. b673]

kāryavaduddiśyamānaṃ pradhānībhavati | pradhānasya vidheyena saṃbandho’tra
na pradhānasya guṇānāṃ, yasmāt guṇānāṃ pradhānopādānanimittaṃ
tadupādānaśeṣatvam |

ke punaramī guṇāḥ ye vibhaktyabhidheyāḥ prātipadikārthasya |
kathaṃ guṇatā vibhaktyarthānāṃ ? prātipadikārthaṃ pratyavacchedakatvāt |
vibhaktyartho hi prātipādikārthamavacchinatti | avacchedakatayā ca
upādānaśeṣatā | tasmāt siddhaṃ saṅkhyādeḥ dravyaṃ prati guṇatvaṃ
vibhaktyabhidheyasya | na ca guṇānāṃ guṇaiḥ saha saṃbandho
vidyate guṇaścāyaṃ saṃskāro nāma | uktaṃ ca guṇānāṃ ca
parārthatvāt” iti | athāpi nāma prātipādikābhidheya
evāyamartho jātyādiḥ viniyojyaśca | tathāpyanuddiśyamāno
naiva pradhānatāṃ śaknoti pratipattum | kimiti ? uddeśohi nāma

[p. b674]

kāryāvacchinnasya vastuno bhavati na padārthamātrāvacchinnasya | ato
jātyādibhiḥ dravyamevopalakṣayitavyam | na hi dravyaviśeṣādṛte
kāryaviśeṣaparyavasānaṃ bhavati | tasmādupalakṣaṇatayaiva tatra jātyā-
deranvayaḥ na punaḥ kārakatayā | nanu caivaṃ sati upādeyatvepi kārya-
viśeṣānavagāt jātyādīnāmupalakṣaṇataiva | na caivamiṣyate jātirevāṅga-
miti mīmāṃsakāḥ | anabhijño bhavān upāttopādeyayoḥ upātta-
dravyalakṣaṇaṃ hyuddiśyamānapadārtho bhavati | vidheyaṃ punaḥ kamupalakṣa-
yati ? vihitatvāddhi kāryasidhyarthaṃ svāśrayaṃ dravyamākṣipati nopalakṣa-
yati | ata uddiśyamānasya upalakṣaṇatvāt dravye saṃpratyayo bhavati

[p. b675]

na punaḥ svasaṃbandhinyabhidheye | tasmāt kāryamātramevāpekṣate’vacche-
dakatayā na punaḥ śrutyā’bhihitamekatvādikam | (tarhi) upāttataiva
tatra viśeṣāvagame hetuḥ | yatpunarupādeyaṃ tat śrutita eva viśiṣṭa-
mavagantavyam, nānyata iti viśeṣaḥ | so’yamukto hetuḥ uddiśya-
mānasya viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamityatrārthe | upāttatvādityukto hetuḥ |
kā vārtā agnīnādadhīta yūpaṃ cchinattīti ? vivakṣitā saṅkhyā, atra
hīdamevopādānavākyam | nanu cāgnipadabhuddeśakam | pradhānabhūtāścā-
gnayaḥ | satyaṃ yathā bhavānāha | kintu dvividho hyuddeśaḥ upāttasyānu-
pāttasya ca | grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭītyupāttoddeśaḥ agnīnādadhītetyanupāttoddeśaḥ

[p. b676]

anupāttañcoddiśyata iti kathamidaṃ ? vidheyaviṣayo hyuddeśa ityucyate,
nopāttatvamanupāttatvaṃ ca uddeśyatve hetuḥ | ataḥ pramāṇāntarasiddha-
viśeṣaṇaṃ uddiśyamānamavivakṣitaviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavati | yatpunaravijñāta-
viśeṣaṇamevoddiṣṭaṃ tat vidheyavat śrutita eva viśiṣṭamavagantavyam |
tasmāt suṣṭhūcyate upāttoddeśe’vivakṣitaṃ viśeṣaṇamiti | nanu
ca grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭīti dvitīyeyaṃ kārakavibhaktiḥ kārakaṃ
ca kriyārthaṃ bhavatīti śābdāḥ | nanu īpsitatame dvitīye-
yam | satyaṃ, sā tu kārake | nanu ca vipratiṣiddhaṃ

[p. b677]

īpsitatamaṃ kārakaṃ ceti ? kathaṃ vipratiṣiddhaṃ ? kimidaṃ
īpsitatamaṃ nāma ? yadi tāvat yataḥ kriyāsiddhiḥ
tadīpsitatamaṃ evaṃ tarhi kārakamātre prasaṅgaḥ tataśca viśeṣā-
bhidhānamanarthakamidaṃ karma idaṃ karaṇamiti | atha yatkriyayā āpyate
utpādyate saṃskriyate vā tatkarmetyucyate | na tarhi kārakaṃ kriyā-
paryavasānāpekṣo hyāptyādiko guṇaḥ | paryavasite hi vyāpāre phalaṃ
niṣpadyate na punaḥ phala[sya]sādhyaiva kriyā bhavati | ataḥ īpsitaṃ
kārakaṃ ceti vipratiṣiddham | atrābhidhīyate---na viprati-
ṣiddham | āptyādiko guṇaḥ kārakasthaścākārakasthaśca yadā kārakasthaḥ

[p. b678]

tadā kārakameva phalam | kimidaṃ kārakameva phalamiti ? |
yadā hi kārakameva kayācidvikārāpattyā vyāpyamutpādyaṃ
saṃskāryaṃ vikāryaṃ ceṣṭaṃ tadā tadeva phalamityucyate tāmevāvasthā-
maṅgīkṛtya īpsitatamavyapadeśaṃ labhata iti | kriyāsiddhayarthaṃ
phalasiddhayarthaṃ vā | ataḥ kārakamīpsitatamamiti na viruddham |
vidyate hi kārakaṃ na phalasamavāyi yathā karaṇādi | vidyate
ca phalaṃ na kārakasamavāyi yathā svargādi | nanu
svargādyapi kārakasamavāyyeva kartṛgāmi kriyāphalamiti vaca-
nāt | na kārakatayā phalasamavāyo vidyata iti |
adhikāralakṣaṇavyāpāro’yaṃ adhikāralakṣaṇe uktaḥ | kathaṃ
tarhi bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ sūtraṃ kartrabhiprāye kriyāphale

[p. b679]

iti ? asyāpi tatraivārtho nirūpyate | ato grahaṃ
saṃmārṣṭīti yadyapi grahaḥ saṃmārgaṃ niṣpādayati tathāpi na tasyāṃ-
śasya vācikā vibhaktiḥ īpsitatamatvabhācaṣṭe phalāṃśaṃmityarthaḥ kriyā-
niṣpādyamiti yāvat | tasmāt śrutito viśeṣāvagama iti
siddham | ataḥ upāttaviṣayaḥ saṃskāra iti sthitam | nanu ca
vyabhicāryetaddarśanam | anīpsitatame’pi dvitīyā bhavatyeva | tataḥ
kim ? idaṃ tato bhavati kārakāṃśābhidhāyinī na phalāṃśābhidhā-
yinī | evaṃ grahamityapi kārakamevācaṣṭe | tataścaikatvamaviva-
kṣitamimi na śakyate vaktum | kathaṃ tarhi grahārthaḥ saṃmārgaḥ
pramāṇābhāvāt | prayojanavatvāt yathā paśupuroḍāśo’gnīṣoma-
devatārthaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---karmepsitatamatvaṃ tāvat dvitīyā’bhidhatte |
tathā yuktaṃ cānīpsitamiti tu kriyānirbāhyamevetyarthaḥ |

[p. b680]

iyān viśeṣaḥ kriyāntarasaṃbandhitayā ekasyopādānaṃ ekasya neti |
evaṃ tarhi gāṃ dadātītyavivakṣitatvaṃ saṅkhyāyāḥ | bhavatviti cenna;
prayojanāntarakalpanāprasaṅgāt | kimiti kalpanā neṣyate ? pramāṇā-
bhāvāt | nanu ca dvitīyaiva pramāṇaṃ; satyaṃ, kāryāpekṣāyāṃ viśeṣaṃ
samarpayāti na punaḥ kāryāntaramavagamayituṃ kṣamā, viniyogaparyava-
sāyinyo hi vibhaktayaḥ na kāryaparyavasāyinyaḥ navā’dhikāra-
śūnyo niyogo’stīti ‘phalavatsannidhau’
 ityatra vakṣyāmaḥ |

[p. b681]

saṃskāravidhayastūktāḥ teṣāmarthena ityatra | uktottaramapi kimiti
bhavatā paricoditaṃ ? (bhāvikārya)vismaraṇāparādhāditi manyāmahe
tadidaṃ smāryate anupātaviṣayamidaṃ prādhānyamiti yūpavat siddhaṃ
vivakṣitatvaṃ saṅkhyāyāḥ | amunā ca viśeṣeṇa sūtrāntarapraṇayanaṃ
bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ akathitamiti | tatrāpi tathāyuktamityanuvartate
tasmāt dvitīyāprādhānyaṃ na vyabhicaratīti sthitam | upātte tu
viṣaye viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamiti siddham | 1grahādiṣvekatvaṃ
brūyāt na dvitīyādīn pratiṣedhet”
 iti | upāttaviṣayatvāt tu grahaśruteḥ
upādeye hyekatvādayo viśeṣaṇam | iha punaḥ upāttataiva viśeṣā-
vargatau heturityuktam |

[p. b682]

nanu ca ‘parisañcaṣṭa’
 iti ko’bhiprāyaḥ ? ayamabhiprāyaḥ---eka-
tvamananvitaṃ syāt nānanvitānāmabhidhānamastītyuktaṃ tadbhūtā-
dhikaraṇe | naitadasti satyamananvitābhidhānamekavacanasya prāpnoti na
punaḥ parisaṅkhyā saṃbhavati aprāptatvāt | grahasaṃbhārgādeḥ saṃbandhasya
prāptau satyāmanyanivṛttau niyogaḥ prabhavati | aprāpte punaḥ yathā
śveti śrutiḥ na kākādīn nivartayituṃ kṣamā | na cānanvitā-
bhidhānam tasyāpi prāptyavagamāt | nanu ca yuktaṃ śvādiṣu | ekatvaṃ
punaḥ bahuṣūpādīyamāneṣu pakṣepi na prāpnoti | ato’nanvitā-
bhidhānaṃ duṣpariharamiti | parihṛtamidaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa na vibhakterva-
canamevaikaṃ prayojanamiti | kathaṃ punaranena parihṛtamidaṃ bhavati ?
itthamanena parihṛtaṃ bhavati-saṅkhyāyā ananvaye’pi karmamātre prayoga-
darśanāt yathā brāhmaṇo na hantavya iti | atrāpi sādhyamiti cet,

[p. b683]

na nañarthe vadhena saṃbadhyamāne ekatvamanvetuṃ kṣamam | anyataḥ
pravṛttyavagamāt | tasmādekatvābhidhānaṃ karmamātre sādhviti
draṣṭavyam |

1ayaṃ cāparo doṣaḥ iti
 | ekatvānvitābhidhāne āśrīyamāṇe
grahatvaṃ nopāttaviṣayaṃ syāt anupāttaviṣayatve ca grahatvasyānanvayaḥ
prāpnoti | ananvīyamāne ca saṃsargavidhānaṃ nirviṣayatvādanarthakam |

[p. b684]

“athaikatvasaṃmārgasaṃbandho vidhīyeta”
 tatrāpi dvayī gatiḥ syāt anena
guṇaphalasaṃbandhavadvidhānaṃ na saṃbhavatīti darśayati | anyatra syāditi
kathaṃ ? ekatvaṃ tāvannopāttasyopalakṣaṇāya alaṃ bhavati |
sammārgeṇa hi saṃbadhyamānaṃ pradhānaṃ vā bhavati guṇo vā
nānyaḥ saṃbandho’sti | kathaṃ nāsti ? prātipadikāśritaṃ hi
saṅkhyābhidhānaṃ vibhaktīnāmityuktam | kasmānna viṣayatayā
uddeśyatayā vidheyatayā vā saṃbandhaḥ saṃbhavati paratantratvāt |

[p. b685]

abhyupagamyāpyaruṇāvanna saṃbhavatītyuktaṃ grahasya pradhānatvāt |
nanu ca pradhānepi saṃbhavatyeva viśeṣaṇaṃ nimittabhūtaṃ kārakatva-
māśritya | kena coktaṃ na saṃbhavatīti ? iha punarvidhānāsaṃbhavaḥ
pradarśita pradhānatvāda्grahasya | athocyeta grahādi lakṣayitvā tasyaika-
tvasaṃbandho vidhīyate na saṃmārgasaṃbandha iti | ko’bhiprāyaḥ ? ayamabhi-
prāyaḥ---kaḥ punaḥ pakṣapātaḥ saṃmārgo’nvayī na punarekatvamiti |
uttaraṃ---kriyāśūnyo’pyanvayo na saṅkhyādeḥ saṃbhavati | asati
tu vidheye saṃbhavatyapi na ca śrutyānvayaḥ ekatvasyāstītyuktam |

[p. b686]

yadyavivakṣitamekatvamiti1 lakṣaṇavirodhaṃ darśayati | bahuṣu bahuvacana-
miti śābdāḥ | na vayametadvicārayāmaḥ2 iti pramāṇataḥ sthite lakṣye
kiṃ lakṣaṇaṃ kariṣyatītyabhiprāyaḥ | na cātra lakṣaṇavirodhaḥ na
vibhaktervacanamevaikaṃ prayojanamiti vyākhyātam | api ca grahaḥ
prātipadikārthaḥ ekatvaṃ vibhaktyarthaḥ | kimato yadyevam ? etadato bhavati
prātipadikārthagataṃ hi vibhaktiḥ svamarthaṃ śrutyaiva vadatīti vibhaktya-
bhihitasya pāratantryaṃ darśayati | tacca vyākhyātam | atha yaduktaṃ
paśumālabheteti upādeyānāṃ śrutito viśeṣa ityuktam |

3saṃskārādvā guṇānāmavyavasthā syāt || 3-1.16 || a. 8.
yadi kāryata upādānāṃ upāttasya ca lakṣaṇaṃ

[p. b687]

śeṣitayaiveti tato grahaśrutirapi hi somarasādhāre pātre lakṣaṇaiva |
ekakāryatvāt | saṃmārgasya ca somarasāvasekanirharaṇameva prayo-
janamityupapannaṃ camasānāmapi saṃmārjanam | darśitaṃ caitadbhāṣya-
kāreṇa | sāmarthyataśca kāryasiddhau kevalasammārgavidhānalābhaḥ
prakaraṇe mantropadeśavat | uktaṃ caitadbhāṣyakāreṇa |

1vyavasthā vā’rthasya śrutisaṃyogāt tasya śabdapramāṇatvāt || 3-1.17 ||
atrābhidhīyate---śrutitaḥ upāttaparijñānaṃ nopādā-
nāt śrutiḥ | śrutiśca grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭīti | nanu ca
somāvasiktaparimārjanaṃ tulyaṃ kāryamityuktam | satyamuktam |

[p. b688]

na śrutibādhe tatsamarthamityuktamasmābhiḥ | śrute yadeva kāryaṃ tadeva
cet kāryaṃ kimityaśrute’pi na bhavati | na hi kāryaṃ śrūyate |
śrūyate hi kāryamityuktam | kāryaṃ cenna kriyate kathaṃ yaveṣu prokṣaṇam ?
yathā tat tathā navame vakṣyāmaḥ | yaduktaṃ saṃsargamātra-
vidhānaṃ lābho labhyata iti | tadayuktam | niyogaviṣayatvāta
niyogasya darśitaṃ ca bhāṣyakāreṇa | viniyoge sati na

[p. b688]

kaścidvirodho niyogasya syāt sannikarṣasyāpi viśeṣāt | sa ca
virodhe | iha tu virodhābhāvādatantraṃ viprakarṣa iti siddham |
ekatvaṃ punaryathā nānveti tathoktam |

1ānarthakyāttadaṅgeṣu || 3-1-18 || a. 9
‘saptadaśāratnirvājapeyasya yūpo bhavati’
 ityatroddeśakaṃ padaṃ kiṃ
vājapeyasya saptadaśeti uta yūpasya saptadaśeti | pūrvapakṣavādino’bhi-
prāyaḥ | yadi yūpa ityuddeśakaṃ padaṃ vājapeyasyetyananvitābhidhānaṃ

[p. b690]

prāpnoti yūpena vājapeyasyāsaṃbandhāt | tasmāt vājapeyasaṃbandhini
yūpaśabdo draṣṭavyaḥ | evamanvitapadārthako vākyārthaḥ samarthito bhavati |
atrābhidhīyate | yadi yūpaśabde śrautānvayini vājapeyasyetyananvitaṃ
syāt syādetadevam | atra punaḥ saptadaśāratniryūpa ityapi saṃbandhe
vājapeyaśabdo’nvita eva | kathaṃ ? saṃbandhasaṃbandhinyapi ṣaṣṭhyantasyā-
nvayadarśanāt devadattasya naptetyevamādiṣu | tasmādyūpaśabdasya
lākṣaṇikatve nāsti pramāṇamiti yūpenaiva saṃbandhaḥ | kimiti punaratra
vājapeyasyetyanena kṛtam ? kimasmākamanena kṛtaṃ na kṛtamiti ?

[p. b691]
 
anvayastāvadvidyate | vidyamānānvaye tu vākyārthe kiñcitkāryayogi
bhavati kiñcit prāptārthaṃ sadanūdyata iti na doṣaḥ |

1kartṛguṇe tu karmasamavāyādvākyabhedaḥ syāt || 3-1-19 || a. 10
abhikrāmaṃ juhotīti kiṃ karmaṇaḥ karmānvayo vidyate uta neti
cintyate | kathaṃ punaranvayo vidyate kathaṃ vā na ? tatra pūrvapakṣavādino-
’bhiprāyaḥ na karma karmasādhyaṃ bhavati | tasmādyaḥ prakaraṇe kartā’bhi-
krāmatītyanvayaḥ | nanu ca prayājeṣvapi yaḥ kartā tatrāpyanvayo bhavatyeva |

[p. b692]

satyaṃ bhavati yadi juhotītyabhidhāyakaḥ syāt | anabhidhāya-
katve tasmin na karturuddeśyatvaṃ saṃbhavati nānṛtaṃ vadeditivat |
tasmāt saṃyogato’viśeṣāt prakaraṇāviśeṣācca vākyabhedaḥ | juhoti-
rodānīṃ kimarthaḥ ? yadarthastadartho bhaviṣyatīti |

1sākāṅkṣaṃ tvekavākyaṃ syādasamāptaṃ hi pūrveṇa || 20 ||
satyamananvayaḥ karmaṇaḥ karmaṇā | kartṛsaṃbandhāt tu
aruṇāvat saṃbhavatīti | nanu ca karturupādānaṃ
nāsti yathā ekahāyanyāḥ, juhotītyasyānabhidhāyakatvādi-

[p. b693]

tyuktam | na brūmaḥ karturuddeśo’stīti | tannimittakastu karmodde-
śe’pyanvayo na virudhyata iti, tatra tasya saṃbhavāt aruṇādhikaraṇa-
vaiṣamyam | tatra dvayorapyanvayinorupādānaṃ iha tu saṃbhavamātreṇaiveti
viśeṣaḥ | yaduktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa pūrvapakṣe saṃbandhamātraparijñānāt
prayogavacanāt kartavyatāsiddhiḥ iti tatraiva karturuddeśyatvaṃ na
virudhyata ityabhiprāyaḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu saṃbhavamātreṇānvaya-
siddherna karturuddeśena prayojanamastīti manyate | ataḥ siddhaṃ karmāpi
karmaṇāmuddeśyatāṃ pratipadyata iti |

[p. b694]

1sandigdhe tu vyavāyādvākyabhedaḥ syāt || 3-1-21 || a. 11
upavyayata ityatra sandihyate---kiṃ sāmidhenyanuvacanasya kartā
upavyānena saṃbadhyate uta kṛtsne prakaraṇa iti | nanu naivātroddeśyaṃ
padaṃ kiñcicchrūyate | anuddiṣṭapadārthaṃ ca yattat prakaraṇenaiva saṃbadhyate
saṃyogato’viśeṣāt prakaraṇāviśeṣāccetyuktam | ucyate---ādhastye
tāvadadhikaraṇe anuddiṣṭenāpi kartrā’nvayo’pi na virudhyata
ityuktam | evamatrāpi sāmidhenīnāṃ kartuḥ sannidhānāt
kriyāntarasādhanatayā upavyānasya loke’vagatatvāt anuddi-
ṣṭo’pi kartā sannihitaḥ kāryaparipūraṇāyālaṃ bhavitumarhati |

[p. b695]
 
1nanu darśapūrṇamāsayoreva prakaraṇamidam | paraprakaraṇe sāmidhenyaḥ śrūyanta
iti |
 ko’sya bhāṣyasyārthaḥ ? varṇito’smābhiruddeśyapadā-
bhāvāt prakaraṇāviśeṣaḥ | “satyaṃ, paraprakaraṇe śrūyante | tathāpi
tāsāmavāntaraprakaraṇamasti”

 iti | kimidamavāntaraprakaraṇam | ekasya
hi adhikāravidheḥ prakaraṇam | na hi aṅgavidhīnāmapi prakaraṇamasti |
pradhānavidhyabhedāt | pradhānavidhīnāṃ ca abhedaṃ caturthe vakṣyāmaḥ |

[p. b696]

saṃskāravidhīnāṃ ca teṣāmarthena saṃbandha ityatrānuvādaḥ pradarśitaḥ |
tasmādavāntaraprakaraṇaṃ grāhakaviniyojakatayā nopapadyate |
ataḥ sannidhānamātramavaśiṣvate | ucyate---sannidhāna evāyaṃ
prakaraṇaśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ | kathamavagamyate | yena tatsannidhā-
vabhidhīyamānaṃ tasyeti jñāyate iti vacanāt | nanu ca
viniyojake’pi prakaraṇe sannidhānaśrutiśabdaḥ upapadyate |
kena coktaṃ nopapadyata iti | kintu tanna saṃbhavatī-
tyuktam | vākyabhedaśravaṇācca, vyavadhānaṃ ca darśayati |

[p. b697]

parārthena vyavadhānamānantaryaṃ vihantiṃ na punaḥ viniyogam | anyathā
svāhākārasyāviniyogaḥ syāt samidādibhirvyavadhānāt | atha
nivitpadānāmanyārthatvāt grāhakasyāpi vyavadhānamiti dvādaśāhīna-
syetyapi vyavadhānaṃ syāt | kiñca kāmyairavyavadhānaṃ nivitpadānāṃ
syāt | tataśca nividarthatā prāpnoti | satyaṃ yadyanuvacanavyavadhāna-
mabhipretam | iha tu sannihitakartṛvyavadhānamabhipretaṃ tacca guṇaphala-
saṃbandhābhidhāne sati karmaṇo vyavadhānādupapannaṃ karturvyavadhānam |

[p. b698]

evaṃ sthite pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ---guṇaphalasaṃbandhepyanuvacana-
śrutau kartā sannihita eveti sāmidhenyarthaṃ upavyānam
rāddhāntavādī tu nāvidhīyamānaṃ sannihitaṃ bhavati svasthānasthitamanu-
vadanīyamanūdyate, na punaḥ sannidhāpyate | anūdyamānavaśavartitvā-
danuvādānām | ataḥ sannidhānāviśeṣāt kṛtsne prakaraṇe niveśa
iti siddham |

guṇānāṃ ca parārthatvādasaṃbandhaḥ samatvāt syāta1 || (3, 1. 22. a. 12) ||
vāraṇo yajñāvacara iti yajñasaṃbandhitvena pātraṃ śrūyate ānartha-
kyāttadaṅgoṣviti sthito nyāyaḥ | tadidānīṃ pavamāna-

[p. b699]

haviṣāṃ yadyādhānāṅgatā tataḥ pavamānahaviṣṣveva pātravidhānametat |
yadi nāṅgatā tadā sarvayajñārthamiti | kiṃ tāvadyuktam ? pavamāna-
haviṣṣu vidhānam | ādhānāṅgaṃ hi pavamānahavīṃṣi prakaraṇe caitacchrūyate
pradhāne cāsaṃbhavat dharmaḥ aṅgeṣu vidheya ityuktam | nanu ca
pavamānahavīṃṣi agnyarthāni yadāhavanīye juhotīti vacanāt |
anārabhyavādo’yaṃ sarvahomānāṃ guṇatvenāhavanīyaṃ vidadhāti na
prādhānyaṃ saṃbhavati | tasmādānarthakyāttadaṅgeṣviti pavamānahaviṣṣu
vidhānam | atrābhidhīyate---naivādhānaprakaraṇe pavamānahavīṃṣi śrūyante
nāpyagnyarthānyeva | kathaṃ ? yadāhavanīye juhotīti vacanāt |

[p. b700]

nanu ca guṇatayā vidadhātyanārabhyavādo’yamityuktam | na brūmo na guṇa-
tayā vidadhātīti | kintvatra prakṛtitaḥ prāptipradarśanārthaṃ evaṃ vacana-
mupanyastaṃ nopadeśārtham | kathaṃ punaḥ prakṛtiprīptau prādhānyaṃ bhavatīti
nanu ca guṇatayaiva prakṛtito’pi prāpnoti | satyamevam | ādhāno-
pakārakatvamagniniṣpattyā bhavati | agniniṣpattiścetprayojanaṃ kimi-
tyādhānopakārakatayā na punaḥ sākṣātprakṛtitaḥ saṃbandhāt | nanu ca
guṇatayā prāptirityuktam | satyamuktaṃ sā punarādhānāṅgatve na saṃbhavati
aniṣpannatvādagneḥ | niṣpādayatīti cet tarhi sākṣānniṣpādakatvam |

[p. b701]

na hi sākṣātsaṃbandhe saṃbhavati parapṛṣṭhena saṃbandho yuktaḥ |
tasmātsarvārthatā vāraṇādeḥ yukteti |

mithaścānarthasaṃbandhāt ||3-1-23 a. 13 ||
tadeva punarudāharaṇāntare cintyate kenacit viśeṣeṇāna-
rthakyāttadaṅgeṣviti | kaḥ punarasau viśeṣaḥ | ayamasau viśeṣaḥ |
tatra yūpaśabdaḥ śrūyate | iha punaḥ nājyabhāgābhidhānam | ataḥ
pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | yuktaṃ vājapeye yūpaśabdābhidhānāt vyava-
dhāne’pi vājapeyetyanvayaḥ | iha punaḥ saṃbandhisaṃbandhyanvaye na
kiñcit pramāṇamasti | katha tarhi dvivacanam | dvayordvivacanam | nanu
caikatvāt prādhānyasyaikaivānuvākyā | kimidaṃ ekatvātpradhānasyeti |

[p. b702]

nanu caikaikatra pradhānatrayamasti | na brūmaḥ ekameva pradhānamiti | ekai-
kasyetikartavyatāsaṃbandha iti vadāmaḥ tataḥ kiṃ dvayorasaṃbandhaḥ |
nanu cājyabhāgayorekena saṃbandhaḥ | satyameva, śrutyā tu dvayotpatteḥ
dvandvābhidhānamaviruddham | iha punaḥ pradhāne svataḥ ekatvāt anu-
vākyāyā ekaikasyāścotpatteḥ pradhānānāṃ dvandvavirodhaḥ | ucyate---na
virodhaḥ | kimiva vacanaṃ na kuryāt nāstivacanasyātibhāra iti |
tatra dvitvamayathārthatvaṃ ca mantrasya bhaviṣyati, vyavadhāne pramāṇā-
bhāvādityuktam |

[p. b703]

saṃbhavati yathāyogyavidhāne yathārthatvadvitvayoḥ pramāṇamasti |
tasmādājyabhāgayoreva mantravidhānamiti siddham |

nārtham |

[p. b704]

ānantaryamacodanā ||1 3. 1. 24-a. 14 ||

rimāṇaṃ śabdādevāvagantavyam | anyato’navagamāditi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. b733]

prakṛtamaprakṛtaṃ vā yāvalliṅgaṃ vidheyam | kiṃ prakṛtābhirliṅgavatī-
bhirupasthātavyaṃ uta dāśatayībhya evaṃliṅgā āgamayitavyā
iti dāśatayīgrahaṇaṃ na prakṛtapratipakṣatayā | etaduktaṃ bhavati |
kiṃ yāvalliṅgā grahītavyā uta prakṛtā evaivaṃliṅgā āgamayitavyā
iti |

‘kimidaṃ 1upadiṣṭopadeśo na nyāyyaḥ’
 ? idamidam | upadiṣṭa-
taiva hi tasya viśeṣaḥ | na cāsau vidheyāṅgamityuktam | prakṛtvaṃ
punarabhidhānāttulyam | atrāha-2virodhe sati liṅgena prakaraṇaṃ bādhyate

[p. b734]

na caitayorvirodha" iti | ko’bhiprāyaḥ ? ayamabhiprāyaḥ prakṛte’pi
gṛhyamāṇe’bhihita evārtho gṛhīto bhavati | yathaikasyāmapi gavyupā-
dīyamāne caritārthaiva gāmālabheteti śratiḥ | naitadevamiti | nātra
liṅgavattā śabdārtha iti manyate | ucyate, talliṅgavattā’neneti
heyatayā’sya pakṣasyopanyāsaḥ | dāśatayyāṃ punaḥ mantravyaktāviti
prakṛta eva saṃpanne liṅgārthe niṣpramāṇakaḥ prakṛtabādha iti
manyate |

[p. b735]

1liṅgajanitastu pratyayaḥ kaścinmithyeti
 | liṅgārthasaṃpattau satyāṃ ko’yaṃ
mithyābhāvaḥ | ayamabhiprāyaḥ---nāyaṃ sāmānyopasaṃhāraḥ śabdārtha
iti | ‘svargakāmo yajeta’
 iti na sampatyā anyaprayogārhopi
śāstrārthaḥ paryavasita iti śakyate vaktum | nanu ca vyaktirapadārtha
iti sāmānyopasaṃhṛtirevopanyastā1 | etadeva na vijānīma
iti pratyayārthapradhānatvāt taddhitāntānāṃ na tadvattā’ṅgamiti

[p. b736]

manyate | tasmānna sāmānyopasaṃhārārthamidaṃ śāstram | nanvetataduktaṃ
kāryāntare prakṛtasyopadeśo nāsāvarthāntara upadekṣyata iti
upadiṣṭopadeśaparihārāyāyamupanyāsaḥ | kimiti ? liṅgavanmātra-
grahaṇāditi darśitam | nātropadiṣṭatā hetuḥ | nanu caivaṃ
sati prakṛtabādhaḥ | bhavatvabhidhānāt prakṛtabādho na doṣaḥ |

[p. b737]

1tadākhyo vā prakaraṇopapattibhyām || 3-1-22 a. 8 ||

iti manvānaḥ prakṛtasyaiva grahaṇamityuktavān | kathaṃ ? uddeśyatvaṃ hi
nāma prayojanavato bhavati |

[p. b738]

gāmānayetivadvā | tasmāt prakṛtapratyarthī na sāmānyaśabdo bhavitu-

copadiṣṭatvāditi | yaduktaṃ tadidam | yadyupadeśamātraṃ sarvatrāsti

[p. b739]

1liṅgasamākhyābhyāṃ bhakṣārthatānuvākasya || 3-1-25 a. 9 ||

iti | kiṃ tāvad‌yuktam ? samākhyāpi prakṛtavanniyamayatyeva
viśeṣe liṅgam | nanu liṅgāntaravirodhaḥ prāpnoti | na, viśeṣaṇa-
tayāpi liṅgāntarasyāvirodhāt | yataḥ svārthe parārthe vā’bhidhāna-
maviśiṣṭam | nanu ca pārārthye lakṣaṇā prāpnoti | tathāpya-
bhidhānamaviśiṣṭaṃ idaṃparatāviśeṣo’yaṃ nābhidhānaviśeṣaḥ |

[p. b740]

tasmānniyantumarhati samākhyā |

1tasya rūpopadeśābhyāmapakarṣo’rthasya coditvāt || 3-2-16 ||

1śruterjātādhikāraḥ syāt || 3-3-1 a. 1 ||
1śruterjātādhikāraḥ syāt
 | ‘uccairṛcā kriyate’
 iti kimayaṃ vedadharmaḥ
uta ṛgdharma iti saṃśayaḥ | ko’yaṃ vedo nāma ? nanu cargapi veda
eveti | ucyate---vidhāyakaṃ vedaśabdenocyate | etaduktaṃ bhavati
---yadṛgvedaśabdavācyaṃ vidhāyakaṃ tadvidheyadharmaḥ uta yatra pādavyavasthā
tasmin mantra iti |

kiṃ tāvadyuktam---uccaiḥ ṛcā kriyata iti vacanādṛgdharmo’ya-
miti | nanu vedopakramametadvākyam | ko doṣaḥ ? ṛkṣvapyayaṃ veda-
śabdo vartata eva | evaṃ ca na vikalpo bhaviṣyati vedāntarapaṭhi-

1vedo vā prāyadarśanāt || 3-3-2 ||
atrocyate---vedadharmo’yam | kutaḥ ? vedaśabdopakrama-

[p. b749]

bhavati tadviśeṣapratipattireva kevalamupasaṃhārādapekṣyate | na

punarnargvedaśabdenocyate ? kena coktaṃ nocyata iti ?

1trayīvidyākhyā ca tadvidi-iti ||

bhavati | vede ṛgādiśabdānāṃ prayogo dṛṣṭa iti | yatpunarvedāntaroktasya

eva | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ vedasaṃyogepi prakaraṇaṃ nānugṛhyate, ṛg‌dharma-

[p. b751]

dvidhānaṃ dharmavidhiḥ |

1guṇamukhyavyatikrame tadarthatvānmukhyena vedasaṃyogaḥ || 2 ||
“ya evaṃ vidvānagnimādhatte”
 iti yājurvaidiko vidhiḥ | “ya 
evaṃ vidvān vāravantīyaṃ gāyati”
 iti ādhānaprakaraṇe sāmavaidika
iti kṛtvā cintyate | yājurvaidike bhavatūpāṃśutvaṃ, sāmavaidike
coccaiṣṭvaṃ, satyamevaṃ yadyaṅgapradhānavidhī bhidyete yathātvabhedaḥ tathā
phalavatsannidhau aphalaṃ tadaṅgamityatra vakṣyati | abhede cobhayaveda-
vidhānāt bhavati saṃśayaḥ | ubhayavedavidhānāt vikalpo
bhavatu | na vikalpaḥ saṃbhavati | evaṃ sati aṅgavidhiranuvāda

[p. b752]

ityaṅgavidheḥ pradhānavidhitantratvaṃ darśayati | tathāpi kaḥ saṃśayaḥ ? aya-
masti saṃśayaḥ kimaṅgavidhirākṣiptasvavidheya eva pradhānavidhitantro
bhavati, uta pradhānenaikībhūto vidheyaṃ vidhatta iti | pūrvapakṣavādī
manyate | svārthaṃ vidhāya prayojanavaśāt pradhānenaikībhavati vāravantīya
gāyatītyasya yaḥ svārthaḥ | ataḥ sāmavaidikasvarayuktasyaivāṅgabhāvaḥ |
atrābhidhīyate---vihitasvārtha eva pradhānenaikībhavati | na punaraṅga-
tāmapi vidhāya | pradhānavidhiraṅgatāṃ vidhatta iti saptamādye vakṣyāmaḥ

[p. b753]

aṅgatayā ca vāravantīyasya prayogaḥ na vāravantīyaṃ gāyatītyasya
vidherviṣayatayā | ato na svārthavidhiḥ pradhānavidhiṃ bādhitumarhati
svārthasya prayogārthatvāt | tadidamuktam---“1guṇasyānuṣṭhānena mukhyaḥ
saguṇaḥ kathaṃ syāditi guṇe pravartate | guṇapravṛttyā cenmukhyayya
guṇahānirbhavati guṇapravṛttau phalameva nāvāptaṃ bhavati | atha pradhānaṃ
saguṇaṃ kariṣyāmīti guṇe pravartamāno guṇasya guṇaṃ vinipātayati nāsya
svārtho hīyate nāsau guṇaṃ saguṇaṃ kartuṃ pravartata”
 iti na guṇavidhau
svārthe pravṛttirītyuktaṃ bhavati | prayogāṅgatvācca guṇavidheḥ pradhāna-
guṇena bhavitavyamityuktam |

2bhūyastvenomayaśruti || 3-3-10 a. 3 ||
sthitaṃ tāvat pradhānavidhinibandhano guṇaḥ guṇavidhi-

[p. b754]

nibandhanaṃ guṇaṃ bādhata iti | idamidānīṃ cintyate---yatrobhayatra
pradhānaśrutiḥ tatra kva pradhānacodaneti | yatra pradhānacodanā
tannibandhanena guṇena bhavitavyam | tatsidhyartheṣācintā tatrā-
gṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvādubhayatretyuktvā bhūyastvenaikatra nirūpitaṃ
vālavyajanādinā bahupu rājapratimeṣūpaviṣṭeṣu rājā’vagamyata iti
dṛṣṭāntābhidhānam | tadidamubhayamayuktam | kva bhūyastvena pradhānasya
prayogābhidhānaṃ dṛṣṭam | na ca bālavyajanaṃ śvetacchatraṃ vā bhūyastvenā-
vagamayati | kiṃ tarhi ? asādhāraṇatayā | tasmādasaṃbaddhamevedaṃ
bhāṣyaṃ manyāmahe |

atrābhidhīyate---nedamasaṃbaddham | bhūyastvena hi prayogā-
vagamassūcyate sakalaprayogo’vagamyata ityarthaḥ | alpāṅgā-

[p. b755]

bhidhāne punaḥ na sakalaprayogāvagatirbhavati | sakale ca prayoge’vagamya-
māne prayogābhidhānamatirbhavati | prayeyāddhi pramāṇānumānamiti
sthitam | yadapi ca vālavyajanamupanyastaṃ tadgamakasāmānyatayā
bhūyastveneti sūktam |

asaṃyuktaṃ prakaraṇāt iti kartavyatārthatvāt || a. 4 ||
1 kimetāvantyeva pramāṇāni ? ke’yaṃ praśnaḥ ? yadi tāvata
sūtrārtha eva na śakyate vyākhyātuṃ praśnena vinetyabhiprāyaḥ | tada-
yuktam | praśnena kimatra; yadasaṃyuktaṃ tatprakaraṇena viniyujyata iti

[p. b756]

nanu kenāsaṃyuktamiti na vidmaḥ | evaṃ tarhi etadeva
praṣṭavyam | kenāsaṃyuktamiti | kvedamupayujyate kimetāvantyeva
pramāṇānīti ? athāyamabhiprāyaḥ | kimiti śrutyā liṅgena
vākyena vā yadasaṃyukteṃ tatprakaraṇena viniyujyata iti | kimetā-
cantyeva pramāṇātīti, tathāpyuttaraṃ tāvadasaṃbaddhaṃ anyadapyastīti |
yadyanyadapyasti kimiti tarhi śrutyā liṅgena vākyena vā’saṃyukta-
mityucyate | athājñānameva naiva śrutiliṅgavākyebhyaḥ pramāṇamanya-
dvijñāyata iti tathāpi svarūpaṃ vaktavyam | nāsaṃyuktaṃ prakaraṇāditi
yujyate kartavyasya itikartavyatākāṅkṣasyeti vakṣyamāṇavadvaktavyam |

[p. b757]

ataḥ praśno’rthe’naupayika iva dṛśyate | atrābhidhīyate---ayamabhi-
prāyo bhāṣyakārasya | lakṣaṇādāvuktaṃ “1śeṣalakṣaṇamucyate |
śrutyādīni viniyoge kāraṇāni”
 iti | tatra tāvat śrutiliṅga-
vākyaiḥ viniyojyaṃ śeṣatvamuktaṃ prakaraṇena viniyojyaṃ punaḥ kimatra
bhaviṣyati | nanu ca prayājādayaḥ santyeva kimiti praśnaḥ |
bāḍhaṃ praśnārthaḥ vidyate prayājādīnāṃ hi ekaniyogalakṣaṇasaṃbandhaṃ
caturthe vakṣyati darśapūrṇamāsādibhiḥ kimatrānyatsaṃbandhe pramāṇaṃ
mṛgyate | evaṃ ca tadbhūtādhikaraṇa evoktaḥ saṃbandhaḥ anvitā-
midhāyibhiḥ padaiḥ sarvatra śabdārthāvagame heturiti vyāpakaṃ
bhavati | pramāṇāntare punaranviṣyamāṇe nānvitābhidhānanibandhanaḥ

[p. b758]

śakyate pradarśayitum | prakaraṇādibhirhi vākyārthānāṃ saṃbandhāvagatiḥ |
na ca vākyārthasya vākyārthasya ca saṃbandhe padābhidhānalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandha
upapadyate | anvitaṃ hi padārthāntareṇa padārthamācaṣṭe padaṃ, na
vākyārthaṃ vākyārthāntareṇa | ato darśapūrṇamāsārthatayā prayājādi-
vākyārthānvayaṃ aśābdaṃ manyamānasya praśnaḥ |

nanu caitānyeva pramāṇātīti aśābdaṃ manvānasya praśnenaiva
bhavitavyam na punaḥ kimetāvantyeva pramāṇānīva | ucyate---eta-
devoktaṃ yadyasaṃyuktaṃ na tarhi viniyojyam | ato’saṃyuktamiti
vadatā etāvantyeva pramāṇānītyuktaṃ bhavati | tasmāt yukto’yaṃ praśnaḥ

[p. b759]

---kimetāvantyeva pramāṇānīti | itarastu nāvaśyaṃ padārthā-
ntarānvitameva śābdam | aidamarthyenāpi śabdaḥ saṃbandhaṃ
pratipādayatyeveti manvāno netyuktavān | itarastu pṛcchati 1kimaparaṃ
kāraṇamiti praśnenaivopakramaḥ pūrvapakṣa iti, pūrvoktapraśnārthavivaraṇaṃ

[p. b760]

kā nāmeyaṃ nadīti ca | na cānirjñātanadyarthasyaivopapadyate praśna iti 
prakaṭīkṛtaḥ praśnārthaḥ ‘kathaṃ punarasaṃyuktasyaiva viniyogaḥ’
 | tadi-
damaidamarthyailakṣaṇaṃ kathayati iti kartavyatārthitvāditi | aidamarthyenāpi
śabdaḥ sabandhaṃ pratipādayatyeveti |

nanu caikaniyogalakṣaṇasaṃbandho’yamityuktam | kimidamiti-
kartavyatārthitvaṃ nāma ? ucyate---ekaniyogalakṣaṇa eva saṃbandho
nopapadyate yadītikartavyatā lakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandho na syāt | na
hyasaṃbandhavidheyasya ekaniyogatā upapadyate vidheyanānātvāt |

[p. b761]

upapadyate ca karaṇetikartavyatayā saṃbandhaḥ | anyatrāpi sādhye
darśanāt | sādhyaścāyaṃ niyogārthaḥ | tasmādupapannaṃ itikartavya-
tārthitvāditi vidheyasaṃbandhakāraṇam | kimiti tadbhūtādhikaraṇe
vākyasyaivārthenautpattikaḥ saṃbandho darśitaḥ | ucyate---asūtrārthajño
bhavān | kriyārtheneti vadatā sarvasyaivāsya prāmāṇyaṃ darśitam |
tasmādaidamarthyalakṣaṇameva saṃbandhaṃ vede vedavido manyante | tatsidhyarthaṃ
tu padārthānāmapi anvitābhidhānaṃ darśitam | aidamarthyameva hi

[p. b762]

pramāṇāntaratve hetuḥ | anvitābhidhānaṃ tu pramāṇāntarapūrvakeṣvapi
tulyam devadattaḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ sthālyāmodanaṃ pacatītyevamādiṣu laukikeṣu
vākyeṣu | ataḥ prakaraṇaviniyojyamapi śeṣatvamastīti siddham |

1kramaśca deśasāmānyāt || sū. 12. a. 5 ||
atredānīṃ kaḥ praśnārthaḥ aidamarthyalakṣaṇameva hi saṃbandhaṃ vedavido
manyanta ityuktam | nanu caitāvantyeva pramāṇānītyākṣepeṇa bhavitavyaṃ
kimidaṃ ‘kimetāvantyeva pramāṇānī’
ti | atrocyate-atrāpyasaṃyukta-

[p. b763]

grahaṇaṃ viṣayāntarapradarśanārtham | satyamaidamarthyavyatirekeṇa saṃbandho
nāsti, tathāpi pramāṇāntaramasti aidamarthyaviśeṣāvagatau sthāna-
sāmānyaṃ nāma tadidamuktaṃ---kramaśca deśasāmānyāditi | prayojanaṃ tu
darśitameva |

1ākhyā caivaṃ tadarthatvāta || sū. 13. a. 6 ||
atredānīṃ praśnārtho nopapadyate aidamarthye sati sthānavat
viśeṣāvagame hetutvāt samākhyāyāḥ | ucyate---astyatra
viśeṣaḥ yuktaṃ tāvatsthānasya viśeṣāvagahetutvaṃ vaidikatvāt |

[p. b764]

samākhyā punaḥ laukikī | na ca laukikamabhidhānaṃ vedārthāvagame
heturbhavati | yathā ‘pacati’
 ityevamādipu | ata idaṃ kimetāvantyeva
pramāṇānīti | atrocyate | samākhyāyā laukikatvepi vaidi-
kasyārthasya viśeṣāvagame hetutvaṃ pratipadyate | kutaḥ ? abhidhānaṃ
hi kṛttaddhitasamāsānāṃ saṃbandhini prasiddhaṃ vaidikasyārthasya cedama-
bhidhānaṃ yadyapi svayaṃ laukikam | ato viśeṣamavagamayati | na
cedaṃ smṛtivat pramāṇāntarāpekṣaṃ autpattikatvāt śabdārtha-

[p. b765]

saṃbandhasya | tasmādyuktaṃ ākhyā caivaṃ tadarthatvāta iti yato
viśeṣāvagame hetuḥ | prayojanamuktam |

śrutiliṅgavākyaprakaraṇasthānasamākhyānāṃ samavāye pāradaurbalyamartha-
viprakarṣāt || 7 ||

1kā punaratra śrutiḥ ? kiṃ liṅgamiti
 | nanu cāyaṃ praśno nopapadyate |
kimiti nopapadyate ? yasmāt uktāni viniyoge kāraṇāni
iyuktam | na cānirjñātasya uktatā saṃbhavati | tasmādvipratiṣiddhaṃ
“uktāni viniyoge kāraṇānīti | kā punaratra śrutiḥ rkiṃ liṅgaṃ”

iti ca |

[p. b766]

athāyamabhiprāyaḥ viniyoga eva śrutyādibhirukto, na
punaḥ śrutyādīnāṃ svarūpamiti | yadyevaṃ tarhi vaktavyaṃ uktaḥ
śrutyādibhirviniyoga iti | na viniyoge kāraṇānyuktānīti |
kiñca viniyogoktyā viniyogakāraṇānyuktāni bhavantyeva | kāryataḥ
kāraṇaparijñānāt | arthāvacchedyameva hi śābdaṃ viśeṣato bhavati |
svarūpato varṇamātragrahaṇāt | ato viniyogoktyāpi1 naiva praśno
na ghaṭate | śrutiliṅgayośca svarūpamabhidhāya punarvākyapraśne ko’bhi-
prāyo na vidmaḥ | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ saṃhatyārthamabhidhati padāni vākyaṃ

[p. b767]

miti spaṣṭamevānyatvamupalabhāmahe | tasmādasaṃbaddhamevedaṃ bhāṣyamiti
arthajñānamānino manyate |

atrābhidhīyate---satyamarthāt pramāṇāvagatistathāpi upapadyata
evāyaṃ praśnaḥ kā punaratra śrutiriti | kathamupapadyate ? satyaṃ gārhapatya-
śravaṇaṃ śrutiḥ satyaṃ ca indrābhidhānasāmarthyaṃ liṅgaṃ tathāpi padāntara-
nirapekṣaṃ naivobhayaṃ śakyate’vagantum | aindrayā gārhapatyaḥ upasthā-
tavya iti | nāpīndraparatā mantrasya | tulye padāntarasavyapekṣatve

[p. b768]

viśeṣaṃ ajānan pṛcchati---kā punaratra śrutiḥ kiṃ liṅgamiti
 | atha
kimityādāveva etanna nirūpitaṃ śrutyādīni viniyoge kāraṇānī
tyāsmin bhāṣya
eva | atrocyate---tatrāpyetat utsūtrameva vaktavyam | iyāṃstu atra
viśeṣaḥ ‘śrutiliṅga’
 ityasya sūtrasya vyākhyātavyatayā aupoddhāti-
katayā saṃbaddhamabhidhānaṃ bhavati | kathaṃ punaridānīṃ tulye padāntara-
saṃbandhe gārhapatyaśabdasya śrutitocyate ? iyamucyate padāntara-
saṃbandhastu indrābhidhāne gārhapatyābhidhāne vā aviśiṣṭaḥ | gārhaṃ-
patyaśravaṇameva gārhapatyābhidhāne vyāpriyate | nanu dvitīyā vyāpriyata
ityuktam | satyamuktam gārhapatyaśravaṇe sati | ataḥ padasyaiva

[p. b769]

nidarśitaṃ na prātipadikamātrasya | evamindrābhidhānepi mantre
padāntaramudāste | evaṃ ca sati vākye praśnottarametadvivekāye-
tyupapannameva | ato’sādhāraṇapratipattinimittatayā saṃhatyārthā-
bhidhāne satyapi śrutirliṅgamiti cocyate | tasmādupapannaṃ śruti-
liṅgayorvirodhe kimubhayaṃ viniyojakaṃ uta śrutireveti | prakaraṇa-
praśnābhiprāyaṃ tu svasthāne vakṣyāmaḥ, kramasamākhyayośca |

nanu caivaṃ sati vaktavyaṃ śrutiḥ liṅgaṃ cobhayaṃ pramāṇaṃ ata śruti-
rbalīyasīti | iha punaḥ śrutiliṅagayoḥ kiṃ śrutirbalīyasī āho-
svit liṅgamiti na ghaṭate | yasmādidamapi kāraṇaṃ śrutiḥ liṅgamapīti |

[p. b770]

tasmādyathaivopanyastamasmābhiḥ tathaivopanyasanīyam, na tu kiṃ
śrutirbalīyasī āhosvit liṅgamiti | tulyabale ete
kāraṇe iti ca pūrvaṣakṣahetudarśanāt | tasmādanyathaivopanyā-
so’nyathaivopasaṃhāra iti | viruddhaṃ phalamapyevamevopanyastam
kimindrasya gārhapatyasya copasthānaṃ kartavyaṃ, uta gārhapatyasyaiveti |
tasmādupakramopasaṃhārayoḥ sāmyaṃ vaktavyam |

yadapi ca evaṃ tarhi liṅgavākye virudhyamāne iha saṃpradhāryete na
śrutiliṅge iti tadapyayuktamiva, sarvatra samavetaṃ viviktaṃ ca dṛśyata
ityuktatvāt vivekasya | yā śrutiḥ sā liṅgena viruddhayata iti vākyaṃ
punaḥ lakṣaṇayāpyupapadyata evetyuktvā athaveti śrutyantaropanyāsaḥ
kimaparitoṣāt uta vikalpārtha ityetadapi naiva jñāyate |

[p. b771]

ucyate---yastāvadupanyāsaḥ kiṃ balīyasī śrutiḥ āho---
svilliṅgamiti tatrāyabhiprāyaḥ | kiṃ liṅgonmardanenaiva yathā śrutiḥ pramāṇa
tathā liṅgamapi | yadi ca ubhayamubhayopamardanenaiva pramāṇaṃ tadopapadyata
evāyamupanyāsaḥ kiṃ balīyasī śrutiḥ āhosvilliṅgamiti |

etaduktaṃ bhavati---na vidmaḥ kiṃ balīya iti | tasmādubhayaṃ
pramāṇaṃ ubhayaprāmāṇye cobhayorabhidhānam | ata eva cāniyama
ityuktam “yadi tulyabale ete kāraṇe”
 iti ca viśeṣāgrahaṇāt |
“tato vikalpa”
1 iti | ko’yaṃ vikalpaḥ ? nanu cāsmin pakṣe
samuccaya eva | abhidhānavikalpamaṅgīkṛtyaitaduktaṃ na prayogavikalpam

[p. b772]

yadi tu liṅgaṃ balīyaḥ’ ityetadapi bhāṣyamupanyāsavat draṣṭavyam |
evaṃ tarhotyukte viśeṣe punaḥ praśnaḥ kenābhiprāyeṇa ? na kevalāt
gārhapatyaśravaṇāta tadupasthānāvagatiḥ | tāvanmātrasya ca śratitva-
mupadarśitam | tadidānīṃ rāddhāntavādyabhiprāyaṃ vivṛṇoti---satyaṃ
vākyādṛte nāyamavagamaḥ gārhapatyaśravaṇāt tadupasthānāvagatiriti |
yā’tra śrutirityuktam indrābhidhānepi tulyatvāt | kathaṃ ? balīya-
syapi hi liṅge aindrayopatiṣṭhata ityetat gārhapatyaśabdena sahaika-
vākyatāmupetyaiva | yadi hi nopeyāt tato liṅgena virudhyeta
tasmāt śrutiliṅgayorvirodhaḥ | atha vā nātra ekavākyatvāditi

[p. b773]

pakṣāntaropanyāsaḥ sakalapadapradarśanārtha iti vyākhyātam | kiṃ
punaratra yuktam ? ubhayatra svārthāvagamāt | liṅgādapi liṅgārtho’vagamyate
śruterapi hi śrutyarthaḥ | vākyasya caudāsīnyāt ubhayatrābhidhānamātraṃ
virudhyate | na cābhidhāne kaścidviśeṣo’vagaṃmyata ityataḥ tulyabale ete
kāraṇe ityuktam | 1“nanu liṅgasya bhaṅgurasyeva rūpaṃ savicikitso hi liṅgātpratyaya”

 iti |
keyaṃ vicikitsā nāma ? asamyak pratyayaḥ | kimatrāsamyaktvam |
nahīndrābhidhānāt indro’bhidhātavya iti pratyayo bhavatītyarthaḥ |
bhavati tu gārhapatyaśravaṇāt vidhāyakavākyagatatvāta gārhapatya-
śabdasya | nanu cāvidhāyakatvāt liṅgasya prāmāṇyameva nāsti, kathaṃ

[p. b774]

virodha iti | satyaṃ---abhidhānasāmarthyamātraṃ naiva pramāṇam-iti-
kartavyatāvagame satyanyato viśeṣāvagame pramāṇaṃ, kartavyatāvagamāt
prakaraṇādibhiḥ | karmavyatāvagame cābhidhānamātramavaśiṣyata ityuktam |
tasmāta tulyabale ete kāraṇe iti | “1balīyānapi hi hetuḥ”
 iti
na ca tāvat balīyān, anvāruhyāpi na virodho’stīti manyate |
nanvayameva virodhaḥ sakṛt upasthānaṃ coditam asakṛdabhinirvartyata iti | naivam---upastheya-
bhedāt pratipradhānamāvartante guṇā iti nyāya evaiṣaḥ na virodho’rthaḥ
 | nanu cedaṃ avi-
rodhottaraṃ na ghaṭate | nahyatropastheyadvayaṃ śrūyate aindrayā
gārhapatyamupatiṣṭata iti gārhapatya evaikaḥ upastheyatayā
śrūyate nendro’pīti | tasmādanuttaramidam |

[p. b775]

ucyate---prakaraṇāt kartavyatāvagamāt indrapadāt indrā-
bhidhānaṃ pratīyata iti manvānena ‘upastheyabhedāt’
 ityuktam |
vākyasāmānyāt | evaṃ tarhi gauṇamukhyatā nopapadyata iti
punarvirodhopanyāsaḥ | nanu ca pratipradhānaṃ guṇāvṛttau satyāṃ
naivāsau virodho ghaṭate | aviniyuktamantrāpekṣo’yaṃ virodhaḥ |
viniyoge tvavirodhaḥ iti siddho’tra pūrvapakṣaḥ |

atrocyate---śrutiliṅgayorvirodhe śrutirbalīyasī iti | kutaḥ ? arthaviprakarṣāt |
kimidaṃ arthaviprakarṣāditi arthasya viprakarṣaḥ arthaviprakarṣaḥ | kaḥ punararthaḥ ? śrutyarthaḥ

[p. b776]

gārhapatyamupatiṣṭhata iti sannikṛṣṭaḥ śrutyarthaḥ | indraḥ upastheya iti viprakṛṣṭaḥ | kathaṃ ?
‘kadācana starī:’
 ityanena mantreṇendra upasthātavya iti na śrūyate | satyapīndrābhidhānasāmarthye
vacanābhāvat anupasthānīyaḥ indra ityavagamyate
 | nanu pūrvamevoktaṃ satyāṃ kartavya-
tāyāṃ indrābhidhānaṃ pramāṇaṃ, tataścobhayatrābhidhāne tulyataiva | ato
dvayoḥ prāmāṇyamiti | kimidaṃ punarākṣiptam samāhitaṃ ca ?
“dharmasya śabdamūlatvādaśabdamanapekṣaṃ syāt iti | yadetat prakaraṇaṃ liṅgaṃ ca ubhyamapyeta-
daśābdam | na cātikrāntapratyakṣa eva evaṃlakṣaṇake’rthe śabdamantareṇa paricchedo’vakalpate |
ato manyāmahe viprakṛṣṭaṃ śrutyarthālliṅgamiti”

 ucyate---aśābdametaditi viśeṣaṃ
pradarśayitum | nanu caivaṃ sati prāmāṇyamevāsya hīyate | kuto

[p. b777]

virodhaḥ | adhāyamabhiprāyaḥ asati virodhe smṛtivalliṅgasya prāmāṇya-
miti | tadayuktam | kutaḥ ? smṛterhi arthamavagamya virodhādviparyayaḥ
kalpyate | iha punaḥ upasthānaṃ naivāvagamyata ityuktam | kathamana-
vagamyamāne’rthe’numānam | tasmāddurgṛhītastenasaṃbhāṣitavadasaṃbandhaṃ
pratibhāti | punarapi kartavyatāpekṣālliṅgādevārthāvagatiṃ darśayati |
śrutisaṃbhave vā avagativākyasādhāraṇye sati kathamayaṃ viśeṣa
iti vaktavyam | atrābhidhīyate---kartavyasyetikartavyatākāṅkṣasya
vacanaṃ prakaraṇamiti prakaraṇādaidamarthyalakṣaṇa eva saṃbandhaḥ na punaḥ
anvitapadārthalakṣaṇa ityuktam | tataḥ kim ? anvitapadārtha-

[p. b778]

lakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ padārthebhya evāvasīyate | aidamarthyalakṣaṇastu kevalaṃ
kāryata eveti na padārthānvaye vyāpāro’vagamyate | evamapi ko
viśeṣaḥ ? ayamasti viśeṣaḥ---1padārthānvitalakṣaṇaḥ tasmin
sati paścātkāryayogaḥ pada rthānvayalakṣaṇastu prathamata eva | so’yamartha-
viprakarṣaḥ | anvayapradarśanārthaṃ ca tārtīyaṃ pramāṇaṣaṭkaṃ śeṣaśeṣivido
manyante | sa cānvayaḥ śrutau prathamataramavagamyata iti na kāryataḥ
pāścātyāyāṃ padārthānvayāvagatau pramāṇamasti | nahyanvitasyānvayo
dṛśyate yataḥ | tasyāt sūktaṃ liṅgāt śrutirbalīyasīti |
ānumānikamabhidhānaṃ ca padārthānvayāpekṣameva draṣṭavyam |

[p. b779]

1vikalpasya cānyāyyatvāditi mantravākyāpekṣam | nendraśabdasyā-
bhidhānadvayaṃ nyāyyamityabhiprāyaḥ | ato na viniyuktasya viniyogo
ghaṭata iti pradarśito hetuḥ | yaduktamavirodha iti; evamukto virodhaḥ
yadutendraśabdasya mantravākyāpekṣayā nābhidhānadvayamupapadyata
iti | aidamarthyasyānvayalabhyatvāt śrutāvuparatā kartavyatāpekṣeti
nākāṅkṣādvayamupapadyata iti virodhaḥ | tadidamakṛtasāmarthyatvaṃ
mantravākyasya niyogasya coktaṃ vākyaprakaraṇaśabdābhyām |

[p. b780]

ata upapanno’rthaviprakarṣaḥ | 1“liṅgavākyayorvirodhe kimudāharaṇam ?
syonaṃ te sadanaṃ kṛṇomi ghṛtasya dhārayā suśevaṃ kalpayāmi
tasmin sīda amṛte pratitiṣṭha vrīhīṇāṃ medha sumanasyamānaḥ iti”
 |

nanu cārthaviprakarṣādityukto hetuḥ sāpekṣanirapekṣatvaṃ ca liṅga-
vākyayoḥ |

[p. b781]

ataḥ prakaṭa evārthaviprakarṣaḥ kimityadhikaraṇāntaramāra-
bhyate | na kevalamidamadhikaraṇaṃ, sarvādhikaraṇākṣepa evāyam |
sujñāno hyarthaviprakarṣaḥ | kathaṃ? liṅgaṃ tāvaduktaṃ nirapekṣatvāditi |
vākyamapi padārthānvayāvagamyamānasaṃbandhaṃ aidamarthyalakṣaṇāt balīya
iti pradarśitam | prakaraṇamapi tadapekṣitvāt kramasya kramādūlīyaḥ|
evaṃ kramo’pi saṃbandhakṛttayā vedāvagamyatvācca samākhyāyā balīyā-
neva | ato nārambhaṇīyānyevaitāni adhikaraṇānītyarthaviprakarṣajñānā-
bhimānino manyate | atrocyate---yattāvadidamuktaṃ nirapekṣatvālliṅgaṃ
balīya iti | tadayuktam | kasmāt ? sakalasya hi mantrasya kartavya-
tāvagamāt na kevalāt padāt kartavyatāvagamo bhavati |

[p. b782]

ato vākyameva prathamataraṃ padānāṃ parasparasaṃbandhāvagame hetuḥ | saṃba-
ndhasya ca kāryāvagatiḥ | tasmādvākyameva balīya iti bhavati matiḥ |
avaśyaṃ caitadevamabhyupagantavyam | anyathā hi avirodhisyonādi-
padāntarasaṃbandho’pi nirapekṣatvālliṅgasya viniyoge na syāt |
ato durjñāno’yaṃ vākyaliṅgayoḥ viprakarṣa iti prārabdhamidamadhikaraṇam |
ayameva ca pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ padāntarasavyapekṣaṃ liṅgaṃ viniyo-
jakamityubhayatra viniyogaḥ | yatpunaridamuktaṃ1 liṅgasya śrutyāpi bādhi-
tatvāditi | ko’yaṃ hetuḥ ? na hi śrutyā bādhitasyānyenāpi bādhyatā |

[p. b783]

tasmāt bāliśabhāṣitamevedaṃ pratibhāti | nedaṃ bāliśabhāṣitam |
anyo’bhiprāyaścodakasya | yathaiva śrutyā padārthānvayasaṃbandhajñānakāraṇa-
tvena liṅgaṃ bādhitaṃ kāryaikyaviniyojyatvādevaṃ vākyenāpīti pūrvapakṣi-
tam | atrābhidhīyate---yajuṣastāvadviniyogaḥ pratipadam | kāryataḥ
punaraikyaṃ padānāṃ, yāvadekaṃ kāryaṃ tāvadaviruddhaḥ padāntarasaṃbandhaḥ |
kāryāntare tu pratyarthini na kāryāntarasaṃbandhitā śakyate pratipattuṃ
pratipadavidhānādeva | atra hi kāryato mantratvam | na mantrataḥ kāryaṃ

[p. b784]

yathā ṛkṣu sāmasu ca | ataḥ kāryanirapekṣakevalapadārthānvaya-
saṃbandhitāṃ vākyanibandhinīṃ bādhate liṅgam |

nanu ca śrutāvapyevameva prāpnoti | satyaṃ prāpnoti yadīdaṃ yajuḥ
syāt tattu śrutiviniyojyatvaṃ ṛkṣu pradarśitam | ṛcāṃ tu
mantrataḥ kāryamityuktam | yajuṣāṃ punaḥ yatrāpi iṣe tveti viniyogaḥ
tatrāpyupādānavaśādvicchedaḥ na punaḥ kāryopādānābhyāṃ anyo
yajuṣṭve heturasti | tasmādyukto vākyabhedaḥ | yatpunaridaṃ bhāṣya-
kāreṇa pradarśitaṃ---1“nanu caikavākyatvepi liṅgameva vyāpriyata”
 iti |
tadayuktamiva pratibhāti | kimiti, kāryato’trārthanirṇayaḥ | kutaḥ ?

[p. b785]

na sāmarthyamātrāt, kāryasavyapekṣaṃ hi sāmarthyaṃ liṅgamiti
nirūpitam | yadapi ca parihṛtaṃ---“arthaviprakarṣo’tra bādhyatve hetuḥ |
asti caikavākyabhūtasya liṅgasyābhidhānālliṅgāt viprakarṣaḥ”

iti | tadapyayuktamiva | ekavākyatāhetorliṅgasyāliṅgatvā-
dityuktam | atrocyate---pratipadaṃ viniyogāt ekavākya-
tayāpi ca kāryopapatteḥ yuktā liṅgatāśaṅkā | parihāraścārtha-
viprakarṣāt | tasmānnātra śrutirliṅgaṃ vākyamiti tantraṃ, viprakṛṣṭaṃ
aprāpakamiti sūtrārthaḥ | ataḥ siddhaṃ kāryabhedādvākyabheda iti | tarhi
tasmin sīdeti kimālambanamidaṃ ? pūrvaprakṛtālambanamevevati brūmaḥ |
nanu caivaṃ sati parārthataiva pūrvasya prāpnoti tataśca vākyaṃ
balavadityuktaṃ bhavati | nāvaśyaṃ parāmarśe tādarthyameva

[p. b786]

bhavati | anyārthamapyanyārthenāvamṛśyate eva sarvanāmnetyadoṣaḥ | yathā
tatprayojako heturiti, yathā ca vede etasyaiva revatīṣviti |
tasmādvalīyo’bhidhānamiti siddham | aya 1vākyaprakaraṇayoḥ
kathamiti | kiṃ punaḥ prakaraṇaṃ nāmeti | ko’yaṃ praśnaḥ ? itikartavya-
tārthitvādityevaṃ nirjñāte prakaraṇe na vidmaḥ kimartho’yaṃ praśna iti |
atrocyate---iti kartavyatārthitvādityuktam | na vidmaḥ kasyeyamiti
kartavyatārthiteti | yasyeyamitikartavyatākāṅkṣā tadatrocyate
---kartavyasyeti niyogārthasyetyarthaḥ | tasyetikartavyatākāṅkṣasya
yeyaṃ tṛtīyākāṅkṣā tatprakaraṇaṃ tena biniyogaḥ | tadviniyoge kāraṇaṃ
na kartavyatāmātraṃ nāpyanyat | ta smādupapannaḥpraśnārthaḥ, uttaraṃ ca |

[p. b787]

idānīṃ vākyaprakaraṇayoḥvirodhe “1tulyabale ete kāraṇe”
 iti
ko’bhiprāyaḥ ? kāryārthatvādanvayasya ubhayaṃ kāryeṇa viniyujyata
iti | yadvā kāryādṛte’nvayasya bādhitatvāt prakaraṇameva balīyaḥ |
tatpratyayatvādvākyasya | evaṃ manyānenoktaṃ vākyasya bādhitatvāditi |

[p. b788]

atrocyate---atrāpyarthaviprakarṣādityukto hetuḥ | kathamartha-
viprakarṣaḥ uktaḥ? kāryārthatayānvayaḥ kalpyaḥ | na ca klṛptānva-
yasya kāryārthatve siddhe’nvayāntaraṃ viniyuktaviniyogavirodhāt
liṅgavacchakyate pratipattum | tasmādvākyaṃ balīyaḥ | durjñānaviprakarṣa-
jñāpanāyādhikaraṇaprapañco’yaṃ liṅgavat sarvatra draṣṭavyaḥ | udāharaṇa-
muktam | “1 atha prakaraṇasya kramasya ca virodhe kimudāharam”
 iti |
atrāpi pūrvapakṣavādī manyate kramaprakaraṇayostulyabalatvamiti |

[p. b789]

kathaṃ ? bhāṣyakārastāvat ajñānameva vyapadiśati1 | kimajñānāttulya-
balatvaṃ sidhyati ? jñānasya hi tulyabalatvaṃ atulyabalatvaṃ vā viṣayaḥ
nājñānasya | nanu cājñānamapi pramāṇatayopanyastameva | na, vyavahāra-
mātraṃ tadityuktam | pramāṇāntaraprameyābhāvamātraṃ prameyam | iha
tulyabalatvaṃ atulyabalatvaṃ ca na pramāṇāntaraprameyabhāvamātraṃ
śakyate vaktum | kathaṃ tarhyetadbhāṣyaṃ “na tāvadviśeṣamupalabhāmaha”

iti | bhāṣyakāra evātra praṣṭavyaḥ |

atrocyate---durvidagdhavacanamātramidam | ayamabhi-
prāyo bhāṣyakārasya | nātrānayorviśeṣo jñāyata iti

[p. b790]

sarvatra prakaraṇānusyūteḥ kramo’pi kāryavānavagamyate eva | kāryataśca
sannidhiranvitamavagamayatīti | pratyuta prakaraṇamevātra bādhyatayāva-
gamyate | viśeṣopasaṃhārāt sannidheḥ | na hi klṛptasya sannidheḥ
kalpanā śakyate vaktum | anvayasyeva | tatpunaridamanvāruhya-
vacanamityuktamādhastye’dhikaraṇe | sarvatra caivaṃ draṣṭavyam |

[p. b791]

atrocyate---kramātprakaraṇaṃ balīyaḥ | kutaḥ ? na hyanā-
kāṅkṣitasya kramaḥ saṃbandhe hetuḥ | sannidhimātraṃ hi kramaḥ |
na ca sannidhimātrāt saṃbandho bhāryā rājñaḥ puruṣo devadattasyetivata |
ākāṅkṣā ca prakaraṇavatāṃ tulyeti sarvapradhānasaṃbandho videvanādīnām
kramāddhi ākāṅkṣā, ākāṅkṣāto’nvayaḥ anvayācca tādarthyamiti |
so’yamarthaviprakarṣaḥ | yaduktaṃ kramādākāṅkṣopasaṃhāra iti tadayuktam |
sarvārthatvepi kasyacitsannidhānabhavaśyaṃbhāvīti pāṭhārthameva tat |

[p. b792]

bādhitasya hyanugraho nyāyya ityuktam | “prakaraṇavataḥ sākāṅkṣatvāt
sannidhāne cāmnātena paripūrṇenāpyavakalpata ekavākyatvaṃ iti | kimidaṃ
paripūrṇenāpi iti ? etaduktaṃ bhavati---na cetkramaḥ prakaraṇaṃ
ekavākyataiva nāsti | nanu ākāṅkṣā sannidhiryogyatvamiti
caikavākyatve hetuḥ | satyaṃ vākyārthāvagatau, iha punarvā-
kyārthasya vākyārthasya ca saṃbandhaḥ | sa cetikartavyatārthitayaiva
saṃbadhyata ityuktam | kramaḥ punaḥ vākyārthasya ca sannidhāna-
mityuktam | yathā bhāryārājñaḥ puruṣo devadattasyetyatra pradarśitam |

[p. b793]

“atha kramasamākhyayoḥ kathaṃ ?”
 kramo hyākāṅkṣāvataḥ sannidhaubhavati
viśeṣāvagatau hetuḥ | samākhyā punaḥ viśeṣanimittaiveti tulyabale
ete kāraṇe iti | tasmādubhayaṃ viniyojakam |

[p. b794]

atrābhidhīyate---kramo balīyān vaidiko hyasau sannidhiḥ |
samākhyāpunarlaukikī | nanu caivaṃ vadatā samākhyā naiva pramāṇamityuktaṃ
bhavati | maibaṃ vocaḥ arthaviprakarṣa evānena prakāreṇocyate | kathaṃ ?
samākhyā hi saṃbandhanimittā satī sannidhānādyavagamayati |
kramaḥ punaḥ svayameva sannidhiḥ | na ca pratyakṣasya sannidheḥ punaḥ
sannidhānāntarakalpanāṃ ghaṭate iti so’yamarthaviprakarṣaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi
samākhyā ? samavāyādibhiḥ upapadyate brāhmaṇagrāmavat |

[p. b795]

1“atha yat tatra tatrābhidhīyate idamanena bādhyaṃ idamanena
bādhyamiti tatra yadbādhyate kiṃ tadbādhakaviṣayaṃ prāptaṃ utāprāptamiti”
 |
kasya vyākhyānametat ? na kasyacidapīti brūmaḥ | kiṃ tarhi anena
atra kriyate | bhāṣyakāra evātra praṣṭavyaḥ | atrābhidhīyate
---śrutyādīnāṃ balābalacintāśeṣabhūtamidaṃ bhāṣyam | kathaṃ kṛtvā ?
balābalacintā hi bādhyāpekṣiṇī | tadidānīṃ bādhyaṃ kiṃ
bādhakaviṣayaṃ prāptaṃ sat bādhyate utāprāptamiti | ubhayathā’bhi-
hito doṣaḥ | nanu ca prāptabādho daśame darśita eva | kimiti
prāptau satyāṃ virodho darśitaḥ | ucyate---kāryataḥ prāptau na doṣaḥ |

[p. b796]

ihopadeśaścintyate | upadeśena ca prāptiḥ durbādheti tatraiva
daśame pradarśayiṣyāmaḥ | iha kasmāttanna pradarśyate ? pradarśita-
meva “pratipradhānamāvartante guṇā”
 iti | kathaṃ pratipradhānāvṛttyabhidhāne-
nābādhaḥ pradarśito bhavati | dvayoḥ prāmāṇyābhyupagamena
pratipradhānābhidhānāt | prāpteścāviśeṣāt | tasmāt ayuktamida-
manena bādhyamiti aprāpte’pi | atredānīmupadeśabādhavido
’bhidadhati | sāmānyataḥ prāptiṃ manvānā viśeṣamapaśyantaḥ smṛti---
pramoṣanimittaṃ prāptijñānaṃ vyapadiśanti śuktikāyāmiva rajata-
jñānam | viśeṣāvagamāttu tatteṣāṃ nivartata iti bādhaśabdaḥ |

[p. b797]

nanu cāyamukto bādhaḥ prathamapāda eva ‘yasya ca duṣṭaṃ
karaṇaṃ’
 ityatra kimiti punarupanyastaḥ ? |

[p. b798]

astyatra viśeṣaḥ tatra hi śuktikārthasyāgrahaṇāt rajate
ca smarāmīti pramoṣāt vyapadeśasiddhiḥ | iha punarāga-
mikatvena dvayasyāpi tulyatāpattirbhavatyeveti punarārabdham |
kiṃ punarāgamikatve dvayasyāpi tulyatāpattiḥ | tulyatāpattau
vā bādhaḥ | atrocyate---svato viparyayābhāvāt | na śuktikādi-
ṣviva svayameva viyarpayo bhavati aparīkṣitakāraṇasvabhāvasyāpi |

[p. b799]

iha punaḥ kāraṇaparīkṣādvārako viparyaya iti prārabdhamidama-
dhikaraṇam | tadviśudhyarthaṃ cedaṃ bhāṣyam | so’yaṃ aprāptabādha
iti sūktam || 8 ||

|| 6 || ahīno vā prakaraṇādnauṇaḥ || 15 ||
idamidānīṃ cintyate---kiṃ niyogasannidhirbalīyān

[p. b800]

uta viniyogasannidhiriti | pūrvapakṣavādī kāryārthatvādviniyogasya
prakṛtakāryasaṃbhave prakṛtakāryasaṃbandho yukta iti manyate | kathaṃ tarhi
viniyogaḥ ? kāryārthatvāt, tadanuguṇo vyākhyāyate | evaṃ tarhi prakara-
ṇādvākyaṃ durbalamityuktam, na, sannidhānāviśeṣe hi vākyaṃ balīyaḥ |
yatra punaḥ kāryasannidhireva nāsti kiṃ tatra vākyaṃ kariṣyati |

[p. b801]

tasmāt kāryānuguṇaiva padārthavyākhyeti yuktaṃ na hīyata
ityahīnaḥ |

1asaṃyogāttu mukhyasya tasmādapakṛṣyate || 16 ||
atra bhaṇyate---uktamasmābhiḥ padārthānvayaḥ saṃbandhakāraṇaṃ
kāryato’pyanvaya evāvagamyate | na cānvayāvagame pratītānvayatyāgo
yuktaḥ | na ca dṛṣṭe hastini tatpādenānumānaṃ saṃbhavati | tasmāda-
pratipakṣameva padārthavyākhyānaṃ yuktam | padārtha eva hi padārthaprati-
pakṣo bhavati na punaḥ kāryam | anyathā hi arthavādapadānāmapi
kartavyatābhidhānataiva syāt | tasmādahīnasyeti kratvantarārthataiva
nyāyyā |

[p. b802]

nanu ca tulyamevātra śrautatvaṃ ekatra na hīyata ityahītaḥ anya---
trānhāṃ samūha iti | kimasmākamanayā cintayā | ekatra śrauto’nyatra
gauṇa iti kṛtvā cintitam | na cedatra saṃbhavati udāharaṇāntaraṃ
saṃbhaviṣyati | yadvā rūḍhiṃ manyate bhāṣyakāraḥ satreṣvadarśanāt |

[p. b803]

vyutpattimātraṃ taddhitavidhiḥ śunaśśepādiṣu samāsavidhiriva |
tasyādidamapyupapadyata evodāharaṇam |

|| 9 || dvibahutvayuktaṃ codanāttasya || 17 ||
nanu caiva pūrveṇaivādhikaraṇena gatamidam | na hi pratīyamānānva-
yatyāgo yuktaḥ kāryānurodheneti | satyam---kintu astyatrādhikā-
śaṅkā | yathāśaktiprayoge nitye karmaṇi yajamānāntarapraveśamapi
manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | patnyabhiprāyaṃ caikaśeṣābhidhānam
tasmādanutkarṣaḥ |

[p. b804]

na prakṛterekasaṃyogāt || 19 ||
atrābhidhīyate---yajamānāntarapraveśastāvanna saṃbhavati | kutaḥ ?
yajamānasya hi yathā śaknuyādityucyate | tatra yanna saṃbhavati
tasyaiva gauṇārthatā, tyāgo vā, na punaryajamānāntaropādānena saṃpat |
sati caivaṃ na yajamānādvitvaṃ bahutvaṃ vā bhavati prakṛtau | yadapyuktaṃ
patnyabhiprāyā dvayoryajamānayoḥ bahubhya iti cābhidhānopapattiriti |
tadapyayuktam | tasmānna patnyabhiprāya iti itaretarayogo ghaṭate |

[p. b805]

ādhāne tu lakṣaṇayā, asaṃbhavāt śrautasya yathā ca patnyā
sahetaretarayogo nāsti yāge tathā ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | satriṇāmeva
hi saḥ | tasmādatrāpi pūrvavadutkarṣaḥ |

|| 10 || jāghanī caikadeśatvāt || 20 ||
kena punaḥ prasaṅgenedamadhikaraṇamāyātam ? anena prasaṅgena |
sthitaṃ tāvat prakaraṇāt vākyaṃ balīyaḥ sannidhāne

[p. b806]

cāsannidhāne ca kāryasya | atredaṃ sandihyate---kimayaṃ vākyārtha
utāyaṃ iti vākyārtha eva saṃśayaḥ | kiṃ jāghanyuddeśena patnīsaṃyājā
vidhīyante uta viparītamiti | pūrvapakṣī tāvat jāghanyuddeśena
patnīsaṃyājavidhānaṃ manyate | kutaḥ ? evaṃ śrauto’rtho-
vihito bhavati | anyathā hi vākyārthavidhānaṃ syāt |
nanu cāsmin pakṣe vākyārtha eva vihito bhavati anyadanya-
sminnucyate | anabhijño bhavān vacanavyaktīnām | na brūmo
vākyārthasya vidhisaṃbandho nāsti iti kiṃ tu vidheyo vākyārthaḥ |
etaduktaṃ bhavati---na sannikṛṣṭatyāgena viprakṛṣṭārthavidhānaṃ yuktamiti |
ayaṃ cāparo lābhaḥ jāghanyā paśupādānakalpanā na kṛtā bhavati |
tasmādatrāpyutkarṣaḥ |

[p. b807]

codanā vā’pūrvatvāt ||

atrābhidhīyate---jāghanyevātra patnīsaṃyājeṣu vidhīyate |
kutaḥ ? yāgavidhāne hi patnyartho vidhātavyo bhavati | nanu cātra
patnīsaṃyājāścodakena saṃnihitā eva | satyam---vidhīyamānatvena tu
prakaraṇāntare prayojanānyatvamiti karmāntarāpattiḥ | nanu cāsannidhānaṃ
tatra karmāntaratve hetuḥ na vidhīyamānatā, na cānanyārthatvamatra, jāgha-
nīyasaṃbandhārthatvādvākyasya | tasmānnāyaṃ vākyabhedaparihāra upa-
labhyate | ucyate---anabhijño devānāṃ priyaḥ śāstra-
bhedasya | vidhānasannidhirhi vihitavidhāne bhedamuparuṇaddhi,

[p. b808]

na padārthamātrasannidhiḥ | uddeśyatve hi bhavati padārthamātrasannidhirapya-
vagamahetuḥ, padārthamātrasavyapekṣatvāt uddeśya bhāvasya | tasmāda्yukto
vākyabhedaḥ | yatpunaridaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇobhayathā saṃbandhividhāna-
mupanyastaṃ tatkimartham ? ucyate | nāntarīyakasākṣādvidhānaviveka-
pradarśanārtham | pradarśanamidānīṃ kimarthaṃ ? anyatarāprāptyarthaṃ
neha prakaraṇe ubhayasyāvāptirupapadyate | tasmāt anyatara-
dvidheyam | sā ca jāghanyeva, patnīsaṃyājānāṃ prāptatvāttasmādanutkarṣaḥ |

[p. b809]

yatpunaridamuktaṃ na paśuṃ prayuṅkta iti asmadīyepi pakṣe laukikyāḥ
prayuktervidyamānatvāttulyam |

|| 11 || 1santardanaṃ prakṛtau krayaṇavadanarthalopāt syāt || 24 ||
atrāpyutkarṣāśaṅkā dīrghaśacdasyānanvayāt | dhṛtyā ityartha-
vādānupapatteśca | anutkarṣavādī tu latādairdhyeṇāpyanuvādaṃ manvānaḥ
dīrghasya vā somasya iti prakṛtāvevaitadvidhānamiti manyate | rāddhānta-
vādī punaḥ somāpekṣaivātra dīrghatā mantavyā na latāpekṣeti
ca nātirekādvinā’rthavādopapattiḥ iti saṃsthāsvityuktavān |
nanu cāsminnapi pakṣe prakaraṇādutkarṣa eva | saṃsthā hi vikārāḥ |

[p. b810]

yāgābhiprāyo’yamutkarṣaḥ kāryāpekṣayā tūtkarṣa evetyadoṣaḥ | yatpuna-
ridamuktaṃ sīsena krīṇātītivadvikalpa iti | nanu ca tatra
samuccayo’nayoḥ na vikalpaḥ daśabhireva krīṇātīti | bidhānavikalpa-
maṅgīkṛtyaitannidarśanam, na prayogavikalpam |

|| 12 || 1saṅkhyāyuktaṃ kratoḥ prakaraṇāt syāt || 32 ||
vākyārthasamavāyāpekṣiṇī prakaraṇādutkarṣacintā gatā, idānī-
mabhidhānavipratipattirvākya eva cintyate---kimayaṃ prathama-
śabdo jyotiṣṭoma eva vartate uta jyotiṣṭomaprayoga iti |

[p. b811]

‘eṣa vāva prathamo yajño yajñānāṃ’
 iti śravaṇāddhi spaṣṭaiva yajñavacanatā
gamyate “1evaṃ ca prakaraṇamanugrahīṣyate”
 iti pratiṣedhavidhyabhiprāyaṃ
tat |

2naimittikaṃ vā kartṛsaṃyogāt liṅgasya tannimittatvāt || 33 ||
atrābhidhīyate---satyamasmāt prayogāt avagamyate kintu
ya etenāniṣṭvā’thānyena yajeteti prayogādapi prāthamyamavagamyate

[p. b812]

nāniṣṭvā’thānyena yajeteti | kathaṃ ? kartṛsaṃbandhitayā śrūya-
māṇatvāt | kartuśca prayogeṇa saṃbandho na prayojyena | prayogapare
ca vākye eṣa vāva prathamo yajño yajñānāṃ yajjyotiṣṭoma iti śrutiḥ
na punaḥ prayojyaniṣṭhā | kvacidasya prayogo’vagamyate jyotiṣṭoma-
śabdasyaiva | ataḥ prayoga evāyaṃ iti na prathame prayoge pravṛñjyā-
dityarthaḥ | yajñaśabdaśca prayojyaviṣayatvāt prayoge’pyupapadyata eva |

[p. b813]

prathamaprayojyo yajñānāmityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca prakaraṇa eva pravargya-
vidhirupapanno bhavati |

|| 12 || 1pauṣṇaṃ peṣaṇaṃ vikṛtau pratīyetācodanātprakṛtau || 34 ||
pauṣṇaṃ peṣaṇaṃ uttaravivakṣayetyuktam |

|| 13 || 2tatsarvārthamaviśeṣāt || 35 ||
idamidānīṃ cintyate kiṃ yāvatpauṣṇaṃ tatsarvaṃ prapiṣṭaṃ kartavyaṃ uta
carureveti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī viśeṣāgrahaṇāt sarvārthamiti manyate |
nanu ca puroḍāśe prāptatvāt na śakyate vidhātuṃ; na, pauṣṇatayā aprāpteḥ

[p. b814]

tasmāt sarvārtham | nanu hṛdayādyākṛtivināśaḥ paśau bhavatu,
vacanāt | kimiva vacanaṃ na kuryāt | atrocyate---puroḍāśe
tāvat prāptatvānna vidhiḥ | nanu pauṣṇatayā’prāptirityuktam | kāryata
eva prāptiriṣyate na saṃbandhitayā anyathā hyarthavādapadānāmapi
kāryārthataiva syādityuktam | nanu caivaṃ sati prakaraṇādvākyaṃ durbala-
mityuktam | kārye satyanvayakalpanā ato balavattā uktā vākyasya |

[p. b815]

tasmānna vidhānaṃ puroḍāśe saṃbhavati | paśau tarhi saṃbhavati | na
hi | tarhi sāpekṣatvaprasaṅgāt śāstrasya | na ca nirapekṣe saṃbhavati
sāpekṣāśrayaṇaṃ yuktamityuktaṃ śabdavidbhiḥ | tasmāt carāveva peṣaṇaṃ
śabdāt pratīmaḥ | yatpunaridamuktaṃ 1yadyapītyevamādinā bhāṣya-
kāreṇa evamapi sāpekṣatvaṃ naiva parihartuṃ śakyata ityabhiprāyaḥ |
anvārohamātraṃ caitata | pūrvameva hyukto hetuḥ | nanu ca carāvapi
carutaiva hīyeta viśadasiddhaudane prayogadarśanāt | na, piṣṭakacarāvapi
paktisāmānyāt ityabhihitaḥ caruśabdaprayoga iti carāveva
peṣaṇamiti siddham |

[p. b816]

|| 15 || 1ekasminnekasaṃyogāt || 39 ||
atra bhāṣyakāreṇa indrapītāgneyaśabdābhyāṃ viśeṣo darśitaḥ
pūṣā prapiṣṭabhāgo bhavatīti | tathā adantako hīti hetuvacanaṃ somā-
pauṣṇe nemapiṣṭaścaruriti | somāpauṣṇe nemapiṣṭa ityanuvādo yuktaḥ |
nemapiṣṭavidhāne hi pauṣṇopalakṣaṇāt carurityavivakṣitaṃ syādityeva-
mādyadhikāśaṅkānirākaraṇārthamidamadhikaraṇam | atrocyate---katha-
māsāmāśaṅkānāṃ nirākaraṇaṃ ? kāryaprayuktatvāddharmāṇāmityukto
hetuḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ kāryeṇa saṃbandho devatāpadayuktānāṃ bhavatīti

[p. b817]

niṣprayojanatvāddevatāyāḥ kāryārthatvācca yāgasyeti lakṣaṇopapadyate |
devatā cedupalakṣaṇaṃ na dvidevatākeṣūpalakṣayituṃ kṣamaḥ | adevatā hi
sā tatra | adantakatvādīnāṃ tu hetuvannigadatvenārthavādatvamuktam |
arthavādāśca guṇavādena vyākhyeyāḥ | nemapiṣṭaśabdasya nyāyā-
petatvāt dvidevatākeṣu viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ bhaviṣyati | tasmādeka-
devatākeṣu peṣaṇamiti siddham |

iti bṛhatyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya
tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b818]

tṛtīyādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1nivītamiti manuṣyadharmaḥ śabdasya tatpradhānatvāt 3-4-1 ||
kathaṃ punaḥ vidhinā tvekavākyatvāditi sthite’rthavādatve puna-
rvidhānāśaṅkā | ekavākyatve hi na vidhidvayamavakalpata iti na nivī-
tatvādividhisaṃbhavaḥ | pākṣikīṃ prāptiṃ manvāno vidhiṃ manyata iti cet |
tadapyayuktam | tasyāpi vacanāntareṇa prāptatvāt upapadyata evārtha-
vādaḥ | ucyate---prakaraṇāṅgatayā’prāptiṃ manvānenedamadhikaraṇaṃ cinti-
tam | vidhānepi prakaraṇāṅgatā na saṃbhavatīti sthitamarthavādatvam |

[p. b819]

nanu cātithyādāvaprāptirvidyata iti yuktam | na | tadanvayāna-
vagamāt | manuṣyamātrasaṃbandhitāyāṃ tu prāyādibhirupapannatvāt
arthavādataiveti siddham |

|| 2 || 1digvibhāgaśca tadvatsambandhasyārthahetutvāt || 3-4-10 ||
|| 3 || paruṣiditadhṛtapūrṇavidagdhaṃ ca tadvat || 3-4-11 ||
tadvadityayamatideśaḥ kimabhyadhikaṃ paśyataḥ ? anyathā
hyupavīte prāptiḥ, anyathā dikṣu, anyathā paruṣi dinādiṣviti
viśeṣapradarśanārtho’yamatideśaḥ |

[p. b820]

|| 4 || 1akarmakratusaṃyuktaṃ saṃyogānnityānuvādaḥ syāt || 3-4-12 ||
uktaṃ nivītādhikaraṇena kratudharmatayā nivītādi pratīyate manu-
ṣyaśravaṇāditi | prāyeṇa ca prāptatvādanuvāda iti | atrāpīdānīṃ
tadvadevānuvādaṃ manvānasya pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | nanu ca tatra manuṣyaśabda-
śravaṇādyukto’nuvādaḥ prāyikīṃ ca prāptimāśritya arthavādaḥ | ucyate |
atrāpi pūrvaprāptapratiṣedhāt gamyata eva pumān yasyāsau pratiṣedhaḥ |
pratiṣedhaśāstre hi sarvatra pratiṣidhyamānakriyākarturevādhikāraḥ

[p. b821]

ataḥ pūrvaprāptasyaivāyamanuvādaḥ | vidyate ca prāptiḥ arthataḥ |
