prāptimātratayā ca pratiṣedhopapatteḥ na prakaraṇārthatā | nanu cāyamanu-
vādaḥ kasya vidheḥ śeṣaḥ ? na kasyacidapīti brūmaḥ | prāptiranuvādatve
hetuḥ nārthavādatā |

[p. b822]

ekavākyatvena hi arthavādo bhavati na prāptimapekṣate
yathā jārtilayavāgvā juhuyāditi | tasmānnānuvādaḥ | nanu cedaṃ
śrutivākyaṃ tasyāḥ smṛtermūlaṃ bhaviṣyati | na bhavitu-
marhati | atra hi śrūyamāṇasya prakaraṇārthatā’vasīyate na puruṣārthatā
anuvādapakṣe punaḥ puruṣārthatayaiva prāpteḥ siddhā puruṣārthatā | nanu ca

[p. b823]

kratvarthatayāvagamyamānasya kathaṃ puruṣārthatayā’nuvādo yuktaḥ kalpa-
yitum | ucyate---kraturapi hi puruṣārtha eva | atastatsaṃbandhi-
tayāpi puruṣasyecchātaḥ prāpnotyevānṛtavadanamiti na doṣaḥ tatsaṃbandhā-
vagateḥ | upanayottarakālabhāvitvācca kratūnāṃ siddho’nuvādaḥ |

1vidhirvā saṃyogāntarāt || 3-4-13 ||
atrābhidhīyate | āyuṣmataivoktaṃ na smṛtivākyasyedaṃ mūlatāṃ
pratipadyata iti | evaṃ cet tvayaivedaṃ saṃyogāntaramabhyupagataṃ saṃyogā-

[p. b824]

ntaratve ca nānuvādaḥ śakyate vaktum | nanu ca pratiṣedhe
pratiṣidhyamānakriyākarturevādhikāra ityuktam | satyamuktam | na tu
tat prakaraṇe śakyate vaktum | kimiti ? padārthamātra eva
pratiṣedhopapatteḥ laukikeṣu punaḥ puruṣecchāta eva pravṛttirviṣayatāṃ
pratipadyate pratiṣedhasyeti tatkriyākarturevādhikāro yuktaḥ | iha
punaḥ kratvarthatayaiva pravṛttirgamyate padārthasyeti na tasya karturadhikāraḥ
śakyate vaktum | nanu ca kartaivākhyāteṣu pradhānatayāvagamyate |

[p. b825]

niyogābhedāttu prayājādīnāmaṅgatvabhavagamyate | saveṣāmekaphalānu-
bandhatvāt | saṃskārakarmaṇāmapi kratvarthasya saṃskāryatayā’vagateḥ
yuktaivāṅgatā | pratiṣedhe punaḥ pratiṣedhyasya akratvarthatvāt
ākhyātena ca kartrabhidhānāt kartureva pratīyate | na ca tatsaṃbandhā-
vagatirviruddhā | kratorapi puruṣārthatvāt upanayanottarakālabhāvi-
tvācca tadarthapratipatteḥ siddho’nuvāda ityuktam |

[p. b826]

atrocyate---syādetadevaṃ yadyākhyātānāṃ kartā’bhidheyaḥ syāt,
na punarākhyātānāṃ kartrabhidhānaśaktiḥ śakyate kalpayitum |
kriyābhidhānādeva sarvakārakāvagateḥ | kathaṃ tarhi bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ
sūtraṃ laḥ karmaṇi ca bhāve cākarmakabhyaḥ cakārāt kartari ceti | saṅkhyā-
vidhānārthametaditi pradarśitam | saṅkhyāviśeṣavidhānāśrayaṃ ca yak
śabiti ca | anyakārakasaṅkhyābhidhānamidānīṃ kimiti na bhavati ?
tulye’vagamyamānatve | na lokaḥ paryanuyoktuṃ śakyate | durjayo
hi mahājanaḥ; laukikaprayogamūlaṃ cedaṃ śāstraṃ bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ

[p. b827]

prayuktasya hi niyamamācaṣṭe | nāprayuktasyābhidhānam | tasmāda-
paryanuyogo’yam | ataḥ kratvarthapadārthamātrapravṛttyāśrayamevedaṃ pratiṣedha-
śāstram | nanu ca pratiṣedhyagatā pravṛttiḥ na kratvarthatyuktam | aco-
ditatvāt | naiva tādarthyena coditatvaṃ saṃbandhahetuḥ upakārakatayāpi
saṃbandhitopapadyata eva | saṃbandhini ca vidhiḥ kratvartho bhavatītyuktaṃ
payo brataṃ brāhmaṇasyetyevamādiṣu | nanu copakārakaviṣayatayā
pratiṣedhasya kratvarthapuruṣārthayorna kaścidviśeṣaḥ | yathāsti viśeṣa-
stathoktam |

[p. b828]

|| 5 || 1ahīnavatpuruṣadharmastadarthatvāt ||3-4-14 ||
kaḥ punaḥ nivītaṃ manuṣyāṇāmityanena viśeṣaḥ jañjabhyamānasya ?
ayamasti viśeṣaḥ nārthavādāt parihāraḥ saṃbhavati | avākya-
śeṣatvāt | nāpyanuvādo nānṛtaṃ vadeditivat | nāpyatrārthaprāptasya
viṣayatvasaṃbhavo nānṛtaṃ vadeditivat | na hi jañjabhyamānatāṃ
kadācidapi prāpnoti anṛtavadanavat | tasmājjañjabhyamāna-
mātrasya mantravidhānamidaṃ na prakaraṇe’vasthāpayituṃ śakyate |

[p. b829]

nanu caivaṃ sati phalakalpanā syāt | bhavatu vacanāt, ko doṣaḥ ?
ato vākyāt prakaraṇabādhaḥ siddho yathā dvādaśāhīna-
syeti | atrābhidhīyate---na phalakalpanāmarhatyetadvacanam | kutaḥ ?
vākyaṃ hi padārthasaṃbandhamātre prabhavati na kārye | kāryaṃ hi
niyogasamadhigamyameva prakṛtaścāyaṃ dārśapaurṇamāsiko niyogaḥ |
sarvamevedaṃ viniyogakāraṇam | na ca kāryāntarakalpanā

[p. b830]

klṛpte sati saṃbhavati iti prakaraṇa eva niveśaḥ | nanu ca jañjabhya-
mānatā na kratvarthatayā’nṛtavadanavat saṃbhavati | mābhūt jañjabhya-
mānatā kratvarthā | kartustu kratvarthatā prakaraṇasaṃbandhe heturbhaviṣyati |
tasmāt kratoḥ kartuḥ dharmo’yamiti siddham | ahīne punaḥ
kāryatvād्yuktaḥ utkarṣaḥ |

|| 6 || 1śaṃyau ca sarvaparīdānāt || 3-4-17 ||
śaṃyau punaḥ śatena yātayāditi phalaśruteḥ na prakaraṇa-
kalpanā nyāyyā | svavākyasthā hi kāryāvagatiḥ balīyasī

[p. b831]

tasmāt puruṣamātradharmau’yaṃ na brāhmaṇāyāvagureditivat | nanu
jañjabhyamānepi ‘prāṇāpānāvevātman dhatta’
 iti phalaśravaṇabhastyeva |
na | dhatta iti vartamānopadeśāt na sādhyatayāvagamyate | iha punaḥ
yātayāta iti bhaviṣyadarthatāṃ liṅavagamayatīti viśeṣaḥ |
tasmānmūlasmṛteḥ prāptasyānuvāda iti |

[p. b832]

|| 7 || prāgaparodhānmalavadvāsasaḥ1 || 3-4-18 ||
malavadvāsasā saha saṃvādapratiṣedhe sandehaḥ kiṃ kratvarthaḥ saṃvādaḥ
uta puruṣārtha iti | kiṃ prāptaṃ ? nānṛtaṃ vadeditivat kratau saṃbhavāt
kratvartha iti prāpte | ucyate---na prāptatvāt | kratvarthatayā hi
“tāmaparudhya yajeta”
 | iti prāpta evāyamasaṃvādaḥ| tasmāt
puruṣārthaḥ vākyaṃ ca prakaraṇādvalīya ityuktameva |

[p. b833]

nanu puruṣārthatayāpi prāpta evāyamasaṃvādaḥ “nākalyāṃ
nārīmabhiramayenna rajasvalāṃ”
 iti | asyāḥ smṛteridameva mūlaṃ
bhaviṣyatīti | anuvādo vā sarvathā tāvat kratvarthatā
nāsti | nanu ca saṃvādamātrapratiṣedho’yam | ucyate--‘nāsyā
annamadyāt abhyañjanaṃ vāva striyā annaṃ”
 ityupagamanapratiṣedha
evāyamiti |

[p. b834]

|| 8 || aprakaraṇe tu taddharmastato viśeṣāt || 28 ||
‘hiraṇyaṃ bhāryaṃ’
 ityanārabhyavādāt puruṣārtho’yam | na hyatra
kratussannihito’vagamyate | tasmātpuruṣārthaḥ | nanu ca prayojanavataḥ
saṃskāro, na niṣprayojanasya saṃskāryatā yuktā dvitīyānirdeśācca
hiraṇyasaṃskāra evāyaṃ yuktaḥ saṃskṛtaṃ kratorupakariṣyatīti |
atrābhidhīyate---syādetadevaṃ yadi saṃskṛtasya hiraṇyasya karmārthatā
upapadyate---na tāvat prakṛtaṃ karmāsti | na ceha karmacodanā, dravya-
devatāsaṃyogābhāvāt | tasmāt dravyasaṃskārānarthakyāt puruṣa-
dharme’yam | nanu cātra phalaṃ kalpayitavyam ? niyogānāṃ
kāryārthatvāt prakṛtasya kāryāsyāvidyamānatvāt na doṣaḥ kalpanāyām |

[p. b835]

tasmāddhiraṇyadhāraṇaṃ karturdharmaścodyata iti sthitam | yadi kartṛ-
saṃskāratvena phalaṃ kalpyate kimiti dravyasaṃskāratayā kratūpakāro na
kalpyate | ayamasti viśeṣaḥ ekavākyāvagatena hyarthavādena
kartṛsambandhe phalakalpanā upapadyate na punaraprakṛtena kratunā artha-
viprakarṣāt | ayaṃ cāparo viśeṣaḥ sarvatra draṣṭavyaḥ kartṛsambandhe
hyadhikārasiddhiḥ adhikṛtasya ca phalakalpanā nyāyyā | na punaḥ
dravyasambandhe prayojanakalpaneti saktuṣu darśitam |

[p. b836]

|| 9 || śeṣo’prakaraṇe’viśeṣāt sarvakarmaṇāt || 31 ||
yena karmaṇertset iti aviśeṣāt sarvakarmārthateti pūrvapakṣavādī
manyate | nanu cānāhitāgnerāhavanīyābhāvāt nāyaṃ homaḥ
saṃbhavati anāhavanīya evāyaṃ bhaviṣyati pradhānānurodhāt | na
guṇānurodhena pradhānasyāṅtyāgo yuktaḥ | guṇa eva hi tattyāgaḥ

[p. b837]

pradhānānurodhenetyaṅgavido manyante | satyamevaitat | kintvatra ayaṃ
nyāyo na saṃbhavati | kasmāt ? vaidike tāvadāhavanīyabādho
nopapadyate | na ca laukikasyaivāṅgamiti śakyate vaktuṃ tvayaivoktama-
viśeṣāditi | ato bhāvābhāvayorvirodhāt bhāvata eva viśeṣā-
vadhāraṇaṃ yuktam na punaḥ abhāvataḥ | anavagame hetuḥ asau | na punaḥ
viśeṣāvagame hetuḥ | tasmādvaidika eva jayāhoma iti siddham |

|| 10 || doṣāttviṣṭirlaukike syāt śāsrāddhi vaidike na doṣaḥ syāt1 || 34 ||

[p. b838]

pauṇḍarīke śrūyate---yāvato’śvān pratigṛhṇīyāt iti | kiṃ
laukike’śvapratigrahe iyamiṣṭiḥ uta vaidika iti saṃśayaḥ | laukike
dośadarśanāt śūdrādanyasmādvā pāpakarmaṇaḥ kṛte doṣo bhavatīti |
“1na doṣasaṅkīrtanaṃ prāyaścittaviṣayaviśeṣaṇam | kintu prāyaścittastu-
tyarthena”
 iti | kimidaṃ prāyaścittaviṣayaviśeṣaṇaṃ kiṃ vā stutyartheneti़
idamidam | asyārthasyāpanuttaye yadā prāyaścittaṃ tadā viṣaya-
viśeṣaṇam | yadā tu kāryāntarārthaṃ tadā stutyarthena doṣa-
nirghātārthaṃ cedam | tasmāt laukike bhavitumarhati | “2nanu
vaidikepi apratigrāhyāt pratigṛhṇato doṣo’styeva | ucyate |

[p. b839]

bhavedetadenaṃ yadi pratigrahasya karturiṣṭirbhavet | sā khalu
yathā hetukartuḥ tathā uttare’dhikaraṇe vakṣyāmaḥ”
 | asambandha-
midaṃ bhāṣyam | yadi laukike pratigrahīturiṣṭiḥ na bhavati tataśca
vaidike’pi aśvaṃ apratigrāhyāt pratigṛhṇato doṣo’stīti satyapi
doṣanirghātārthe prāyaścitte kasmāllaukika eva pratigrahītuḥ prāyaścittā-
śaṅkā | atha upakramāvagatārthavādadarśanāt dāturiṣṭirityucyate
laukike’pi tulyam | atha laukike pratigrahapakṣepi dāturevetyabhiprāyaḥ
śūdrādanyasmādvā pāpakarmaṇaḥ iti bhāṣyaṃ asambaddham | kathaṃ voparitane-
’dhikaraṇe pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | vedo vā prāyadarśanādityupakrameṇopasaṃhārāva-
dhṛtteḥ | atrābhidhīyate-nedamasaṃbaddham | ayamabhiprāyaḥ pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ |

[p. b840]

laukike’śvapratigrahe doṣaśravaṇaṃ viṣayaviśeṣaṇatayaivopapatteḥ | yasmā-
dasmin pakṣe pratigrahītureveṣṭiḥ | dāturyadīyamiṣṭiḥ syāt tato
dānasyāṅgaṃ vā kalpayitavyaṃ phalāntaraṃ vā | dānāṅgatā tāvanna saṃbhavati
nimittatvenāsyopakṣayāddānasya | atha viparivṛttyā punaraṅgitvaṃ
dānasya tadā idamevāyuktam | guṇavādenāpyarthavādopapatteḥ | pāpa-
pramocanasya phalatayā vā vākyenāvagateḥ | na ca dātuḥ pāpaṃ
śakyate kalpayitumiti | tasmālaukike pakṣe pratigrahītureveṣṭiḥ

[p. b841]

coditatvālloke vede ca dānasya | rāddhāntavādī tu yathāśrutasya
pāpasyāśvapratigraho na nidānatayā pramāṇāntareṇa prasiddhaḥ | nāpi
vacanāt | vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | tasmāt pāpamātravacanamidaṃ
vaidikepyupapadyata eva, rakṣaṇapoṣaṇādinā duḥ khadarśanāt | phalaṃ
ca na kalpayitavyaṃ bhavati karmāṅgatayopapatteḥ | prakaraṇaviniyojyatayā
karturvā bhavatu pratigrahīturvā |

[p. b842]

|| 11 || acoditaṃ ca karmabhedāta1 || 30||
yuktaṃ tāvat yadā kartuḥ tadā karmāṅgaṃ; yadā pratigrahītuḥ tadā
kathaṃ ? upakramopasaṃhāravaśāt pratigrāhayeditivat vipariṇāmāt
kalpyatām | ṛtvijo yājanīyāḥ upavāsavat |

yatpunaridamuktaṃ---kathaṃ punaḥ vedo vā prāyadarśanāditi
sthite’dhikaraṇe uparitane’dhikaraṇe pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | doṣapradarśanādeveti
manyate | arthavādabhūto’pi doṣaḥ pratigrahīturevāñjasyenopapadyate |
upakramo’pi laukikapakṣavata dāturapi tāvat aśvapratigrahe doṣaḥ
kiṃ punaḥ pratigrahīturiti vyākhyāyate | rāddhāntavādī tu avaśyaṃ

[p. b843]

tāvat nirvāpayediti vipariṇāmaḥ kalpyaḥ | tasmādvaramupakrama-
darśanāt pratigrāhayedityevaṃ manyate | ato nedaṃ punaruktam |

|| 12 || pānavyāpacca tadvat1 || 32 ||
tadvadityātideśiko’pi viśeṣaḥ | ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ doṣadarśanam |
nanu ca pūrvasminnadhikaraṇe doṣadarśanādeva laukika ityuktam |
satyamanupapattistatroktā doṣasya | iha tu glānirvamanādupapadyata
evendriyāṇāmityadhikāśaṅkā | ata eva vamanārthaṃ taditi parihṛtam |

[p. b844]

glānyarthameva yat na tasya glānirdoṣatvena śakyate kalpayitumiti |
tasmāt pūrvavadevedaṃ karmaphalakalpanābhayāt coditasomapānavamana-
nimittakamityarthaḥ |

|| 13|| tatsarvatrāviśeṣāt1 || 34 ||
kiṃ yāvantassomavāminaḥ tāvatāṃ vamane etatprāyaścittaṃ uta
svāmina eveti | aviśeṣāt sarvārthamiti prāpte | ucyate
---svāmina evedaṃ prāyaścittam | kutaḥ ? na yajamānasya ṛtvijāṃ
caikā codanā saṃbhavati | ekatra yajeteti codanā anyatra yājayediti

[p. b845]

na caikasmin vākye ubhayametat bhavati | kasmāt ? avipariṇatā eṣā
codanā | sā ca svābhivamana evāvagamyate | tasmāt phalavatsanni-
dhāvaphalaṃ tadaṅgamiti bhavati tadidamuktam | nānyadīyo yāgo’nyasyo-
pakarotīti | tasmānna ṛtvigvamananimittamiti | na hyanyasya
coditamanyasyopakārotītyarthaḥ | ataḥ svāmivamananimittameveti
siddham |

|| 14 || 1sarvapradānaṃ haviṣastadarthatvāt || 3-4-37 ||

[p. b846]

ihedānīṃ sandihyate | ‘āgneyo’ṣṭākapāla’
 iti kiṃ kṛtsnasya
haviṣastyāgaḥ uta dvirhaviṣo’vadyatīti tāvanmātrasyaiva tyāga iti |
nanu cātra sandeha eva nopapadyate āgneyo’ṣṭākapāla iti pari-
samāptā codanā | dvirhaviṣo’vadyatīti saṃskāracodanā haviṣaḥ
agnestṛṇānyapacinotītivat | nanu ca juhotītyapi śrūyate
haviṣo’panītasya pratipattirbhaviṣyati | yathā yāgottarakālaṃ
sviṣṭakṛdādayaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---naitadevam | juhotiḥ yāgāśca
saṃbandhidravyasādhyāḥ | sakalasya ca parityāge homo
yāgāśca sviṣṭakṛdādayaḥ nopapadyante |

[p. b847]

aṣṭākapālaviṣayāścodanāḥ | ata upapadyate saṃśayaḥ |
asmiṃśca saṃśaye kasya pakṣasyādhyavasānaṃ yuktaṃ ? pūrvapakṣavādī
tāvanmanyate---apratipakṣatvādāgreyacodanāyāḥ tatsavyapekṣatvāccotta-
rāsāṃ pūrvaiva balīyasīti | tadidamuktam | “sarvapradānaṃ haviṣasta-
darthatvāt”
| kathaṃ tarhi caturavattaṃ juhotīti sviṣṭakṛte sama-
vadyatīti | gauṇo vā yajyartho juhotyarthaśca grahītavyaḥ, taddharmakaṃ vā
dravyāntaramutpādyam | dvirhaviṣo’vadyati havirdharmakādavadyatītyarthaḥ |

kṛtatvāttu karmaṇaḥ sakṛtsyāt dravyasya guṇabhūtatvāt|| 3-4-40||
atrābhidhīyate---satyamevaṃ yathā bhavānāha| yadi
āgneyo’ṣṭākapāla iti tyāgaparyantastaddhitaḥ syāt|

[p. b848]

sāsya devateti saṃbandhimātratayā’vagateḥ ekadeśatyāgādapyupapadyata
eva saṃbandhitā | tadidamuktam---“akṛtsnasaṃbandhepi taddhitasaṃbandha-
syopapattiḥ darśitastato grahītavyamiti saṃbandhaḥ"
| sati caivamāgneya-
codanāyāmupapannāyāṃ na caturavattaṃ juhoti sviṣṭakṛte samavadyati iti
gauṇī vṛttiḥ śakyate kalpayitum | havirdharmakasya vā dravya-
syotpādaḥ | tathā caturavattenaiva kṛtaḥ śāstrārthaḥ āgneyo’ṣṭāka-
pāla iti sviṣṭakṛdādidarśanaṃ copapannam | yatpunaridamāśaṅkitaṃ
dvayavadānasya saṃskāratvānna sarvaparityāge virodha iti tadidamāśaṅkā-
mātrameva haviṣo yāgārthatvādityuktam |

[p. b849]

|| 15 || aprayojakatvādekasmātkriṃyeran śeṣasya guṇabhūtatvāt || 42 ||
iha śeṣakarmāṇyudāharaṇaṃ---kiṃ sakṛt kṛtvoparantavyam uta
yāvaccheṣamāvṛttiḥ iti | pūrvapakṣavādī kṛtatvācchāstrārthasya sakṛ-
tkaraṇaṃ manyate | nanu pratipradhānaṃ guṇā āvartanta iti śeṣasyā-
prādhānyādāvṛttikevātrāvagamyate | ayamabhiprāyaḥ pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ
---pradhānopakārāya hi śeṣasya saṃskāraḥ, sakṛt kṛtena ca kṛtaṃ kāryamiti
nāvṛttiṃ manyate | na hi śeṣatvāt saṃskāryatvam | nanu ca
grahādāvapyevameva prāpnoti | prāpnotyeva yadyatra na samādhīyate | tatra
tarhi kā cintā ? ekatvasyāvivakṣetyuktam | atrābhidhīyate
---yāvaccheṣamāvṛttiḥ | nanu ca kṛtatvācchāstrārthasya sakṛt ityuktam

[p. b850]

ucyate | śeṣasaṃskāradvāraṃ hi pradhānasyopakāryatvamuktam | na ca
śeṣe viśeṣo’vagamyate | ayamayaṃ ayaṃ veti | ato’guhyamāṇa-
viśeṣāt, pratipradhānamāvṛttiḥ liṅgāni darśitāni ca bhāṣye |
yatpunaraprayojakatvāt śeṣāṇāmiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ tadevedaṃ na śeṣatvāt
saṃskāryatvamiti yaduktaṃ, na punarguṇabhāvaṃ śeṣasya manyate | na hi
śeṣasya prayojakatvamaprayojakatvaṃ copapadyate | tasmāt sa eva pūrvaḥ
pakṣaḥ |

|| 16 || ekasmāccedyārthākāmyamaviśeṣāt || 46||
pūrvādhikaraṇapūrvapakṣe anuṣṭhānacinteyam | atrāviśeṣādyataḥ
kutaściṃditi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. b851]

mukhya eva nimitte saṃbhavati na naimittikopaghātaḥ śakyate
kartumiti rāddhāntaḥ | jaghanyānāṃ tu kṛtatvāt naimittikasya
(nimittāvagatiḥ) yuktaivākriyā śeṣakāryāṇām | iti |

|| 17 || bhakṣāśravaṇāddānaśabdaḥ parikraye || 48 ||
idaṃ brahmaṇa iti dvyavadānopasaṃhārādyāgasya śeṣeṇa parikrayaṃ
manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | rāddhāntavādī tu tasyāvadeyayogyatayaivotpatteḥ
yāgārthataiveti nārthāntare viniyogamarhatīti | kathaṃ tarhi
sviṣṭakṛdādiyāgaḥ ? atyaktatvāccheṣasya vacanāccopapadyate |

[p. b852]

nanu ca dānamapi vacanādbhaviṣyati | satyam---yadi vacanamasti |
ta tvatra dānaṃ śrūyate tādarthyaśrutistūpakārādapyupapadyata evetyupa-
kāro darśitaḥ | śeṣabhāvastu viparīta evetyanavada्yam |

iti bṛhatyāṃ tṛtīyasyādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b853]

tṛtīyādhyāyasya pañcamaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || ājyācca sarvasaṃyogāta1 || 3-5-1 ||
yāvaddhaviḥ kiñciccoditaṃ sarvasmāccheṣādavadyatīti sthitam |
upāṃśuyāje saṃśayaḥ---kiṃ dhruvāsthādaviśeṣādaṅgapradhānasādhāraṇādana-
vadeyaṃ sviṣṭakṛte utāṅgapradhānaśeṣamapi pradhānaśeṣamityavadeyaṃ gṛhītā-
dvā jauhavāt camasavat | aviśeṣādavadeyamiti prāpte | na sādhāraṇe,
śeṣatvābhāvāt | śiṣṭamapi hi tatsādhāraṇameva | pradhānaśeṣācca
sviṣṭakṛdavadānam | na ca tatkevalapradhānaśeṣa iti śakyate vaktuṃ

[p. b854]

sādhāraṇatvādeva | yattu gṛhītasyaiva pratipattiścamasavaditi
tadayuktaṃ, caturavattaṃ juhotīti śravaṇāt śeṣayitavye pramāṇābhāvāt |
sviṣṭakṛdavadānavacanameva pramāṇamiti cenna | aprayukte śeṣe’rtha-
vatvāt | yattu camasavadityuktaṃ, tanna | anuvaṣaṭkāraśruterananya-
viṣayatvāt (na sviṣṭakṛdavadānam | na ca tatpradhānaśeṣa iti
śakyateṃ vaktum) camasasya grahaṇamātraśruteḥ | darśanaṃ pratyuktam |

|| 2 || sākaṃ prasthāyye sviṣṭakṛdiḍaṃ ca tadvat1 || 13 ||
kena viśeṣeṇāyamatideśaḥ ? prakṛtivaditi
prāpteḥ kāryārthatayā śeṣaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī |

[p. b855]

vacanāt kāryaṃ balavaditi rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 3 || sautrāmaṇyāṃ ca graheṣu1 || 14 ||
sautrāmaṇyāṃ ‘ucchinaṣṭi na sarvahutaṃ juhoti’
 iti punarapi
śeṣāśaṅkāyāṃ sviṣṭakṛdi़ḍaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | tasyānyatra
viniyogāditi rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 4 || 2dravyaikatve karmabhedāt pratikarma kriyeran || 16 ||
sarvapṛṣṭhe tu pratipradhānaṃ śeṣasaṃskārādāvṛttiṃ manyate |

[p. b856]

agṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatayā kṛtaḥ śāstrārtha iti rāddhāntaḥ | nanu
sādhāraṇe naiva sviṣṭakṛdastītyuktam | satyaṃ, akṛtatvātkāryāntarāṇāṃ
guṇaprādhānyācceti viśeṣaḥ |

|| 35 || aindravāyave tu vacanāt pratikarma bhakṣaḥ syāt1 || 18 ||
nanu cātra pūrvapakṣa eva nāsti vacanāt |
na hi pūrvapakṣe vacanasyānyārthatā śakyate vaktum |
atocyate | ayamabhiprāyaḥ pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ somasaṃskārārtha-
tvādbhakṣasya dviriti prayogāntarāpekṣo’nuvādo bhaviṣyati |

[p. b857]

rāddhāntavādī tu vacanātkāryakalpaneti vacanādityuktavān |

|| 6 || 1some’vacanādbhakṣo na vidyate || 19 ||
some śeṣabhakṣo’sti nāstīti saṃśayaḥ | nanu saṃśaya
evāyamayuktaḥ somasya śeṣabhāvāt | yuktaṃ puroḍāśādīnāṃ
dvirhaviṣo’vadyati iti vacanāt tatra yāgopasaṃhārāt śeṣavattvam
tasya ca bhakṣaḥ | iha tu tadabhāvāt bhakṣaśaṅkaiva nāsti | kathaṃ
tarhi vacanānyetāni ‘ dviraindravāyavasya bhakṣayati’
 ‘sarvataḥ parihāramā-
śvinaṃ’
 ‘anuvaṣaṭa्karoti’
 iti ca | atrāpi ca bhavāneva praṣṭavyaḥ atha
vā bhavantvarthakarmāṇi | kathaṃ tarhi ‘aindravāyavasya’
 ‘āśvinaṃ iti ca |

[p. b858]

taddharmalakṣaṇārtho bhaviṣyati | tasmādanupapannamevedaṃ somebhakṣo-
vidyate uta neti | athaitebhyo’pi vacanebhyaḥ śeṣayitavyaṃ
iti kalpyate | na, aprayojakatvāccheṣāṇām | saṃskāryapradhānaṃ hi
saṃskārakarma bhavati | na cāpradhānaṃ prayuṅkte | tasmāt vaktavyo’tra
nyāyaḥ | ucyateḥ---nātra sarvahome pramāṇamasti | aindravāyavādiṣu
hi tādarthyena grahaṇam | gṛhītasyaikadeśenāpi kṛte kṛtaivaindravāyavatā,
kimiti sarvasya homaḥ | nanu ca sarvasya yāgārthatā śrūyate ‘somena
yajeta’
 iti | tatrāpi ekedeśainaiva somenetyupapannatvāt | tathā

[p. b859]

ca asya grahaṇaṃ ṛjīṣādeśca tyāgaḥ | yadyevaṃ kimiti ‘dvirhaviṣo-
’vadyati’
 ityetasmādevopasaṃhāraḥ | yathakathaṃcit tatrāpyupasaṃhāra-
siddheḥ | niyamārthaṃ taditi cet | sarvapradānaṃ tarhi pūrvapakṣavādino na
ghaṭate kiñca ekadeśenāpyupapadyata iti naitāvatā sarvaṃ na hotavyam |
sarvahomenāpyupapadyata eva | tasmādvaktavyo viśeṣaḥ |

ayamabhidhīyate---kṛte śāstrārthe punaḥ karaṇaṃ tāvanna vidyate | eka-
deśena kṛtaḥ śāstrārtha iti pradarśitam | kathaṃ tarhi sarvapradānāśaṅkā ?

[p. b860]

avakhaṇḍyāvakhaṇḍyadātavyamiti vacanārthavagamāt | na yāgārtha-
tvāt dravyasyetyupasaṃhāraḥ | ataḥ ekadeśena niyama evāyam |
ata eva laukikāḥ pāyasādi devatoddeśena kṛtvā tata uda्dhṛtya
kiñcit devatoddeśena tyaktvā śeṣaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ viniyuñjate | tasmā-
dupapadyate saṃśayaḥ---kiṃ bhakṣaṇenaiva śeṣapratipattiḥ uta neti | vacanā-
bhāvāt na bhakṣaṇeneti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | bhakṣaṇaviśeṣavidhānāt astīti
dvitīyaḥ | anyathā hi viśeṣavidhānaṃ nopapadyate | yadi viśeṣavidhānaṃ
nopapadyate kāmaṃ mopapādi, na punarvacanādṛte bhakṣaḥ śakyate pratipattum
yadā bhakṣayati tadā dviriti evamevopapatteḥ, evaṃ tarhi tānyeva
vacanānīti rāddhāntaḥ yāvadvacanaṃ vacanam |

[p. b861]

||7|| 1camaseṣu samākhyānāt saṃyogasya tannimittatvāt || 22 ||
camaseṣu idānīṃ vacanābhāvānnāstīti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | taccamasa-
taivānyathā nopapadyate | ayamasya camasa iti vacanaṃ yathaiva viśeṣa-
vidhānamanyathā nopapadyata iti pūrvatra | dravyāntaracamanaṃ takṣaṇādi
ca na kalpanāmarhati iti pradarśitaḥ |

|| 8 || udgāṃtṛcamasamekasya śrutisaṃyogāt2 || 8 ||

[p. b862]

prodgāṃtṝṇāmityatra kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ na gānaṃ lakṣayati samā-
khyaiva kimiti lakṣayati ? ucchabdasyāvivakṣā prāpnoti gānakāriṣu
lakṣyamāṇeṣu | tathā ca darśitam---anyadeva gānaṃ anyadevodgānamiti |
samākhyāne tu nāyaṃ doṣaḥ | nanu codgānaṃ nāma gānameva nāsti

[p. b863]

bhaktirhi sā | sā codgīthavācyā | yaḥ punarudgāyedityeva-
mādiṣu prayogaḥ udgīthaṃ gāyediti tatrārthaḥ | ucchabdo hi
tatra bhaktilakṣaṇārthaḥ | gānaṃ tu gānameva | tasmādudgātetyṛ-
tvigviśeṣanāmaivaitat | na punaḥ udgānasya kartā udgātā | ata
eva samākhyaivātra lakṣayituṃ prabhavati na gānakriyeti rāddhāntitam |
ata eva apasubrahmaṇyavādino’pi apanyāyavādina eva | nātra

[p. b864]

stutyādiśravaṇamiva saṅkocakapramāṇamasti | nanu prodgātṝṇāmiti
triṣveva bahuvacanopapatteḥ kimiti samubrahmaṇyānāṃ bhakṣa iti ?
yadi bahuvacanopapattyarthaṃ satyameva | atrodgātṛśabdaḥ tadarthakāriṣu
vartata ityuktam |

|| 9 || grāvastuto bhakṣo na vidyate’nāmnānāt1 || 27 ||
uktaṃ vacanāt samākhyānācca bhakṣaṇam | idānīṃ vacanārtha-
vipratipattau sampratipattyarthametānyadhikaraṇānyārabhyante | sarve hāri-
yojanasyānte lipsinta iti camasināmeveti pūrvapakṣa-

[p. b865]

vādinā śaṅkitam | vākyaśeṣāt | asandigdhatvāt prakṛta-
vacanatayā sarvaśabdasya nopaṃsahāro yukto vākyaśeṣāditi rāddhāntaḥ
pakṣaḥ |

|| 10 || vaṣa्ṭakārācca bhakṣayet1 || 31 ||
atra vaṣaṭa्kartuḥ prathamabhakṣa iti guṇaśravaṇāt na bhakṣa-
vidhānamiti āśaṅkya viśiṣṭavidhānāditi rāddhāntaḥ | nanu
cātra viśiṣṭavidhānaṃ nāsti | na hi prāthamyaṃ nāma bhakṣaṇasya
kārakaṃ yena yāgaviśiṣṭā kriyā vidhīyeta |

[p. b866]

ata eva pūrvapakṣavādī na viśiṣṭavidhānamatra saṃbhavatītyāśaṅkatavān |
kathaṃ tarhi rāddhāntaḥ? prathamabhakṣa iti padadvayasya caikārthyābhidhā-
nāt abhihitavidhānāccaikārthyaṃ manyate | tadayuktam |
samāsepi hi tada्bhūtādhikaraṇanyāyāt apagatirnāsti | satya-
mevaṃ, tathāpi pūrvottarapadasaṃbandhāyattā samāse ca padārthāntarasaṅgatiḥ

[p. b867]

iti yuktamuktaṃ samāse ca vidadhato nānekaguṇavidhānaṃ duṣkara-
mityataḥ siddhamatna viśiṣṭavidhānam|

|| 11 || hāmābhiṣavābhyāṃ ca1|| 32 ||
abhiṣutya hutvā bhakṣayati ityapi bhakṣavidhānam| homābhiṣavayoḥ
prāptatvāt|

[p. b868]

aṅgatayā ca vidhānānupapatterbhakṣa eva vidhātavyaḥ |

|| 12 || pratyakṣopadeśāccamasānāmavyaktaśśeṣe1 || 33 ||
hotṛcamasa ityevamādyābhiḥ śrutibhiḥ samākhyābhiḥ sakala-
camasasaṃbandhāvagateḥ abhiṣutya hutvetyanyatra camasebhyo bhakṣa iti
manyate | evaṃ prāpte ucyate | abhiṣavahomanimitto bhakṣaścamaseṣu
bhavatyeva | nanu ca hotṛcamasa iti sakalacamasāvagatirityuktam |
satyamuktaṃ na punarabhiṣavanimittatāṃ bādhitumarhati | prāpakāṇyetāni
vacanāni na hotṛcamasa ityasyāṃ saṃbandhāvagatau saṃbandhāntaranivṛttiḥ
śakyate’vagantum | prāptimātrāvagateḥ | prāptau hi satyāṃ kasye-
damiti vivakṣāyāṃ anyanivṛttiḥ pratīyate | yathā āgneya ityukte

[p. b869]

naindrāgnasyāvagatiḥ | iha punaḥ prāptiphalatvādvākyasya nānyanivṛttiḥ
śakyate’vagantum | nanu ca prāptāvapi sakalāvagatervirodho vidyata
eva | satyaṃ yadi samākhyayā prāptiḥ iha punaḥ praituhotuścamasaḥ iti
vacanāt | kalpyaṃ caitat abhiṣutya hutvetyanena |

|| 13 || ekapātre kramādadhvaryuḥ pūrvo bhakṣayet1 || 36 ||
sthitaṃ camasino’bhiṣotāro hotāraśca bhakṣayanti iti |
atredānīṃ kaḥ prathamaṃ bhakṣayediti vicāryate | adhvaryuḥ
bhakṣayati hastagatatvāt yasya haste sa jayatīti nyāyāt |

[p. b870]

nanu vaṣaṭa्kartuḥ prathamabhakṣa iti | vacanāt kathamadhvaryorbhakṣaḥ ? bhakṣa-
mātravidhānādapi vaṣaṭa्kartureva prathamaṃ bhakṣa iti manvānasya
punaridamadhikaraṇam | pradarśitaṃ ca prāthamyādvaṣaṭa्kārasyeti | vaca-
nāccetyupasaṃhṛtam |

|| 14 || vacanādanujñātabhakṣaṇam1 || 40 ||
kiṃ anujñātetaiva bhakṣayitavyaṃ uta ananujñāteneti prāptidarśa-
nārthamidamadhikaraṇam | asati vacane’niyamaḥ | vacanāttu niyama

[p. b871]

eva | niyame sati kiṃ laukikena śabdenānujñāpanaṃ uta vaidikeneti |
mantrānāmnānāt vaidikeneti niyamaḥ | idamapi mantraprāpti-
darśanārthameva |

|| 15 || tatrārthāt prativacanam1 || 42 ||
idānīṃ prativacane saṃśayaḥ kiṃ mantreṇa prativacanaṃ uta lauki-
kena śabdeneti | kathaṃ punarupahūta iti mantrāmnānāt sandehaḥ
upahrayasvetyasyaiva śeṣaṃ manvānasya | na hyanujñāpanāt pūrvapaṭhitaṃ
prativacanasamarthamapi prativacanāyālaṃ bhavatītyabhiprāyaḥ | rāddhānta-
vādī tu pūrvataivātra bādhyata iti rāddhāntitavān |

[p. b872]

|| 16 || tadekapātrāṇāṃ samavāyāt1 || 43 ||
paṭhite jñāpane prativacane ca kimekapātrāṇāmevānujñāpanaṃ utā-
viśeṣeṇeti | avidhānādaviśeṣaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | dṛṣṭārtha-
tayā tu rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 17 ||yājyāpanayanenāpanīto bhakṣaḥ pravaravat2 || 44 ||
yājyāmātrāpanayanaṃ manvānasya pūrvapakṣavādino hotureva bhakṣa
ityabhiprāyaḥ | kimanavānaśrutiḥ niṣadya yajatītivat vacanaṃ na
paśyati | na na paśyati, yājyāmātre | evametada्dvayamapyupapadyata

[p. b873]

ityabhiprāyaḥ | yājyābhidhānena yajeḥ kartṛtvamupapadyata ityarthaḥ |
rāddhāntavādī tu manyate | satyamupapadyate na punarvaṣaṭa्kārāpanaye kiñcana
pramāṇamasti iti savaṣaṭa्kārameva yājyāprayogamātrepyupapadyata iti
vyāpāramaṅgīkṛtya kartṛtvaṃ yuktamiti | yattu pravaro nāpanīyata iti
tadatraupadeśikatvāt yathā pravaro nāpanīyate tathā pradarśitam |

|| 18 || phalacamaso naimittiko bhakṣavikāraḥ śrutisaṃyogāt || 47 ||
kiṃ phalacamaso bhakṣavikāra utejyāvikāra iti | asmin
saṃśaye brūmaḥ naivātra saṃśayaḥ tamasmaibhakṣaṃ prayacchet iti bhakṣasaṃba-

[p. b874]

ndhitvena vidhānāt | bhakṣe somaṃ vikāroti na yāge | na ca
yāgavikāraḥ śakyate vaktum | utpattiśiṣṭatvāt somasya |
vidhīyamāne tu karmāntarāpattiḥ | tataśca bhakṣavikāro’pi na syāt
dūre ijyāvikāratā | atrābhidhīyate---nejyāvikāramantareṇa bhakṣaṃ
vikartuṃ samarthaḥ phalacamasaḥ | ijyāśeṣasya hi somasya bhakṣaḥ | na
cāśeṣabhūte bhakṣyamāṇe sa vikṛto bhavati | tasmādijyāśeṣataiva phala-
camasasyāpi mantavyā | nanu cotpattiśiṣṭatvāt somasya yāge vidhānaṃ
na saṃbhavatītyuktam | yāgānuvādena vidhāne hi apratyabhijñāna-

[p. b875]

muktam | na punaḥ kāryeṇāpi saṃbandhe | kāryata evāyaṃ saṃbandhaḥ |
nanu ca bhāṣyakāreṇa yāgasaṃbandhābhāvaṃ codayitvā yāja-
yediti saṃbandha uktaḥ yajinā | yāgānvayapradarśanārthaṃ taduktam
na punaranū्dya vidhānāyetyadoṣaḥ | bhakṣaṃ kuryāditi copasaṃhāraḥ
imamevārthaṃ prakaṭīkaroti | tasmādijyāvikāraḥ phalacamasa iti
siddham | guṇavidhānāni ca pradarśitāni |

|| 20 || anuprasarpiṣu sāmānyāt1 || 52 ||
daśadaśa ekaikaṃ camasamanuprasarpantīti vidhānāt śataṃ brāhmaṇāḥ
somān bhakṣayantītyanuvādaṃ manvānasya nābhakṣo rājeti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. b876]

satyamevaṃ yadyanuvādaḥ syāt | na cānuvṛtte rājani anuvādaḥ
śakyate vaktum | ayathārthānuvādaprasaṅgāt | nanu cānuvāde
ayathārthatvaṃ adoṣaḥ bhūyastvena lakṣaṇā bhaviṣyati | satyaṃ yadye-
kāntato’nuvādaḥ iha punarvidheyatayāpyupapadyata eveti nāyathārthatvaṃ
śakyate | tasmāt dvayamapyetadvidhīyate śataṃ brāhmaṇāḥ daśa ekaikaṃ
camasaṃ iti ca | nanu ca ‘samaṃ syādaśrutatvāt’
 iti nyāyena śate
vibhajyamāne ekaikatra daśadaśaprāpteḥ daśa daśa ekaikaṃ camasamanu-
prasarpanti ityanuvādaḥ |

[p. b877]

na ekaikatra śataprāpteḥ pratyuddeśaṃ vākyasamāpteḥ | tasmāda-
bhakṣo rājā brāhmaṇavidhānāditi | yatpunaratra śataṃ brāhmaṇāssomān
ityanekārthavidhānaṃ manyate, tadayuktaṃ, yasmāt karmāntaravidhānenāpi
prākṛtakāryārthatāṃ daśame vakṣyāmaḥ |

iti bṛhatyāṃ tṛtīyasya
pañcamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b878]

bṛhatyāṃ

tṛtīyādhyāyasya ṣaṣṭhaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || sarvārthamaprakaraṇāt1 || 1 ||
kiṃ punaratra kāraṇaṃ pakṣadvayopanyāsasya | kasya pakṣadvayopanyā-
sasya ? | codakādanārabhyavidhiḥ balīyān sāmānyatodṛṣṭatvācceti |
prathamasya tāvatpakṣasya idaṃ prayojanaṃ naivātra dviruktatā vidyate |
upadeśātideśābhyāṃ prakṛtivikṛtyoḥ sruksaṃbhavān | evaṃ tarhi
sāmānyato darśanena prakṛtyarthateti | aparasya pakṣasyopanyāsaḥ prāya-

[p. b879]

darśanavat | ayaṃ tāvadanantaraḥ pakṣo nirākriyate | na sāmā-
nyato dṛṣṭaṃ śabdārthāvagatau hetuḥ | nāpi prāyadarśanam |
prakṛtāviva vikṛtāvapi saṃkṣepavistāropadeśadarśanāt | idānī-
maparasya pakṣasya nirasanaṃ codakāpekṣaścet anārabhya vidhiḥ |
prakṛtau pūrvamupadeśo’pekṣaṇīyaḥ upadeśāpekṣatvādatideśasya |
upadeśena cedupasaṃhṛto’nārabhyavidhiḥ nātideśamapekṣate |

[p. b880]

kāryata eva vikṛtau prāptatvāt | ato dviruktatā’saṃbhavāt advirukta-
tvāditi sthito hetuḥ | yastu yāgasaṃbandhopanyāsaḥ1 asāvavaśyaṃ
bhāvī codaka iti pradarśayitum | avaśyaṃbhāvitve cādviruktatvasya
siddhiruktā |

|| 2 || prakaraṇaviśeṣāttu vikṛtau virodhi syāt2 || 1 ||
nanu cānārabhyādhītaṃ sāptadaśyaṃ, yacca prakaraṇādhītaṃ
pāñcadaśyaṃ ubhayamapi śrutyaiva sāmidhenīsaṃbandhitayā

[p. b881]

codyate | ko’ya prakaraṇopanyāsaḥ | naivātra(yotra) prakaraṇaṃ vini-
yojakaṃ yena prakaraṇavidhirbalīyān bhavati | athāyamabhiprāyaḥ
prakaraṇasavyapekṣā eva śrutyādayo viniyojakāḥ iti | satyaṃ grāha-
kalakṣaṇaprakaraṇasavyapekṣāḥ na tu viniyojakamapekṣante | grāhakasa-
vyapekṣā cānārabhyavidherapi tulyaiva | ato’trāpi dvirukterasaṃbhavāt
prakṛtāveveti yuktam |

astyatra viśeṣaḥ | prakṛtatve hi pāñcadaśyasya grāhakapratyabhi-
jñānaṃ saṃbhavati | avyavāyāt | anārabhyavidheḥ punaḥ kāryato
grāhakāvamarśaḥ |

[p. b882]

evaṃ ca sati na klṛpte kalpanā saṃbhavati iti na prakṛtau
niveśaḥ | pratipattyavirodhinī hi kalpanā na pratipannopamardinī |
tasmādvikṛtāvanārabhya vidhānamiti sūktam |

|| 3 || naimittikaṃ tu prakṛtau tadvikāraḥ saṃyāgaviśeṣāta1 || 10 ||
kathaṃ punaratra pūrvapakṣāśaṅkā | itthamāśaṅkā kratvarthe bādhyamāne upa-
deśaḥ pīḍito bhavati | nacopadeśāśādṛte prakṛtau sāda्guṇyāvagatirbhavati |

[p. b883]

vikṛtau punaḥ kāryata eva sāda्guṇyāvagatiḥ ityekakārya-
tvāt puruṣārthenāpi kratoḥ sāda्guṇyaṃ śakyate vaktuṃ kārya-
viśeṣāt | tasmāt puruṣārthānāṃ vikṛtau niveśaḥ | satyamevaṃ
yadi prakṛtāvapi kāryaviśeṣo na syāt | iha tu dṛṣṭārthatvādupadeśasya
tulyaḥ kāryaviśeṣaḥ | (kārya)viśeṣāvagamācca sāmānyato balīyastvaṃ
viśeṣopadeśasyeti prakṛtāveva niveśaḥ |

|| 4 || iṣṭyarthamagnyādheyaṃ prakaraṇāt1 || 11 ||
kathaṃ punaratreṣṭeḥ prakaraṇaṃ ? iṣṭerapi naivādhikāraśrutirasti |

[p. b884]

ādhānasyaiva hi sādhikārasya vidheḥ prakaraṇam | tasmānneṣṭeḥ prakaraṇaṃ
iti śakyate vaktum | kathaṃ tarhi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ ? bhavato vā
ko’bhiprāyaḥ | na kiñcitpratibhāti | atrābhidhīyate---dvayamapya-
trodāharaṇam | kimādhānaṃ uteṣṭaya iti | dvayoḥ prādhānyamayukta-
miti manvānasya iṣṭeścāṅgabāhulyāt prādhānyāropeṇa pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
rāddhāntastu dvayorapyadhikārābhāvāt | agnyarthatayā ca
viniyogopapatteḥ dvayoragnyarthatvam | tathā ca liṅgadarśanam |

[p. b885]

|| 5 || tatprakṛtyarthaṃ yathā’nye’nārabhyavādāḥ || 14 ||
kimidānīṃ prakṛtyarthamādhānaṃ uta sarvārthamiti | etaduktaṃ
bhavati---kiṃ kratvarthanāmagnīnāmādhānaṃ uta āhitānāṃ kratvarthateti |
kaścātra viśeṣaḥ ? ayamasti viśeṣaḥ---yadi kratvarthānāmādhānaṃ tataḥ
parṇamayītvavat prakṛtigāmitvamādhānavidheḥ | atha tu āhitānāṃ
kratvarthatā tadā homādimātraśeṣatayā’gnīnāṃ sarvārthamādhāna-
mityucyate | kiṃ punaratra yuktam ? parṇatādivat kratusaṃbandhānāmevāgrī-
nāmādhānamiti | āhitāgneradhikārāt āhitānāṃ kratvarthateti |

[p. b886]

yathā vā āhitāgnerādhikārastathā ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | vasantādikāla-
vidhiścaivamupapanno bhaviṣyati |

|| 6 || tāsāmagniḥ prakṛtitaḥ prayājavat1 || 16 ||
kathaṃ punaḥ pavamāneṣṭiṣu pavamāneṣṭisaṃskṛtā agnayaḥ āśaṅkya-
nte | yā api hi tāḥ agnyarthā iṣṭayaḥ tā api
pavamāneṣṭaya eva | tasmādanāśaṅkanīyamevedamāśaṅkitam | atrocyate
---idamevātra cintyate | kimayamatideśaḥ prakṛtau yāva-
dupakārakaṃ tāvadatidiśati uta yathākāryamatideśa iti |

[p. b887]

tatra pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ | yāvadupakārakaṃ tāvadati-
diśatīti | rāddhāntavādī punaḥ yathākāryamati-
deśaḥ | upadeśetu viparītam | tasmānnaitadupadeśavad
draṣṭavyam | upadeśaviśeṣapradarśanārthaṃ caitadupadeśalakṣaṇe cintitam |

[p. b888]

anyathā hi anvārambhaṇīyādhikaraṇenaiva gatam syāt | agnyartha-
tayā ca pavamāneṣṭīnāṃ upadeśānyatve yatnaḥ iti viśeṣaḥ |

|| 7 || tulyaḥ sarveṣāṃ paśuvidhiḥ prakaraṇāviśeṣāt1 || 18 ||
kathaṃ punaridamadhikaraṇaṃ prārabdham | yathaiva bhāṣyakāreṇa | na tathā
śakyate vaktum | tasmādyadi tāvadidaṃ jyotiṣṭomākhyaprakaraṇaṃ tadā

[p. b889]

śrutyādībhireva paśuyāgasaṃbandhāt viśasanādīnām | kimidaṃ
prakaraṇaviśeṣāditi sarvārthatocyate | atha prakaraṇe aviśeṣaḥ
paśūnāmityaviśeṣatocyate kathaṃ tarhi sūtraṃ---asti tu prakaraṇe viśeṣa
iti | nacāvāntaraprakaraṇaṃ nāma kiñcidasti jyautiṣṭomikamahāprakaraṇe |
prakaraṇāntare cābhyupagamyamāne(’rthe) na tāvatprakaraṇamagrīṣomīyā-
derupapadyate | kimityeva hi tadā jyotiṣṭomasaṃbandho’bhyupagataḥ
syāt | evaṃ ca dīkṣaṇīyāderitikartavyatābhāva eva hīyeta |

[p. b890]

[p. b891]

darśapūrṇamāsayośca prayājāderitikartavyatāprasaṅgaḥ prakaraṇaviniyo-
jyatayā |

[p. b892]

tataśca ‘avyaktāsu tu somasye’
ti somikī itikartavyatā
prāpnoti dīkṣaṇīyāderiva dārśapūrṇamāsikī | kimiti vā na bhavati ?

[p. b893]

uktamavāntaraprakaraṇābhāvāditi | na hyekasmin prayogavacane
nānākimityāṅkṣā saṃbhavati | ekaścāyaṃ prayogavacanaḥ setikarta-
vyatākasya prayogasyaikaśabdayāditi vakṣyati |

kathaṃ tarhi dīkṣaṇīyāderātideśikā dharmāḥ ? mā bhūvan,
na punaḥ kimityākāṅkṣādvayamekasmin prakaraṇe saṃbhavati |

[p. b894]

evaṃ sthite yadidamucyate prakaraṇātsavanīyasyeti sthānāccāgnīṣo-
mīyasyeti sarvamidamandhapadam | tasmāt mahatā prayatnenātra bhāṣyaṃ
vyākhyeyam | atrābhidhīyate---prakaraṇaṃ tāvadidaṃ jyotiṣṭomasya |
aupavasathye’hani dharmāścodyante | na ca paśavastasminnahani sarve
sannihitāḥ sannihitavacanaṃ coddeśakaṃ padam | ataḥ kasyāyamuddeśaka
iti cintyate viśasanādividhiṣu | tatra prakaraṇāviśeṣāt sarve
paśavo jyotiṣṭomārthatayā prakṛtā iti sarvārthatāṃ manyate |

apareṇa pūrvapakṣiṇā ahassāmānyasannidhānāttu (sannikṛṣṭa iti)
sannidhiviśeṣo vidyata iti asti tu prakaraṇaviśeṣa ityuktam |

[p. b895]

prakṛtalakṣaṇaṃ ca prakaraṇamatrābhipretam | ataḥ sthānādagnīṣomīyasyeti
yuktam | (siddham) | aprakṛtasiddhayarthaṃ ca sautye’hani vidhānaṃ
savanīyasyetyevamāda्yupapannam | nanu sannidhānaviśeṣeṇāgnīṣomī-
yārthatve siddhe jyotiṣṭomaprakaraṇe cāmnānasyārthavatve tasminneva
prakaraṇe paśudvayasyātideśaḥ | na hyekaḥ prayogavacanaḥ upadeśena
atideśena ca tāneva dharmān gṛhṇāti | asya codyasya parihāraṃ
atideśa eva vakṣyāmaḥ kṛtsnavidhānādapūrvaḥ soma ityatra | asmin

[p. b896]

punaradhikaraṇe upadeśaviśeṣo nirūpitaḥ | upadeśaviśeṣatvāccheṣa-
lakṣaṇasya | kaḥ punaḥ aupavasathye’hani savanīyeṣu vidhīyamāneṣu
āśvinakāle vākyabhedaḥ? nanvidaṃ pūrvapakṣī codayati | kathaṃ ca
sautyehani savanīyeṣu vidhīyamāneṣu vākyabhedaṃ rāddhāntavādī
codayati | kiñca ubhayatra vākyabheda eva coditaḥ | ucyate |
anekaguṇavidhānameva vākyabhedaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | rāddhānta-
vādyapi vapāpracāramātrotkarṣaṃ manvānaḥ punarālambhavidhāne ca vākya-
bhedameva coditavān | āgneyyaudāsīnyamuktavān |

[p. b897]

kiṃ punaranayorvākyabhedayordūṣaṇam | rāddhāntavāda्yuktameva
dūṣaṇam | aparihāryaṃ hi tatra vākyāntaratvam | pūrvapakṣavāda्yuktasya
tu viśiṣṭavidhānādapi siddhiḥ “saṃskāro’yaṃ paśuyāgaprayuktaḥ
na jyotiṣṭomaprayukta”
1 iti | kimidaṃ jyotiṣṭomaprayuktaṃ iti ?
nanu ca sarvamevedaṃ jyotiṣṭomaprayuktam | tatprakaraṇāmnānāt |
ucyate | viniyogābhipnāṃyo’yaṃ granthaḥ | na punaḥ prayojyābhiprāyaḥ |

[p. b898]

na prakaraṇaviniyojyatayā prayuktiḥ(ityarthaḥ) | kiṃ tarhi ? śrutyaiva
viniyoga | ‘prakaraṇavantaśca’
 iti | śaknoti hi svasthānepi
prayogavacanaḥ prayoktumiti darśayati | ata eva ca prakaraṇasthāne
api prakṛtābhiprāyeṇaivetyuktam | darśanācchidrāpidhāne pratyukte |

|| 8 || 1dohayoḥ kālabhedādasaṃyuktaṃ śrṛtaṃ syāt || 28 ||
pūrvavadatrāpi sannidhānaviśeṣamaṅgīkṛtya pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ|

[p. b899]

rāddhāntastu naiva dadhipayasoḥ kālabhedena vidhānamasti | yadi paraṃ
krame kālabhedaḥ sa ca prakaraṇe pratyarthini na viniyojaka
ityuktam |

|| 9 || 1tadvat savanāntare grahāmnānam || 30 ||
atrāpi pūrvavadeva sannidhānaviśeṣāśaṅkā pūrvapakṣavādinaḥ |
rāddhāntastu na dharmāṇāṃ savanaviśeṣe vidhānamiti sarvārthatā |

[p. b900]

|| 10 || 1raśanā ca liṅgadarśanāt || 31 ||
raśanāyāstvagnīṣomīyaprakaraṇa2 eva vidhānamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
rāddhāntastu satyaṃ tatprakrame vidhānaṃ yūpastveka iti sarvārthato raśanā-
yāstaddharmāṇāṃ ca | tathā ca liṅgamuktam |

|| 11 || 3ārācchiṣṭamasaṃyuktamitaraissannidhānāt || 32 ||
aṃśvadābhyayostu prakaraṇe avidhānāt uddeśakasya ca sannihita-
vacanatvāt na taddharma iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu na sanni-
hitamātravacanamuddeśakaṃ prayojanavatsannihitavacanaṃ hi tat |

[p. b901]

abhinnaṃ cātna prayojanam | prakṛtānāmaprakṛtānāṃ ca grahaṇām | tasmāt
prayojanābhedāta prayojanāvacchinnagrahajātīye grahaśabdo na prakṛtatvena
saṅakocamarhati | maitrāvaruṇe tu nirdeśāt yuktam | iha tu viparīto
nirdeśa ityapi darśitam |

|| 12 || agnyarthamaprakaraṇe tadvat || 35 ||
kaḥ punaranayoradhikaraṇayorviśeṣaḥ ? ayamasti viśeṣaḥ |
tatra hi ekadravyasaṃbandhitvādyukto viśeṣaḥ| iha punaḥ ekaikeṣṭakā-
vyāpārasaṃbandhitayāpi dharmavidhayo’rthavanto bhavanti | tadayuktam |

[p. b902]

tatrāpi hi ekaikagrahavyāpārabhedo vidyata eva | evaṃ tarhi citi-
bhedāt abhyadhikāśaṅkā | rāddhāntastu yadyapi citibhedaḥ tathāpi
citau dharmāṇāmapratiṣṭhitatvāt |

|| 13 || naimittikamatulyatvādasamānavidhānaṃ syāt || 36 ||
idamidānīṃ cintyate---kiṃ naimittikānāṃ dharmavidhayaḥ samānā
uta kāryavanto bhavantīti | tatra tulyatvāt prakaraṇasya samānā dharma-
vidhaya iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | satyaṃ tulyaṃ prakaraṇam dharmavantastu nityāḥ

[p. b903]

naimittikāśca godohanādayaḥ | taddvāraṃ ca prakaraṇasya grāhakatvamiti
sthitam | naimittikaṃ ca nityakāryāpannaṃ bhavati | ataḥ kāryataḥ
siddheḥ na punarupadeśamarhati iti | dviruktatādoṣaścoktaḥ |

|| 14 || pratinidhiśca tadvat || 37 ||
athedānīṃ pratinidhau kā vārtā | tatra kāryataḥ prāptiriti pūrva-
pakṣavādī manyate kāryeṇākṣipratvātpratinidheḥ | atrābhidhīyate---satyaṃ
kāryeṇākṣiptaḥ pratinidhiḥ upadeśasaṃpadā tulyaḥ, nānyasādhyatayā,

[p. b904]

tasmādaupadeśika eva dharmasaṃbandhaḥ na kāryasādhanatayā dravyāntaraṃ
nīvārāḥ |

|| 15 || niyamārthā guṇaśratiḥ || 40 ||
yatra punaḥ śrūyate pratinidhiḥ tatra śrutāśaṅkatayā pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
śrutopapattyartho’yaṃ niyama iti rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 16 || saṃsthāstu samānavidhānāḥ prakaraṇāviśeṣāt || 41 ||
nanu cedaṃ phalacamasena gatamevādhikaraṇam | na naimitti-
kasya dharmā bhavantyupadeśata iti | ucyate---astyatrādhikā---

[p. b905]

śaṅkā | kā punarasau ? iyamasau | ukthyādayo hi grahaṇa-
viśeṣāḥ | te ca saṃskārāḥ svayamitikartavyatābhūtāḥ na ceti-
kartavyatā itikartavyatayā saṃbadhyate | kiṃ tarhi ? yāgena |

[p. b906]

sarvagrahaṇasaṃskāryadravyakaścāyaṃ sa eva yāgaḥ somena
yajeteti | nānyotpattirvidyate | sa cedyāgaḥ grahaṇāntare-
ṣvapi itikartavyatāpi saiva | na ca grahaṇaviśeṣo
yāgasyetikartavyatāprāptau nimittaṃ; kiṃ tarhi prayoga-
codanaiva | na ca grahaṇāntareṣu prayogāntaracodanā yāgasya |
guṇānāmeva hi tatra phalāya codanā na yāgasya, yathā
godohanena paśukāmasya praṇayediti na darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ

[p. b907]

prayogaścodyate | tasmādbhavantu grahaṇāni grahaṇadharmopadeśaśūnyāni phala-
camasavat na punaryāgo’pi yāgadharmaśūnya iti śakyate vaktum | 
sarvasaṃskāreṣvekaprayogasya coditatvāt | prayogagrāhyatvācceti-
kartavyatāyā ityuktam | ata eva cāgniṣṭomagrahaṇaṃ samuccayaśca
pradarśitaḥ | paśūnāmadṛṣṭārthatayā samuccaya eva nyāyyaḥ | atrocyate
-savanīyasamākhyayā sarvapaśūnāṃ tulyakāryāvagamāt | atrābhidhīyate
---satyamevaṃ syādyadi saṃsthantāreṣu ukthyādiṣu prayogacodanayaiva
labhyate | yathā godohanena darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ | iha tu agniṣṭoma-

[p. b908]

paryantaiva yāgasya prayogacodanā | ata ūrdhvaṃ grahā ukthyādaya
eva codanāmākṣipanti yathā grahāḥ āvṛttim nimitte | evaṃ
cenna yāgacodanayā dharmāḥ śakyante grahītuṃ ukthyādiṣu | ataḥ
phalacamasavat nityānityasaṃyogavirodho yuktaḥ | kāryataśca dharmaprāptiḥ
tasmāt samuccayagrahaṇaṃ vacanāt bhaviṣyati | agniṣṭomaśabdaścānu-
vādaḥ | tathā homādīnāmabhāvadarśanamupapannaṃ bhavati |
 
iti bṛhatyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya
ṣaṣṭaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b909]

[p. b910]

tṛtīyādhyāyasya saptamaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || prakaraṇāviśeṣādasaṃyuktaṃ pradhānasya || 1 ||
barhirdharmāśca kimaṅgapradhānārthā uta pradhānārthā eveti sandehe
prakaraṇāt pradhānārthā ityuktvā upakārakatvenāṅgapradhānārthatāmāha |
tadayuktam | śrutyādimirhi tādarthyamavagamyate nopakāreṇa |

ucyate---śrutyādaya evopanyastā barhiṣi havīṃṣyāsādayatīti |
upakārakagrahaṇaṃ tarhi kimarthaṃ ? kadācit tadabhāvāt śruteḥ saṅkocaḥ
syāt | asti cāsāvekarūpa upakāro nāmeti tadabhidhānam |

[p. b911]

evaṃ tarhi vākyenaiva śeṣāvagatiḥ | kimiti tarhi barhirmātre(ṇa)
na bhavati ? grāhakatve sati śeṣatvamityuktam | grāhakaśca dārśapaurṇa-
māsikaprayogavidhiḥ sannidhānāt | liṅgadarśanamuktam |

|| 2 || phalasaṃyogātsvāmiyuktaṃ pradhānasya 2 || 2 ||
iha purūṣasaṃskārā udāharaṇam | te kiṃ karkṛbhūtasya utādhi-
kṛtasyeti | kartṛbhūtasya | evaṃ karmāṅgatopapadyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. b912]

kimityaṃśopanipātinī itikartavyateti saptamādye vakṣyāmaḥ |
ākāṅkṣādvayasya tatprasūteḥ | tatra yadarthaḥ kartṛbhāvaḥ saṃskāro’pi
tadartha eva yuktaḥ | adhikāraniṣpattyarthatvācchāstrasya | tasmādadhi-
kṛtasyasaṃskāra iti yuktam |

|| 3 ||cikīrṣayā ca saṃyogāt1 || 7 ||
barhirdharmādhikaraṇena kimatrānyatvaṃ yenedamādhikaraṇamārabhyate |

[p. b913]

pradhānacikīrṣāṃ dhurvapakṣavādī manyate | yadarthā cikīrṣā tadarthā-
nyaṅgānītyabhiprāyaḥ |

|| 4 || tathābhidhānena1 || 8 ||
abhimarśane tu sākṣātpradhānaśabdasaṃyogāt pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | satyaṃ,
pradhānaṃ cikīrṣyate | tattu sāṅgaṃ adhikāranirvṛttāviti rāddhāntaḥ |
evamabhimarśanamapi adhikārasādhanasyaiveti ubhayoraṅgapradhānārthatā |

[p. b914]

|| 6 || dīkṣādakṣiṇaṃ tu vacanātpradhānesya1 || 11 ||
dīkṣādakṣiṇamapi sāṅgādadhikāranirvṛtteḥ sāṅgasyetyāśaṅkya
vacanātpradhānasyeti rāddhāntaḥ | yuktaṃ tāvat vacanāt dīkṣā
prādhānasya | dakṣiṇā punaḥ yadā pradhānasyaiva kathamapi tadā
kathamaparikrītairaṅgāni kriyante | kena voktamaparikrītairaṅgāni kriyanta
iti prākṛtamānatikāraṇaṃ bhaviṣyatyeva | yadyevaṃ na tarhi prasaṅgaḥ |
yathā prasaṅgaḥ tathā prasaṅgalakṣaṇa eva vakṣyāmaḥ | darśanamuktam |
 
|| 6 || tathā yūpasya vediḥ2 || 13 ||
ardhamantarvedīti kiṃ vediḥ yūpāṅgamuta neti saṃśaye

[p. b915]

śruteraṅgamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | bahirvedipadaikavākyatvāt na śrutyartha-
vidhānaṃ saṃbhavatīti deśalakṣaṇā | ubhayavidhāne vākyabheda-
prasaṅgāt |

sāmidhenīstadanvāhuriti havirdhānayorvacanātsāmidhenīnām1 || 15 ||
kaḥ punaḥ pūrveṇāsyādhikaraṇasya viśeṣaḥ ? ayamasti viśeṣaḥ
padāntarāsaṃbaddhaṃ hyatra havirdhānaṃ śrūyate nāntarvedivat bahirvedipada-
saṃbaddham | tasmādaṅgāṅgibhāva iti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate śruteḥ

[p. b916]

vijñānaviparītaṃ hi virūddhamabhidhīyate | rāddhāntavādītu ubhayo---
ranyārthatvāt deśalakṣaṇasaṃbandha eva nyāyya iti manyate | tathā
ca ‘havirdhānaṃ’
 ‘sāmidhenya’
 iti ca samākhyāpi na pīḍitā syāt |
anyathāhi havirdhānaṃ sāmidhenīnāṃ sāmidhenyo vā havirdhānasyeti
devadattasyedaṃ yajñadattasyeti yathaitadasaṃbaddhaṃ tathedamapi syāt | tasmāda-
trāpi pūrvavat deśaviśeṣalakṣaṇaiveti yuktam | āgneyyadhikaraṇavacca
sāmānyaviśeṣasaṃbandhe vākyabhedaḥ syāt |

śāstraphalaṃ prayoktari tallakṣaṇatvāt tasmātsvayaṃ prayoge syāt1 || 18 ||
kathaṃ punaratra parikrayādiṣu śrūyamāṇeṣu saṃśayaḥ |

[p. b917]

ayamabhiprāyaḥ kartṛgāmikriyāphalamatra śrūyate | atastadvirodhāt
gauṇaṃ adṛṣṭārthaṃ vaha manyate | kathaṃ tarhi samākhyāḥ ādhvaryavamityeva-
mādyāḥ ? granthasaṃyogādupapannā bhaviṣyanti | tasmātsvayaṃ kartṛ-
taiva nyāyyeti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | aniyamavādī tu parīkrītairapi
kriyamāṇaṃ tenaiva kṛtaṃ bhavati | sa hi teṣāṃ prayoktā | prayoktuśca
kartṛtvasmaraṇam | na punarāhatyaiva kartṛtvam | utsargastu svadravya-
tyāgalakṣaṇatvāt svayameva kartavyaḥ | evaṃ kartṛtve upapanne na
parikrayasya gauṇatā, adṛṣṭārthatā vā kalpayituṃ śakyate | samā-

[p. b918]

khyāśca mukhyārthāḥ na granthadvārikāḥ kalpitā bhaviṣyanti | na hi
adhyetāro’dhvaryuśabdavācyāḥ adhyayanakāle | kathaṃ punaraniyamaṃ
manyate | ayamabhiprāyaḥ yadā svayamanutsāhī tadā parikrayaḥ yadāsau
parikrayaḥ tadā anyaireveti manyate | rāddhāntavādī tu manyate
nityavadānnānāt parikrayasya yadā svayaṃ na śaknuyāt tadā
parikrayeṇeti na śakyate kalpayitum | nanu ca sāmarthyādbhaviṣyati
lokavata | na hi śrutau pratyarthinyāṃ sāmarthyaṃ prabhavatītyuktam |
loke tu dṛṣṭārthatvāt yathārthamiti yuktam |

[p. b919]

|| 9 || 1tatrārthāt kartṛparimāṇaṃ syodaniyamo’viśeṣāt || 21 ||
evaṃ sthite parikrītakartṛtve anyatrotsargāt aniyamaṃ
saṅkhyāyāḥ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | ekasminnapi samākhyāvaraṇayo-
rupapatteḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu utpattau tāvat samākhyāto bhedaṃ manvānaḥ
na punareka eva saṃjñī | codanāvagamyatvāt saṃjñānām, codanā-
bhedācca yāvaccodanaṃ saṃjñibhedaniyamamuktavān | evaṃ darśapūrṇamā-
sādiṣvapītyuktam |

[p. b920]

|| 10 || 1camasādhvaryavaśca tairvyapadeśāt || 25 ||
camasādhvaryavaśca kiṃ ṛtviksaṅkhyāsaṅkhyeyāḥ uta saṅkhyāntara
paricthedyāḥ | nanu ca hotṛkāṇāṃ camasādhvaryava iti vyapadeśāt
anyairbhavitavyam | parasparavyapadeśādapyetadupapadyata iti pūrvaḥ
pakṣaḥ | na hi ṣaṣṭhayantānāṃ parasparasaṃbandho vidyata iti rāddhāntavādī
vyatiriktaṃ manyate | utpattau ca saṃjñābhedāt | tasmādanye te
iti |
 
|| 11 || 2utpattau ca bahuśruteḥ ||
idamidānīṃ sandihyate---kimaniyatā uta bahava iti

[p. b921]

prātipadikārthasiddheraniyatā iti | aniyame’pi bahuvacanopapatteḥ |
na, nityavadvahuvacanasya śrūyamāṇatvāt | utpattau ca bahuvacanasya
vivakṣitatvāt | asiddhārthalakṣaṇaṃ hi prātipadikaṃ na vacanama-
pekṣate | notpādyavacanam | tasmādvahavaḥ |

|| 12 || 1daśatvaṃ liṅgadarśanāt || 27 ||
sthite bahutve kimaniyamaḥ uta niyataiva kācit bahuvacanā-
bhidheyā saṅkhyā uta tritvasaṅkhyaiveti | kapiñjalādhikaraṇena tri-
saṅkhyaiveti prāpte darśanāda्daśapeye, svāmidaśatvācca daśaiveti niyamaḥ |

[p. b922]

anyatrāpi śrūyamāṇā niyatā saṅkhyā aniyatopadeśaṃ niyantumarhati
avirodhāt | tathā coktaṃ kāraṇādatikrama iti |

|| 13 || 1śamitā ca śabdabhedāt || 28 ||
evaṃ sthite śabhitāpi śabdabhedāt camasādhvaryuvadbhidyate |
saṃjñābhedaścopapanno bhavati | evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate-nātra bhedaḥ utpatti-
nibandhano hi bheda uktaḥ | na cāsyotpattirasti | kathaṃ tarhi saṃjñānyatvaṃ ?

[p. b923]

naiveyaṃ saṃjñā asya, virodhāt | kiṃ tarhi ? yaugikābhidhānameva |
evaṃ cādhvaryavamiti samākhyopapannā bhaviṣyati |

|| 14 || 1upagāśca liṅgadarśanāt || 30 ||
kena punarviśeṣeṇāyamadhikaraṇātideśaḥ ? ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ
upagā iti gānakāriṇāṃ saṃbandhāt camasādhvaryava iva hotra-
kāṇāmiti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | utpatteraśravaṇādityatidiṣṭo
rāddhāntaḥ | liṅgamuktam |

[p. b924]

1vikrayī tvanyaḥ karmaṇo’coditatvāt || 31 ||
somavikrayī punaḥ kaḥ ? teṣāmevānyatama iti manyate | nanu
cācodito vikrayaḥ | coditasya ca ṛtvijaḥ kartṛtvenopādīyante |
krayopādānaśeṣatvaṃ vikraye manvānasya pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu
lobhādevākṣepasiddheḥ dravyārjanavanna śeṣaṃ manyate | tasmādanā-
rtvijametat | ato’nya iti rāddhāntitam |

|| 16 || karmakāryāt sarveṣāmṛtviktvamaviśeṣāt || 32 ||
nanu cātra rāddhānto na ghaṭate | kathaṃ na ghaṭate ?

[p. b925]

utpattāvapi hi parisaṅkhyaiva prāpnoti | camasādhvaryūṇāmapyutpatti-
śravaṇāt | atha parisaṅkhyaiveṣṭā, ṛtuyajananimittanirasanamanarthakaṃ
syāt | atrocyate---utpattau naiva parisaṅkhyā prāpnoti | ekaikatra
udgātrādiśabdatulyatvāt ṛtvika्śabdasya nātrāpahnavanīyaṃ kiñci-
dasti | ṛtuyajananimitte punarapahnavaḥ prāpnoti | na cāsau
śakyate | evaṃ coparitanamadhikaraṇamupapannaṃ bhavati |

[p. b926]

|| 17 || 1niyamastu dakṣiṇābhiḥ śrutisaṃyogāt || 36 ||
ke ta iti | ṛtvigbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dadātīti prakramya yān
saṃkīrtayati te ta iti rāddhāntaḥ | tathā ye yajamānāsta ṛtvija
iti darśitam |

|| 18 || 2svāmisaptadaśāḥ karmasāmānyāt || 37 ||
saptadaśa ṛtvija iti śrūyate | tatra kiṃ saha yajamānen saptadaśa
uta yajamānavyatiriktā anye saptadaśeti karmakarasaṅkhyaiṣā | na ca
svāmī karmakaraḥ | ekadharmayuktānāṃ cāvāntarasaṅkhyopapadyate ityanya
eveti yuktam |

[p. b927]

atrocyate---svāmisaptadaśāḥ saptadaśaḥ svāmī yeṣāṃ te
tayoktāḥ | nanu ca karmakarasāmānyaprayuktaivaiṣā’vāntarasaṅkyā |
kena voktaṃ neti | kathaṃ tarhi svāmisaptadaśāḥ saptadaśa | na
svāmitayā yajamānaḥ saptadaśaḥ | kiṃ tarhi ? kartṛtayaiva | yajamānā-
pekṣaiṣā saṅkhyā nādhikāritayā | nanu ca sadasyaṃ vṛṇīta iti
varaṇotpattibhaṅgīkṛtya eṣāṃ saṅkhyā, na ca yajamāna utpadyate |
ucyate---ṣoḍaśānāmeva varaṇenotpattiḥ | nanu ca sadasyaṃ vṛṇīta iti
sadasyaḥ saptadaśo bhaviṣyati yadi brahmaṇyevāyaṃ sadasyaśabdo vartate |

[p. b928]

tasmāt svāmisaptadaśāḥ parigṛhyante | atha kasmādekaścama-
sādhvaryuḥ na parigṛhyate ? na te yajamānena varitavyā ityuktam |
tasmāt karmakaraṇasāmānyāt yajamāna evetyuktam |

|| 19 || 1te sarvārthāḥ prayuktatvādagnayaśca svakālatvāt || 39 ||
idamidānīṃ sandihyate---kiṃ sarvārthā ṛrtvijaḥ yāvatpuruṣakāryaṃ
yajamānasya tāvattaiḥ kriyate uta yāvadvacanamiti | “agnayaśca
svakālatvā”
diti | kimidaṃ ? nanu cādhānasya sarvārthatāṃ vadatā
agnīnāṃ sarvārthatvamuktam | kimidaṃ punaḥ sarvārthatocyate ? saiveyaṃ

[p. b929]

sarvārthatā’tra sādhyate svakālatvāditi | nanu ca svakālatvāt
yāvadagnikāryaṃ tatsarvamagnimirityucyate yathā yāvatpuruṣakāryaṃ tatsarva-
mṛtvigbhiriti | tataḥ kim ? tadeva nopapadyate samadhigatametat
agnayaśca svakālatvāditi | na hi svakālatvādyāvadagni-
kāryaṃ tadarthatā siddhayati | kiṃ tarhi ? svakālatvena
sarvakarmārthatā siddhayati na hi pratikarmotpattirbhavatītyarthaḥ |

[p. b930]

evaṃ tarhi bhavataivoktam | yasmādyasyaiva hetuḥ sa eva pratijñātārthaḥ |
tasmāt sūtradvayamevaitat | ādhastyasūtrasya parihāro’yamucyate |
idaṃ tu sthitameva | na hyatra vacanāni nityānuvādatayā kalpyante |
tasmādyavadvacanamevaitat ṛtvijāṃ kāryam |

|| 21 || 1tasyopadeśasamākhyānena nirdeśaḥ || 41 ||
idamidānīṃ cintyate | yadyena samākhyāyate tat
tenaiva kartavyaṃ uta kiñcidanyenāpi iti | eṣa tāvadutsargaḥ
yadyena samākhyāyate tattena kartavyamiti | ucyate---vacanāt

[p. b931]

samākhyānācca apavādaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi samākhyāḥ ? brāhmaṇagrāma-
vadityadoṣaḥ | darśanamuktam |

|| 22 || 1praiṣānuvacanaṃ maitrāvaruṇasyopadeśāt || 43 ||
maitrāvaruṇaḥ preṣyati cānucāheti kiṃ praiṣamātraṃ anuvacanamātraṃ
maitrāvaruṇena kartavyaṃ, uta saṃyuktameveti | saṃyuktasya vidyamāna-
tvānnāsaṃyuktaṃ parigṛhyate | caśabdaścaivaṃ samarthito bhavati |
darśanamuktam |

[p. b932]

|| 23 || 1camasāṃścamasādhvaryavassamākhyānāt || 46 ||
kiṃ camasaiścamasādhvaryavo juhvati utādhvaryureveti | samākhyai-
vānyathā nopapadyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | rāddhāntastu---adhvaryuśabda-
syotpattinimittatvāt | adhvaryuriva camaseṣu bhavantīti cama-
sādhvaryavaḥ tasmādadhvaryurjuhotīti | kathaṃ tarhi adhvaryuriva bhavati ?
anyatra vyāpṛte tasmin tatkāryakaratvāt | darśanamuktam |

|| 24 || 2vedopadeśāt pūrvavadvedānyatve yathopadeśaṃ syuḥ || 50 ||
śyenavājapeyāvudāharaṇam | kiṃ śyenaḥ udgātrā kartavyaḥ
samākhyānāta adhvaryuṇā ca vājapeyaḥ uta ṣoḍaśabhirapīti |

[p. b933]

viśeṣasamākhyānāta udgātrā’dhvaryuṇā ceti prāpte upakāradarśanāt
ṣoḍaśabhirapīti vadāmaḥ | klṛpto balīyānityuktau hetuḥ |

iti bṛhatyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya
saptamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b934]

tṛtīyādhyāyasya aṣṭamaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1svāmikarma parikrayaḥ karmaṇastadarthatvāt || 1 ||
samākhyayā ādhvaryavamiti prāpte adhikāraśrutivirodhāt
yājamānamiti rāddhāntaḥ | vacanādanyeṣāmṛtvijāmapi parikrayaḥ
syāt |

|| 2 || saṃskārastu puruṣasāmarthye yathāvedaṃ karmavadvayavatiṣṭheran ||
vapanādīnāṃ samākhyayā nādhikāro virudhyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. b935]

rāddhāntavādī tu puruṣasaṃskāraṃ manvāno na saṃskartuṃ puruṣāḥ pari-
krīyante | kiṃ tarhi kārayitum, ataḥ sāmarthyāt samākhyābādhaṃ
manyate | yatra punaḥ śrutyaiva saṃskāraścodyate na tatra sāmarthyā-
nnivṛttiḥ yathopavāsaṃ vakṣyati | athobhayānugrahāya samānavidhānaṃ
kasmānna bhavatīti cet ? ṛtvikṣu kriyamāṇo yajamānena kṛtaḥ
kārito vā na bhavati |

[p. b936]

kimiti na kṛtaḥ kārito vā ? 1sādhāraṇyādeva | nanu ceḍā-
bhakṣaṇavat svayaṃ ca karoti kārayati ca | na | tatra kartṛtvenā-
viśeṣāt | iha tu prādhānyāviśeṣādanupapattiḥ | tasmādyājamāna-
iti siddham | vacanādvā yajamānapañcamā iti |
*****
|| 3 || 2tapaśca phalasiddhitvāllokavat || 9 ||
kimatrātidiśyate ? rāddhāntaḥ | kā punaratra pūrvapakṣā-
śaṅkā ? iyamasti kleśarūpatvāt tapasaḥ | parikrayaśca
kleśāpanuttaye dṛṣṭārtho bhavati |

[p. b937]

na saṃskārasāmānyāditi rāddhāntaḥ | darśanamuktam | upa-
vāsaśca vacanādityetadapyuktam |

|| 4 || 1guṇatvācca vedena na vyavasthā syāt || 12 ||
hiraṇyamālyādiṣu tu guṇatvādeva padārthasya na samākhyayā
kartṛtvaṃ niyamyata iti sarvartvijāṃ pratipradhānāvṛttyā guṇo bhavitu-
marhati | yatra punaḥ kartṛtayaiva saṃbandhaḥ yathokthyādiṣu tatroktaḥ
samākhyayā niyamaḥ |

[p. b938]

|| 5 || 1tathākāmo’rthasaṃyogāt || 13 ||
kāmasaṃyoga idānīṃ śrūyamāṇaḥ kimārtvijo bhavatu guṇaphala-
saṃbandheṣu uta yājamāna iti | kiṃ yuktam ? ārtvijaḥ |
kutaḥ ? ṛtvika्padārthāśritatvāt guṇaphalasaṃbandhasya kāmasaṃyogo’pi
ṛtvijāmeva | ato guṇakāmeṣu ṛtvijāmadhikāra iti | atro-
cyate | yajamāna evādhikriyate sāṅge karmaṇyadhikṛtatvāt | nanu
cāsāvārtvijapadārthaḥ | neti brūmaḥ | tatkartṛko hi saḥ na punaḥ
tasya saḥ | yasya ca karma tadāśritamapi tasyaiveti yajamāna
evādhikriyate | evaṃ cātmanepadābhidhānamupapannaṃ bhavati |

[p. b939]

yatra ca punaḥ vacanādārtvijo’dhikāraḥ tatrānyadīyapadārthāśritepi
guṇe vyāpṛtasya phalaṃ bhavati iti kalpyate |

|| 6 || 1mantrāścākarmakaraṇāstadvat || 15 ||
idānīmāśīrmantrā udāharaṇam | kiṃ yājamāna utārtvijā
iti | ārtvijāssamākhyayeti prāpte brūmaḥ---liṅgādyājamānā iti |
liṅgaṃ hi samākhyāto balīya ityuktam | proṣitasamidādhānadarśanaṃ
ca liṅgam | nanu ca mama yajamānasyetyevamādi liṅgamupapadyate |

[p. b940]

ayameva doṣaḥ yadutādhyāhāro nāma | tasmādvaktureva mametyupapannaṃ
bhavati |

|| 7 || dvayāmnāteṣūbhau dvayāmnānasyārthavattvāt || 17 ||
kecinmantrāḥ yājamāne cādhvaryane cāmnātāḥ | tatra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ
dvābhyāṃ vaktavyāḥ utādhvaryuṇaiveti | kathaṃ punaḥ dvayāmnānāta dvābhyāṃ
ca samākhyātāḥ ekena vaktavyā iti | ekakāryatvānmantrāṇā-
miti pūrvapakṣavādī ekaprayojyatāṃ manyate | rāddhāntavādī tu
ekasyānuṣṭhānasmaraṇādanyasya ca pratyavekṣaṇāt āmnānadvayasyāpi
arthavattvāt dvābhyāmapi prayoktavyāḥ | samākhyādvayaṃ cānugṛhītaṃ
bhaviṣyati |

[p. b941]

|| 8 || 1jñāte ca vacanaṃ na hyavidvān vihito’sti || 18 ||
k‌lṛptīryajamānaṃ vācayatīti śrūyate | kiṃ vidvāneva vācayi-
tavyaḥ utāvidvānapīti saṃśayeḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ viduṣo’dhikārasiddheḥ
aviduṣo’dhikārāśaṅkā ? vācayatītyetasmādeva hi na viduṣo
vācayatīti dṛṣṭārthatā saṃbhavatīti | evaṃ tarhi aviduṣa eva vācanaṃ
bhavatu | bhavatu ko doṣaḥ ? viduṣo’dhikārahāniḥ prāpnoti |
nādhikāraṃ nivārayāmaḥ aviduṣo’pyastīti | idaṃphalamidamadhi-
karaṇam | atrocyate---nādhikāramākṣeptumidaṃ vacaḥ | bruvantameva
hi vācayatīti bravīti | nābruvataḥ pravṛttimāha | viduṣaśca

[p. b942]

prakṛtyarthe pravṛttiḥ nāviduṣaḥ śrutaḥ | ato yathādhikāraṃ vācanamiti
siddham |

|| 9 || 1yājamāne samākhyānāt karmāṇi yājamānāni syuḥ || 19 ||
iha dvandvānyudāharaṇam | kiṃ yajamānena kartavyāni utādhva-
ryuṇeti saṃśayaḥ | ekatra padārthāmnānāt | anyatra samabhyā-
śīkaraṇāt | tatra viśeṣasamākhyayā yājamānāni | padārtha-
pūrvakatvāt samabhyāśasya | atrocyate | padārthāstāvadādhvaryavā eva

[p. b943]

akriyamāṇe padārthe na yajamānena samabhyāśaḥ śakyate kartumiti
ādhvaryava eva | viśeṣasamākhyāpravṛttistu darśitā |

|| 10 || 1vipratiṣedhe karaṇaḥ samavāyaviśeṣāt itaramanyarateṣāṃ
yato viśeṣaḥ syāt || 21 ||
ṛtvigantarakāryopadeśe kimātmīyaṃ karma karotu uta ati-
diṣṭamiti saṃśaye atidiṣṭaṃ pratyakṣavidhānāditi siddham |

|| 11 || 2praiṣeṣu parādhikārāt || 23 ||
praiṣapraiṣārthau kimekena kartavyau uta dvābhyāmiti |

[p. b944]

prāptakālabhrāntyā ekeneti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | yuṣmadarthaparityāga-
kāraṇābhāvāditi rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 12 || 1adhvaryuśca darśanāt || 23 ||
idānīṃ kimadhvaryuḥ praiṣārthaṃ karotu utānya iti saṃśayaḥ | artha-
vādadarśanādanya iti prāptam | mantraliṅgadarśanāt samākhyānārtha-
saṃbhavāccādhvaryuḥ | arthavādastu guṇavādena parihṛtaḥ |

[p. b945]

|| 13 || 1ṛtvikphalaṃ karaṇerṣvathavattvāt || 25 ||
kaḥ punarāśīrmantrairasya viśeṣaḥ ? samākhyātaḥ sthite’dhvaryu-
kartṛtve agnyanvādhānasya śratyā mantravidhānam | na ca guṇānu-
rodhena pradhāna (kartṛtva)bādhaḥ śakyate vaktum | tasmādādhvaryavo mantra
iti sthite kasyāśīriti cintyate | liṅgādadhvaryoḥ | vaktā hi
uttamapuruṣeṇa saṃbadhyate | nanu karmasaṃbaddhaphalasaṅkīrtanāt karmārthatā
mantrāṇām | na | karmaprayuktayaśaḥ prakāśayataḥ upapannaiva karmārtha-
teti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | rāddhāntavādī tu yaścārthādartho na sa
codanārtha iti manvānaḥ karmasaṃbaddhameva phalaṃ prakāśayan mantraḥ

[p. b946]

karmortho bhavati | yajamānasaṃbaddhameva phalam | yajamānasya mameti
mantrārthaḥ |

|| 14 || 1karmārthaṃ tu phalaṃ teṣāṃ svāminaṃ pratyarthavattvāt || 28 ||
yatra punaḥ dṛṣṭārthameva tat kimṛtvijāmeva uta atrāpi karma-
phalameveti | uktena nyāyena karmaphalameveti vadāmaḥ | atrocyate |
yaścārthādartho na sa codanārtha iti anupakāramaṅgīkṛtyaitaduktam | yat
punaḥ karmaṇa upakaroti tadupādānaśeṣatayā bhavatyeva karmārthamiti
nākarmārthatā mantrāṇām |

[p. b947]

|| 15 || vyapadeśācca || 29 ||
yatra ca vyapadiśyata eva tatra bhavatyeva phaladarśanam---tannau
saheti |

|| 16 || dravyasaṃskāraḥ prakaraṇaviśeṣāt || 30 ||
vedirvedidharmāścetyuktamuttaravivakṣayeti |

|| 17 || nirdeśāttu vikṛtāpūrvasyānadhikāraḥ || 31 ||
sthitamaṅgapradhānārthaṃ prakṛtau barhiriti | vikṛtāvidānīṃ
cintyate---kimatrāpi aṅgapradhānārthe barhiṣi dharmā bhavanti
codakena uta prākṛtakāryaprayukta eveti | tatra prakṛtivadvikṛtā-
vapoṃti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

[p. b948]

rāddhāntavādī tu kāryamukhenāṅgapradhānārthasaṃbandhitayā sāmānyena
prakṛtivadvikṛtāviti dharmāṇāṃ vikṛtau nirūpaṇam | na yathā
prakṛtisaṃvandhitayā | saṃbandhasāmānyena hi sarvatropadeśaḥ
syāt | tataścohadarśanaṃ nopapadyate | tasmātsiddhaṃ na yūpabarhiṣi
dharmāḥ kartavyāḥ | añjanājye na ājyadharmāḥ |

|| 18 || 1virodhe ca śrutiviśeṣādavyaktaḥ śeṣe || 32 ||
anekatra śeṣabhāvo na nyāyyaḥ iti paribhojanīyādanyat
kartavyam |

[p. b949]

|| 19 || 1ayanayastvekadeśasya || 33 ||
pūrvādhikaraṇanyāyenānekatra śeṣabhāvo na nyāyya iti manvānasya
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | dvayavadānopasaṃhārāt pratipatteśca rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 20 || 2vikṛtau sarvārthaḥ śeṣaḥ || 34 ||
idānīṃ vikṛtābupāṃśutvādayo dharmāścintyante | kiṃ
tatrāpi prakṛtivat aṅgapradhānārthatā dharmāṇāṃ uta neti
saṃśayaḥ | prakṛtivadaṅgapradhānārthatā dharmāṇāmiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. b950]

naupadeśikātideśikaiḥupadeśaḥ saṃbadhyata iti | upadeśāpekṣa-
tvādatideśasya sāpekṣatvaṃ rāddhāntaḥ | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ na
saṃbadhyata iti ? upadeśāpekṣatvādatideśasya sāpekṣanirapekṣayoḥ
nirapekṣaṃ balīya iti nyāyavidaḥ |

|| 21 || 1sannidhānaviśeṣādasaṃbhave tadaṅgānām || 36 ||
śyene tvaupadeśikasaṃbandhābhāvāt ātideśikaireva saṃbandha iti
viśeṣaḥ |

[p. b951]

|| 22 || 1tatkāle vo liṅgadarśanāt || 39 ||
idānīṃ liṅgadarśanabhrāntyā sutyākālānāmevātra vidhānamiti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | aviśeṣāt sarveṣāmiti rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 23 || māṃsa tu savanīyānām || 42 ||
tarasāḥ savanīyāḥ puroḍāśā bhavantīti | atra savanīya-
puroḍāśayoḥ vivakṣāyāṃ vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt kasyāyathārthatvamiti |
dṛṣṭavyabhicārāt puroḍāśasya ayathārthatvamiti rāddhāntitam |

iti bṛhatyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya
aṣṭamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b952]

[p. b953]

caturthādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1athātaḥ kratvarthapuruṣārthayorjiṃjñāsā || 1 ||
kratvarthapuruṣārthayorjijñāsā kimiti pṛthak pratijñāyate ?
aidamarthyalakṣaṇameva hi śeṣatvam; śeṣaḥ parārthatvādityuktam | kratvartha-
tāpi tadantaḥpātinī pratijñātaivāthātaḥ śeṣalakṣaṇamityanena |
tasmāt punaḥ pratijñāyāḥ prayojanaṃ na vidmaḥ |

[p. b954]

atha mataṃ na śrutyādibhiḥ puruṣārthatā’vagamyate tasya lipsārtha-
lakṣaṇeti vakṣyati | kratvarthagrahaṇaṃ tarhi pratyuktam | na ceyamapi
puruṣārthatālakṣaṇā śakyate vaktum | na ca śabdādṛte yāgaḥ svargārtha
iti jñānamutpadyate | na ca tatsādhyaḥ svargaḥ puruṣārtha iti svato’ya-
mapyasamādhiḥ | yadapi śrutyādīnāṃ saṅkīrtanaṃ tadapi kasmai prayojanā-
yeti na vidmaḥ | śrutyādibhiśśeṣalakṣaṇaṃ nānyena pramāṇena | ataḥ
śeṣalakṣaṇamityetāvataiva tathā | tataḥ śrutyādisaṅkīrtanaṃ mandaprayo-
janamiva pratibhāti | yadapi kratvarthapuruṣārthayoḥ prayojakāprayo-
jakādīnantarbhāvayan pratijñātān kathayati tadapyasamañjasa iva |
kutaḥ ? antarbhāvo hi svarūpato lakṣaṇataḥ kāryato bhavati |

[p. b955]

trayamapyatra nāsti | svarūpaṃ tāvat pṛthagabhidhānādeva bhinnam |
lakṣaṇamapi svarūpabhedādeva bhinnam | lakṣaṇabhedādṛte na svarūpabhedaḥ
kāryamapi yaddarśitaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa tadayuktam | pradeśāntaraviruddha-
ścārthavādastāvat stutyarthaḥ prādhānyamapi upakāryopakāranibandhanam |
prayojakamapi prayojyaṃ prati prādhānyamātmanaḥ pratipādayati | na
punarātmanaḥ parārthatām | tadidaṃ rātriprayāṇamivādeśakaṃ bhāṣyaṃ
vyāmohamupajanayati |

[p. b956]

atrocyate---dvayamapyetad‏yuktaṃ bhedopanyāso’ntarbhāvaśca yathā
tatra | yatpunaḥ tādarthyaṃ śeṣatve heturiti satsu śrutyādiṣu
tadityuktam | nanu caivaṃ sati kratvarthatā pratyuktetyapyuktam |
bhavedetadevaṃ yadi vainiyogikamucyate | iha tu upādānanibandhana-
meva pratijānāti | kimupādānaṃ nāma ? niyogākṣepa upādānam |
tatkṛtaṃ ca prokṣaṇādeḥ kratvarthatvam | viniyogāt punaḥ vrīhyarthataiva |

[p. b957]

bhedārthapratijñānaṃ tarhi kimiti ? kimitivā’ntarbhāvaḥ | avagati
hetubhedādbhedaḥ upakārasāmānyādantarbhāvaḥ | prayojakaḥ kaścit
puruṣārtha iti kaścid‌grahaṇaṃ kiñcitprayojakasyāpyapuruṣārthatājñāpanārthaṃ
yathā---kasya ? ācāryakaraṇavidheḥ svādhyāyasya |

1yasmin prītiḥ puruṣasya tasya lipsārthalakṣaṇāvibhaktatvāt || 2 ||
“tasya lipsārthenaiva bhavati na śāstreṇa”
 iti viruddhamuktam |

[p. b958]

na śāstrādṛte svargaḥ sādhyatayā pratīyate | kartṛgāmitayā ca |
saṃbandhaśāstramaṅgīkṛtyaitaduktam | arthaśca niyogasiddhiḥ taccopādāna-
mityuktam | nanu ca kratvarthatāpyupādānenaivoktā | viniyoge-
satītyuktam | arthādeva hi puruṣārtha iti viśeṣaḥ |
kimidamavibhāgāditi ? puruṣasya kratusaṃbandhāvibhāgādityarthaḥ |

[p. b959]

etacca ṣaṣṭhe nipuṇataramupapādayiṣyāmaḥ | pariśeṣādviniyoge satīti
kratvarthatoktā bhavati |

evaṃ vā | nanu guṇaphalacodanā etā ityuktam | ko’yaṃ
punarvicāraḥ ? ucyate---pradarśitamasmābhiḥ viniyogatastaduktamiti |
iha punarupādānato dṛṣṭaprayojanasaṃbhavāt nādṛṣṭaṃ prayojanaṃ saṃbhavati
iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

na, yathāviniyogaṃ kāryāvagateḥ | viniyuktasyaiva hi kārya-
kalpanā nāviniyuktasyeti rāddhāntaḥ |

[p. b960]

ato viniyogataḥ upādānataśca puruṣārthataiva guṇaphalacodanā-
sviti sthitam | bādarigrahaṇaṃ punarupakārapradarśanārtham | na punaḥ
parārthatvāditi śeṣahetunivṛttyartham | kathaṃ ? viniyoge hi sa
uktaḥ | nanu bādariṇāpi viniyoga evāsāvupanyastaḥ | ata
evāsau nirākṛtaḥ | iha punaḥ kāryata evopādānamityupanyastam |

[p. b961]

ayathāviniyogatayā nirasyata ityavirodhaḥ | evaṃ vā | iha
dravyārjananiyamā udāharaṇam | tatra pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | niyamaniyo
mānāmadhikāraśūnyatvāt kāmaśrutiniyogenaikavākyatvāt adhi-
kārasiddheḥ | evaṃ ca kṣāmavatyādīnāmapi spaṣṭaiva karmārthatopapannā bhavati
iti | tasmādanadhikāriṇe vidherānarthakyaṃ mābhūditi kratvarthatā |
nanu kratvarthatve svatvameva na bhavatīti yāga eva na saṃvarteteti
pralapitamidaṃ kenāpi |

[p. b962]

ārjanaṃ svatvaṃ nāpādayatīti vipratiṣiddham | kathaṃ tarhi
kratvarthatā ? vanīvananavat tadarthaṃ svatvamityabhiprāyaḥ |

atrocyate---neme adhikāraśūnyā niyamavidhayaśśakyante
vaktum | jīvanāya pravṛttā adhikṛtā bhaviṣyanti |

[p. b963]

na ca śabdādevādhikāraḥ adhikārādeva [hyadhikāraḥ] sa tu kvaci-
cchabdāvagamyaḥ kvacit pramāṇāntarāvagamyaḥ | ataḥ svatvasyā-
viśeṣāt sarvapuruṣārthanirvṛttau na viśeṣaśśakyate’vadhārayitum |

1api ca yadi śāstrāt karmārthaṃ dravyaṃ tannānyatra viniyujyeta,
tathā’’rjitaṃ, tatra sarvatantraparilopaḥ syāt2 | ko’rthaḥ ? kimidaṃ
tantram ? jīvanamiti cet aniyamāttarhi jīviṣyati | niyamasya
kratvarthatvāt | atha kratvantarāṇi tantraśabdābhidheyānyabhipretāni
tathāpi pratikratu niyamānna lopaḥ |

atrocyate---kratvantarāṇyeva tantraśabdābhidheyānyabhipretāni |

[p. b964]

nacārjanasya viśeṣopādānatve pramāṇamasti | na ca sarvatantraṃ sarveṇa
dravyeṇa śakyate kartuṃ iti tantraparilopaḥ sarvakarmaṇām, ayaṃ
cāparo doṣaḥ nānyasya tantre pratate anyattantraṃ pratāyate iti darśanaṃ
nopapadyate | atha naivedaṃ kasyacidekasyāṅgamiti | evaṃ tarhi naivedaṃ
prayogavacanena gṛhyate, anupāttaprayogavacanasyāṅgataiva nāsti |
asatyaṅgatve yasya kila aṅgatā nāsti tasyāprayogeṇānupa-
kārakatvamiti so’yamaṅgatā’bhāva evānupakārakatvena darśitaḥ1 |

[p. b965]

na cādhānavadbhavitumarhati | na ca dravyārthamevedamārjanaṃ ādhāna-
vadagnyarthameva bhavitumarhati, jīvanāya pravṛttasyādhikārāvagamāt |
ataḥ puruṣārthamevedamārjanam | tasmāt kratvarthatve tantraparilopaḥ
sthito doṣaḥ | kṣāmavatyādīnāmāhitāgneradhikārāvagamāt bhavatu
kratvarthatā phalaṃ kalpayitum | puruṣārtha eva vā naimittiko’stu |
tasmāt puruṣārthā dravyārjananiyamā iti sthitam | ato niya-
mātikramaḥ puruṣasyānarthaḥ na kratoḥ |

|| 3 || tadutsarge karmaṇi puruṣārthāya śāstrasyānatiśaṅkyatvāt
na ca dravyaṃ cikīrṣyate tenārthenībhisambandhāt kriyāyāṃ puruṣaśrutiḥ || 3 ||

[p. b966]

yasmin prītiḥ puruṣasya tasya lipsā arthalakṣaṇetyuktam |
iha tu prīteraśravaṇāt arthataśca śravaṇādanavagamāt adhikāri-
śūnyatā vidheḥ | na cānadhikāraṃ puruṣārthaṃ bhavati iti tasya kratvartha-
syaivekṣaṇasyāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ niyamo vā | atrābhidhīyate---prītyu-
tsargepyetaduktam | adhikārāśravaṇepi adhikāro yuktaḥ kalpayitum
na punaḥ kratusaṃbandhakalpanā | kaḥ punaratra viśeṣaḥ ? ayamasti
viśeṣaḥ | yathaivādhikāre śrūyamāṇe gamyamānatatkartṛtaiva nyāyyā |

[p. b967]

evaṃ gamyamānakartravagamāt adhikārakalpanaiva nyāyyā | kathaṃ tarhi
kartradhikaraṇam ? tatra hi viniyogo’dhikārakalpanāyāḥ
pratipakṣaḥ | iha tu apratipakṣā’dhikārakalpaneti | kiṃ bhūtaḥ
punaratrādhikriyate ? “etāvatā hainasā viyukto bhavati”
 iti
śravaṇāt jīvata eva vā’dhikāraḥ |

[p. b968]

ato nityo’yaṃ jauvanānupālanīyo niyamaḥ svadharmatayā | nanu
ca prasajyapratiṣedho nyāyyaḥ | satyam, tasya vratamityanvayāvagate-
rlakṣaṇaiva nyāyyā | ādhānavadapyatra naiva dravyārthatā saṃbhavati |
aviniyuktadravyasaṃyogāt | dṛṣṭasya ca saṃskārasyāvidyamānatvāt |
kiṃ dṛṣṭārthatā śakyate vaktum ? bāḍhaṃ śakyate darpaṇapramārjanavat
loka evopayogadarśanāt | tasmāt puruṣārtho niyam iti yuktam |
dṛṣṭārthāsaṃbhavena ca karkarīkarṇādayo’pi puruṣārthā ityuktam |

[p. b969]

|| 4 || dravyoṇi tvaviśeṣeṇānarthakyāt pradīyeran || 7 ||
uktaṃ ‘sphayaśca kapālāni ca’
 ‘sphayenoddhanti’
 ityetayoḥ
kiṃ viniyojakamiti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī “sphayenoddhanti”

ityevamādibhirvākyaiḥ sākṣāt yajñasaṃbandhānavagamāt yajñā-
yudhānīti vidhyantaraṃ manyate | nanu vājapeyasya yūpa itivat
praṇāḍyāpi saṃbandha upapadyate | yuktaṃ yūpābhāvātkratostatra |

[p. b970]

iha punaḥ kiṃ nopapadyata ityabhiprāyaḥ | evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---yajñā-
yudhānīti tāvat lakṣaṇā | lakṣaṇā cet sākṣādvayavadhāne ca na
kaścidviśeṣa pramāṇāntarānusāriṇī hi lakṣaṇā | na ca vidyamāne
pramāṇe pramāṇāntarakalpanā śakyā | sphayenoddhantītyubhaya
siddhatvāt | evaṃ ca vikalpadoṣaḥ parihṛto bhavati | liṅgaṃ cāpyupapannaṃ
bhavati |

[p. b971]

|| 5 || tatra caikatvamayajñāṅgamarthasya guṇabhūtatvāt || 11 ||
ekatvaṃ viniyogābhāvānna yajñāṅgamiti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhi-
prāyaḥ | kathaṃ punarekatvaṃ na viniyujyate ? vibhaktayo hi prāti-
padikārthasya svārthasaṃbandhamabhidadhati | tasmādekatvaṃ karaṇatvaṃ ca
prātipadikārthasyaiva | naikatvasya karaṇabhāvaḥ | karaṇasaṃbandhe sati
prātipadikārthasaṃbandho na syāt | yathā ‘arūṇayā’
 iti na
dravyeṇa ataḥ pratīyamānamapyetanna kriyayā saṃbadhyate yathā pūrvo
dhāvatītyaparapratyayaḥ | liṅgadarśanāni caivamupapannāni bhavanti |
evaṃ prāpte ucyate---na viniyogādeva kriyāṅgatā, kiṃ tarhi ?

[p. b972]

upādānādapi hi śeṣatābhavati | yathaiva hi karaṇībhūtasyai-
vopādānaṃ evamekatvayuktasyāpi | na hyakārakasyopādānamasaṅkhyā-
yuktasyāpi saṃbhavati | tathā ca laukikāḥ gāmānayetyevamādiṣu
tathaiva pratipadyante | tadidamuktaṃ “na śrutirbrūte (pramite)
vākyārtho nāsti”
 iti1 | na viniyogo brūte upādānaṃ
nāstītyarthaḥ | nanu ‘na śrutirbrūte vākyārtho nāsti’
 iti
anya evārthaḥ pratīyate | ko’rthaḥ ? ayamarthaḥ pratīyate |
na śrutyā vākyārthasya virodho’stīti | śrutyarthāparityāgena
vākyāt kratvarthatāvagamyate | virodhe na daurbalyamuktam |

[p. b973]

uktamasmābhiḥ viniyogo na saṃbhavatīti karaṇasyevaikatvasyeti |
kathaṃ tarhi vākyaśabdaḥ ? samabhivyāhāraparyāyo’yaṃ na viniyo-
jakaparyāyaḥ | tasmātsiddhamupādānataḥ śeṣatvaṃ saṅkhyāyā liṅgasya
ca | evaṃ darśanānyupapannāni pūrvapakṣadarśanaṃ cānuvādaḥ |

evaṃ vā | liṅgādhikaraṇam punaḥ evaṃ veti
vadataḥ ko’bhiprāyaḥ aparicchedakatvaṃ liṅgasya pūrvapakṣavādī
manyate | paricchedikā hi saṅkhyā dravyasya parimāṇamācaṣṭe

[p. b974]

śravaṇāviśeṣālliṅgamapyupādīyata iti rāddhāntaḥ | nanu cāpari-
māṇāpādakatvālliṅgaṃ nopādātavyamityuktam | nātra parimāṇa-
mupādāne upayujyate | viśeṣaniṣṭhatvādupādānasya | yathā’nuṣṭhāna-
viśeṣākāṅkṣā tathā kārakaviśeṣākāṅkṣāpi | atassaṅkhyāvadupādāna-
miti siddham | śeṣaṃ pūrvavat |

|| 7 || 1āśrayiṣvaviśeṣeṇa bhāvo’rthaḥ pratīyeta || 18 ||
ihāśrayiṇaḥ padārthā udāharaṇam | nanu bhāṣyamayuktaṃ
kiṃ yajimātraṃ saṃskāro devatāyā uta yajinā’dṛṣṭaṃ
devatāyāṃ kriyata iti yasmātsaṃskāramātramadṛṣṭa[ma]kurvadanarthakam |

[p. b975]

dvividhamākhyātamuktaṃ paraprāptaniyogānuvādakam | avahantītyāgne-
yādicodanāvagataniyogānuvādakaṃ svaniyogasaṃbandhitayā’dhikāra-
sādhanaṃ ca | ihedānīṃ sandihyate---kiṃ paraprāptaniyogānuvādātmakaṃ
uta svaniyogasaṃbandhitayādhikārasādhanamiti | kutaḥ saṃśayaḥ ?
aṃśena dṛṣṭasya saṃbhavaḥ aṃśenādṛṣṭasyeti pūrvapakṣavādī dṛṣṭe
saṃbhavatyadṛṣṭakalpanā na nyāyyeti paraprāptānuvādaṃ manyate | nanu
yajyartho’tra na niṣpadyate | anuṣṭhānād‌dṛṣṭopapattyartho
(’dṛṣṭo)bhaviṣyatītyabhiprāyaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---dṛṣṭopapattyarthataiva
na ghaṭate | kasmāt ? niyogasiddheravivakṣitatvāt |

[p. b976]

niyogāddhi niyogārthe pravṛttiḥ | atha tadarthamutpattiniyogo
vivakṣyate siddhamapūrvārthatvam | paścādadhikāraniyogārthatve yāgasya
---nirūpaḍhe’ṅgasaṃbandhe sati tad‌dvāreṇa dravyadevatāgatadṛṣṭasaṃskāraniṣpa-
ttyarthamityanumanyata eva | na ca tṛtīyaprakāro’yaṃ niyoganiṣpatte-
stulyatvāt samidādivat | prayojanaṃ tu dṛṣṭamadṛṣṭaṃ vā bhavatu |
prayojanavaśācca anuṣṭhānamananuṣṭhānaṃ vā bhavatu | sarvathā sidhyati
svaniyogārthatā |

[p. b977]

|| 8 || 1arthe samavaiṣamyamato dravyakarmaṇām || 21 ||
‘atikrāntastṛtīyaviṣayaḥ’
 iti | ko’yaṃ tṛtīyaviṣayaḥ ?
pārārthyapratipattiḥ | sā’tikrāntā | idānīṃ punaḥ sthite pārārthye
kevalamupādānameva cintyate kena prayojanenopādānaṃ kena neti |
nanu ca prāgapyasmāt prayojanakṛtaivopādānacintā | kena viśeṣeṇa-
damucyate idamidānīmiti | satyaṃ---kintu pārārthyamapi tannimitta-
kameva, na (vi)niyoganimittakam | ata idamuktaṃ tṛtīyaviṣaya
iti | tṛtīyasyāyaṃ viṣayaḥ na punaḥ tārtīyaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ śakyate-
’vagantum | ato vidhinimittakamevāsya pārārthyamiti caturthe
cintitam | etacca vyākhyātam---na śrutyādibhiḥ sarvasya

[p. b978]

pārārthyaṃ śakyate pratipādayitumiti | etadevātra kathyate | tathā ca
yāgaphalayoradhikārāt pārārthyaṃ vakṣyati | ata eva vādarereṣā
bhrāntiḥ dravyaguṇasaṃskāreṣveva śeṣatvamiti | śrutyādīnāṃ tatra
dravyādau saṃbhavāt upādānādhikārayoḥ sāmarthyamapaśyato’va-
dhāraṇāt | ata evedaṃ lakṣaṇāntaramucyate śeṣāvagamatulyatvepi |

|| 9 || ekaniṣpatteḥ sarvaṃ samaṃ syāt || 12 ||
ataḥ prabhṛtīdānīṃ kevalopādānavyāpāra eva cintyate |
śeṣatā tu viniyogata eva | nopādānataḥ | pūrva-
pakṣavādī tu kevalopakāradarśanādevopādānamāha, na śeṣatā-
’pekṣaṇīyeti manyate | yathaivopādānaśeṣeṣu paśvekatvādiṣu |

[p. b979]

tasmādubhayaṃ prayojakamiti | atrābhidhīyate---nātra prayojakatvaṃ
vājinasya | kutaḥ ? śeṣabhūtasya hi prayojanākāṅkṣā | sā ca
yasya śeṣabhūtā tenaiva prayojanena nivṛtterna prayojanāntaraṃ prayoja-
katvenāpekṣate | kathaṃ tarhi aśeṣabhūtasyopādānaśeṣatvaṃ, ekatvādeḥ
aśeṣabhūtasya prayojanākāṅkṣaiva nāstīti | aśeṣatve upādāna-
syaivānupapatteḥ | atrocyate---śeṣasyāvagamāt anyathānavagamāt
avagamapūrvakatvāccopādānasya | kiṃ ca śeṣabhūtasya prayojyatvānya-
thānupapatteḥ | na hyanavacchinnamupādātuṃ śakyata ityuktam |

[p. b980]

yatpunarna śeṣabhūtaṃ na ca śeṣasthamavagamyate na śeṣopādānenā-
pekṣyate na tat prayojyaṃ bhavitumarhati | yathā na dadhyānayanaṃ
na vājinaśeṣabhūtaṃ tathā pradarśitaṃ bhāṣye | tathā ca liṅgaṃ
pradarśitam |

|| 10 || padakarmāprayojakaṃ nayanasya parārthatvāt || 25 ||
kaḥ punarādhastyenādhikaraṇenāsya viśeṣaḥ ? ayamasti
viśeṣaḥ tatra hi dadhyānayanaṃ āmikṣānirvṛttisamakālameva
vājinamanuniṣpādayati | ato yuktaṃ vājinaniṣpatteḥ na
kāraṇāntarāpekṣā | iha punaḥ saptamaṃ padaṃ ityukte bhavatyā-
kāṅkṣā kasyeti | prakṛtatvādekahāyanyā iti gamyate |

[p. b981]

tasmādubhayārthāmekahāyanīṃ manyate | evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---satyaṃ
yadi kasyetyākāṅkṣopapadyate anirjñātasaṃbandhāntare saṃbandhinyu-
padiśyamāne bhavatyākāṅkṣāpūrvikā prajñā | yatra punassaṃbandhyantarapūrvaka
evopadeśaḥ | tatra yathānirjñātasaṃbandhipurassara evopadeśaḥ iti
darśitamaśvapratigrahe | ataḥ krayārthaivaikahāyanī saptamapadasaṃbandhinīti na
prayujyate | atra bhāṣyam---‘prasaṅgamupajīvatā’
 iti | nanu cedaṃ prayojana-
lakṣaṇam | kaḥ prasaṅgasyāvasaraḥ ? vidyamāne hi prayojakatve prasaṅga
uktaḥ | iha tu prayoktṛtaiva nāstīti | ucyate---śāstraprasaṅga-
maṅgīkṛtyaitaduktam | na prayogaprasaṅgamityadoṣaḥ |

[p. b982]

|| 11 || 1arthābhidhānakarma ca bhaviṣyatā saṃyogasya tannimittatvāt
tadartho hi vidhīyate || 26 ||
‘puroḍāśakapālena tuṣānupavapati’
 iti nātra śāstra-
prasaṅgaḥ saṃbhavatīti prayoktṛtvaṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate
tuṣopavāpasyāpi na ca nāntarīyakaḥ śakya upavāpo vaktum
vājinavat | tasmādubhayaṃ prayojakamiti | atrābhidhīyate
---satyaṃ viniyoge nāsti śāstraprasaṅgaḥ | nāpi nāntarīyaka-

[p. b983]

bhāvaḥ | kintūpāttasyāyaṃ viniyoga iti nopādānakalpa-
nā kramate | tadidamuktam---“bhaviṣyatā saṃbandho’yaṃ na bhūtavartamānā-
bhyāṃ”
 iti | yadi punaḥ bhūtavartamānābhyāṃ saṃbandhaḥ kimupādānaṃ
syāt ? ucyate---na bhūtasya, tasya bhaviṣyadarthatayā anupātta-
syaiva pālanaṃ bhaviṣyati | pālanabhūtamevopādānaṃ yataḥ | vartamāna-
syāpi yaḥ saṃbandhī tasyānyatra viniyogo na saṃbhavatītyarthādanya-
dupādeyam | bhaviṣyadarthatve punaḥ anyārthamevopāttasya viniyogaḥ
saṃbhavatīti siddhamaprayojakatvam | tuṣopavāpastu kartavyo na
kartavya iti nāsyādhikaraṇasyāyaṃ viṣayaḥ |

[p. b984]

|| 12 || paśāvanālambhāllohitaśakṛtorakarmatvam || 27 ||
āsminnadhikaraṇe hṛdayādyavadānavacanaṃ lohitaśakṛtsaṃpravyādha---
vacanaṃ ca tulyam | prakṛtapaśuviṣayatayā ‘agnīṣomīyaṃ paśumālabheta’

iti dvayorapi prayojakatāṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī |

atrābhidhīyate---‘hṛdayasyāgre’vadyapi’
 iti yāgo-
pasaṃhṛtiriyam | aṅgaistṛtīyasavana iti śravaṇāt |

[p. b985]

śakṛtsaṃpravyādhastu tāvanmātraparyavasāyitvāt prakṛtāśrayatvācca |
pratipattiśca na prayojikā | na pratipādyaṃ pratipattyarthamupādīyata
iti nyāyāt | ato na prayojakaṃ paśoḥ śakṛtādīti |

|| 13 || ekadeśadravyaṃ cotpattau vidyamānasaṃyogāt || 28 ||
nanu cātraṃ pūrvapakṣa eva nopapadyate | upāttadravyaviṣayatvāt
iśukhaṇḍavaccheṣāvagateḥ | satyamevaṃ, kintvatra puroḍāśasyānyārtha-
tvāt nānyatra kāryāntare viniyoga upapadyata iti |

[p. b986]

ato dravyāntaramupādeyamiti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | rāddhāntavādī tu
na dvayavadānamātropasaṃhāraḥ dvayavadānamātreṇaivāgneyayāganirvṛttimupa-
saṃhāramanyatra viniyogopapattyarthamuktavān | yadyanyasmai dīyate
dīyamānaṃ hi tadā saṃpradānārthamiti tannopapadyate | tasmānna yāga-
kāryādanyatra viniyogassaṃbhavati | yāgakāryatayā punaḥ nānyārthatā
śakyate vaktum | ato nāgneyādicodanāyā viroṃdhaḥ | tasmāda-
pratipakṣekṣukhaṇḍanyāyenānupādānaṃ siddham śeṣadarśanāt tathā samava-
dānaśrutirityuktaṃ bhāṣye |

|| 14 || 1abhidhāraṇe viprakarṣādanuyājavatpātrabhedaḥ syāt ||

[p. b987]

kiṃ punaḥ puroḍāśakapālenātra viśiṣyate ? dravyopādānaṃ
tāvat tadvannāstīti sthitameva | dhāraṇasya tūpādānaṃ haviṣaḥ
prādhānyānnānyārthatayā virudhyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | kathaṃ
punaḥ haviṣaḥ prādhānyaṃ dvitīyāśravaṇādabhidhāraṇasya tadarthatvāt
prayājaśeṣasya ca puroḍāśakapālavat tadarthatvāt |
siddhāntastu na prādhānyaṃ haviṣāṃ dṛṣṭārthatayā’’jyasyaiveti yuktam |

[p. b988]

tasmāt pratipattireveyaṃ prayājaśeṣasyeti | kathaṃ tarhi havīṃṣya-
bhidhārayatīti bahuvacanaṃ ekena haviṣā’bhidhāraṇanirvṛtteḥ | agṛ-
hyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt bahuvacanamaviruddham | ato dravyopādānavat
dhāraṇopādānamapi na yuktaṃ sannihita eva haviṣi pratipattisiddheḥ
iti |

nanu dvitīyātṛtīyayoranyārthatā grahītuṃ na śakyā yathārthatayāpi
vacanāvirodhāt | satyaṃ, yadyavirodhī vacanasya syāt | iha tu

[p. b989]

prayājaśeṣaśabdena virudhyate pratipattau punaḥ nāyaṃ virodha iti
prātipadikārthānurodhinī guṇabhūtārthavādinī vibhaktiriti yuktam |
atrāpyanyārthataiva nyāyyā dvayoḥ karmaṇorekasmin vākye saṃbandhānu-
guṇaḥ saptamyartho draṣṭavyaḥ | darśanamuktam |

|| 15 || 1samānayanaṃ tu mukhyaṃ syālliṅgadarśanāt || 40 ||
‘atihāyeḍo barhiḥ prati samānayate’

ityatra sandehaḥ---kimuparitanaprayājadvayārthamevedaṃ samānayanaṃ

[p. b990]

tattaścaupabhṛtasyājyasya prayājānūyājārthasya taddharbhāṇāṃ ca prayojakam |
uta miśrīkaraṇārthamevedaṃ jauhavasaṃskārārtham | tataśca paraprayukta-
dravyasādhyatvāt aprayojakamājyasya dharmāṇāṃ ceti | tatra pūrva-
pakṣavādī samānayanamātraśravaṇāt miśrīkaraṇamātraṃ manyate | aṣṭā-
vupabhṛti gṛhṇātīti ca sarvaprayājārthatāṃ manyate | kutaḥ ?
viśeṣāśravaṇāt | tasmādadṛṣṭārthamevedaṃ samānayanamiti |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---prayājadvayasādhanārthamevedaṃ samānayanam |
kutaḥ ? evaṃ dṛṣṭārthatā samānayanasya bhavatiupabhṛti ca
prayājārthasya grahaṇasya |

[p. b991]

nunu cāviśeṣeṇa tadaupabhṛtaṃ sarvārthaṃ śrūyate | śrutānu-
sāreṇa cādṛṣṭārthatā nyāyyā | nāviśeṣaśrutiḥ sāmarthyena viśeṣe-
’vasthāpyamānā śabdārthatāṃ jahāti | tasmāt śabdasyāpratirodhi---
tvāt dṛṣṭe saṃbhavati adṛṣṭakalpanā na nyāyyeti rāddhāntadarśana-
muktam |

|| 16 || tatrotpattiravibhaktā syāt || 42 ||
kaḥ punaratra pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ ? ubhayamubhayārthamiti
vadataḥ iti | ayamabhiprāyaḥ aviśeṣeṇa śravaṇāt---caturjuhvāṃ
gṛhṇātīti, viśeṣataśca kāryasiddheḥ prayājagrahaṇamanūyāja-
vannityānuvādaṃ manyate jauhave |

[p. b992]

atrocyate---yadyupādānata eva prāptiḥ syāt syādetadevaṃ
upādānamātrādubhayārthatāpratīteḥ | evaṃ tarhi anūyājamātrārtha-
mevaupabhṛtaṃ bhavatu | kevalānūyājārthaśravaṇāt | ucyate---prāptārtha-
tvādasya viniyojakasya kevalānūyājasaṃbandhino’nuvādaṃ manyate |
kutaḥ prāptiḥ ? prayājānūyājabhyastadgṛhṇātītyubhayārthatvāt |

nanu samānayanādhikaraṇe sthite kathamidānīmāśaṅkyate | tatra
hi uparitanaprayājārthamaupabhṛtaṃ samānīyata iti vadatā ubhayārthatva-
muktam | kathaṃ kevalānūyājārthatā āśaṅkyate | ucyate---sthite
etasmin tadbhavati anyathā’dṛṣṭārthameva samānayanaṃ saṃskāramātraṃ
syāt |

[p. b993]

|| 17 || 1tadaṣṭasaṅkhyaṃ syāt || 46 ||
aṣṭāvupabhṛti gṛhṇāti iti śrūyate | atra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ dve
ete caturgṛhīte utāṣṭagṛhītamekamevedamiti | nanu samānayanādhi-
karaṇa idamuktam dvauṃ prayājau yaṣṭumekamidaṃ caturgṛhītaṃ samānīyata
iti | kayā yuktyā ? evaṃ ca dṛṣṭārthatā bhaviṣyatītyanayā yuktyetyarthaḥ |
tadevedamākṣipyate | aṣṭasaṃṅkhyāśravaṇāt | dṛṣṭārthatā ca samānayana-
syopapadyata eva āghāre prācīne prayājatraye ca vyāpṛtasya jauhavasyā-
jyasya svakāryaṃ pratyaśaktasyānugrahāya samānīyata iti | ato’tra

[p. b994]

pūrvapakṣavādī śrutatvādaṣṭasaṅkhyākameva grahaṇamiti manyate |
rāddhāntastu bhinnaviṣayatvāt catussaṅkhyāṣṭasaṅkhyayoḥ nānya-
tarasyāprāptiḥ śakyate varṇayitum avirodhāt | kathaṃ punaraṣṭasaṃkhyā
catussaṅkhyāṃ niṣyādayati home ? yasmāddve ete caturgṛhīte saṃyo-
ginī, prayājānūyājebhya ityubhayārthatvena viniyogāt | ubhayośca
dvābhyāṃ caturgṛhītābhyāṃ prayojanamaṣṭagrahaṇena saṃpādyate aṣṭāvupabhṛti
gṛhṇātīti | dve ete caturgṛhīte iti darśanaṃ ca pūrvavat samarthitaṃ
bhavati | catugṛhītānyājyāni bhavantīti hetuvannigadaśca
samarthito bhavati |

iti bṛhatyāṃ caturthādhyāyasya
prathamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b995]

[p. b996]

caturthādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || svarustvanekaniṣpattiḥ svakarmaśabdatvāt || 1 ||
ekamatrādhikaraṇaṃ vākyāntarābhiprāyeṇa varṇitam | dvitīya-
ntvekavākyamaṅgīkṛtya | yuktayaścaikavākyavicāre vākyāntarā-
pekṣiṇyāṃ ca mīmāṃsāyāṃ tulyā iti pradarśitam | yuktiśca
śrutyā vidhānam |

[p. b997]

[p. b998]

|| 2 || śākhāyāṃ tatpradhānatvāt || 7 ||
kimarthamidamadhikaraṇamasmin prayojakāprayojakalakṣaṇe ?
nātra vidhitaḥ pūrvapakṣo rāddhānto vā’vagamyate |
atrocyate---vidhita eveyaṃ cintā | kiṃ vṛkṣaprāgde-
śāt śākhopādānamuta vihāraprāgdeśādānayanopādānamiti |

[p. b999]

viniyogavadabhidhānamanuvartate upādānamiti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
rāddhāntastu---aparihāryalakṣaṇamidamābhidhānamiti śākhaivopādeyā
prācīnānayanameva deśaviśiṣṭam | ayaṃ cāparo viśeṣaḥ |
avaśyopalakṣayitabyā śākhā pakṣadvayepītyayameva pakṣo nyāyyaḥ |

|| 2 || śākhāyāṃ tatpradhānatvāt || 7 ||
atra parivāsanopādānaṃ śākhopādānaṃ vā cintyate, avi-

[p. b1000]

śeṣāt | kiṃ punaratra yuktam ? ubhayaṃ parivāsanasya
prayojakaṃ mūlamagraṃ ca dvayorapi śākhābhidhānatvāt | nanu
ca kriyādvayamatra śrūyate parivāsayati karoti iti ca |
tataḥ kim ? idaṃ tataḥ bhavati | śākhāparivāsanasya karma
karoterupaveṣaḥ | tasmānnopaveṣaḥ karmakārakasya prayoktā | ucyate
---śākhāśabdaḥ samūlamagramācaṣṭa ityuktamasmābhiḥ | tatra mūlamupaveṣaṃ
karoti | agramapi kāryāntare viniyuktamiti dvayoḥ prayojakatvam |

[p. b1001]

atrocyate---syādetadevaṃ yadi mūlamupaveṣaṃ karotīti saṃbandhaḥ | kathaṃ
tarhi saṃbandhaḥ ? upaveṣaṃ karotīti mūlata iti | kimato yadyevaṃ ?
etadato bhavati | anyato niṣpannānmūlādupaveṣa iti | tasmāt
yadyapi samūlamagraṃ śākhāśabdenocyate tathāpi na parivāsanamupa-
veṣārthaṃ iti siddham |

kiṃ ca śākhāśabdābhidhānaṃ ca kāryāntareṣu agra
eva prayuktaṃ śākhayā vatsānapākaroti iti | ato viniyo-
