gāta mūlaparivāsanārtho’yamagre śākhāśabdaḥ prayukta iti |

[p. b1002]

tasmānnopaveṣaḥ prayojakaḥ | prayojanamuktam |

|| 3 || 1haraṇe tu juhotiryogasāmānyāt || 10 ||
nanu cedaṃ haraṇaṃ prastarapratipattyarthameva; yadyapi haratiḥ yajatyartho
māntravarṇiko devatāvidhiḥ tathāpi pratipattikarmaivaitat yathottamaḥ
prayājaḥ | pratipattau ca na dravyasya guṇabhāvaḥ | nanu ca yathottamaprayāje
ājyaṃ pratipattāvapi guṇabhūtaṃ tathātra śākhāpi bhaviṣyatīti cet |

[p. b1003]

naivam | dravyasyātra pratipattiḥ | tatra punardevatāsmaraṇārtho
yāga iti yukto dravyasya guṇabhāvaḥ |

nanu cottamaprayājo naiva pratipattiḥ dravyaṃ hi yatra pratipādyate
tatpratipattikarma | kenedamuktaṃ kārakārtho yo yāgaḥ pratipattikarme-
tyucyate kvaciddeśavirecanāt saṃskāraḥ, kvacit smaraṇamiti |
prayojanaviśeṣā hyete | ato na śākhā guṇabhūteti śakyate pūrva-
pakṣayitum | ucyate---pratipattāveva tṛtīyāśravaṇāt guṇabhāvaṃ pūrva-
pakṣavādī manyate, nāvaśyaṃ pratipattau sarvameva pradhānaṃ bhavati | yadyathā
śrūyate tattathaiva bhavitumarhati yathā juhvā juhotīti guṇabhūtaiva juhūḥ |

[p. b1004]

atrābhidhīyate | bhavedetadevaṃ yadyatra sahaśabdo na śrūyate |
sahaśabdaśravaṇe tu tulyayogitvaṃ prastareṇa saha śākhāyā avagamyate |
tasmācchākhāpi pratipādyata eva | anyathā hi tulyayogo na syāt |
ataśśākhāyāmapi dvitīyārtha eva prastaravad‌draṣṭavyaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi
tṛtīyā ? na ceyaṃ kārakalakṣaṇā kiṃ tarhi sahayogalakṣaṇārthā |
prasiddhena ca sahayogo lakṣyata iti vibhaktivyatyaya uktaḥ |
prasiddhatvāt prastarasya praharaṇe | śeṣamuktam |

[p. b1005]

|| 5 || 1utpattyasaṃyogātpraṇītānāmājyavadvibhāgaḥ syāt || 14 ||
apaḥ praṇayatīti kāryadvayāsaṃbandhamapāṃ praṇayanamutpannam |
paścātkāryadvayaṃ śrutamityaviśeṣeṇa dvayoḥ prayoktṛtāṃ manyate | nanu
ca ‘apo ninayati’
 iti dvitīyā śrūyate | anīpsitatvāt
kārakamātre bhaviṣyatītyabhiprāyaḥ | atocyate---nānīpsitatve
heturasti | kṛtaprayojanamapi hi tat pratipādyatayā iṣyata eva |

[p. b1006]

na cepsite saṃbhavati anīpsitāśrayaṇamupapadyate tatpurassarā-
vagateḥ | tasmāt saṃyavane kṛtārthānāmapāṃ pratipattireva ninayanam |
iti siddhamaprayoktṛtvam |

|| 6 || 1prāsanavanmaitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍapradānaṃ kṛtārthatvāt || 16 ||
maitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍaṃ prayacchatīti pratipattirapāmiveti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
nanu ca karmaṇā yamabhipraiti sa sampradānamiti siddho guṇabhāvaḥ

[p. b1007]

sasaṃpradānake karmaṇi | sampradānagaprītyutpādakatayaiva karmaṇo
guṇabhāvaḥ na punaḥ karmāntarasādhyatayā karmabhāvānupapatteḥ | kathaṃ
tarhi ‘daṇḍī praiṣānanvāha’
 ityādidarśanam saṃpradāne yadṛcchayā
prāptasyāyamanuvādaḥ ityabhiprāyaḥ |

atrābhidhīyate---maitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍaṃ iti vyapadeśāt daṇḍī
praiṣānanvāha ityevamādi ca darśanāt | arthakarmaivedaṃ na pratipattiḥ |

[p. b1008]

nanu ca yadṛcchayā prāptasyāyamanuvāda ityuktam | ayameva doṣaḥ
pākṣikatvaprasaṅgāt | kathaṃ tarhi dvitīyā ? tathā yuktaṃ cānīpsita-
mityuktam | tadayuktamiva pratibhāti | anupādeyaṃ hi tatrodā-
haraṇam ahiṃ laṅghayati iti | tathā ca saktuṣu vibhaktivyatyaya
uktaḥ upādeyatvādanīpsitatvaṃ na bhavatīti | ucyate---suṣṭhūktaṃ
saktuṣu homārthaṃ tatropādānam | iha tu na prayacchatyarthamupādānaṃ
upāttameva hi prayacchatīti | atastathāyuktamevedaṃ karmeti śakyate
vaktum nepsitatamam | nāpi vibhaktyantarayogyam tasmādartha-
karmeti sūktam |

[p. b1009]

kiṃ punaratra dhāraṇameva prayujyate utotpattipīti cintāntara-
metat | asti tāvat prayoktṛtvamiti rāddhāntaḥ | adhikaraṇā-
ntaramuktam |

|| 8 || 1saumike ca kṛtārthatvāt || 20 ||
kaḥ punaratra prayājaśeṣeṇa viśeṣaḥ ? nanu ca prayājaśeṣeṇeti

[p. b1010]

prayājārthatā’vagamyate | somalipteneti karmārthatā(vagatiḥ) kimiti
na viśeṣaḥ | ucyate---somaliptenetyapi somayāgārthatayaiva soma-
saṃbandhaḥ | evaṃ tarhi avabhṛthaśruteḥ karmāntarayogaṃ somaliptasya
manvānaḥ śeṣabhāvaṃ manyate |

atrocyate---karmāntarayogepi nānyaśeṣo’nyaśeṣatayā
pratipattuṃ śakyate anyatra vyāpṛta ivānyatra | tasmātkarma-
śabdo lakṣaṇayaiva pratipattavyaḥ |

[p. b1011]

yantīti ca prakṛtyartho’ntarṇītaṇijartho vihito bhavati |
anyathā’nuvādamātraṃ dhātvarthaḥ syāt | tadidamuktam---“śrutiśca
vākyādbalīyasī”
 iti |

|| 9 || kartṛdeśakālānāmacodanaṃ prayoge nityasamavāyāt || 23 ||
nanu cānuvādakatvapakṣe apratipādakatvāt vākyānarthakyameva
prāpnoti | na brūmo viniyoge ānarthakyamiti | viniyoge itthameva
anvito vākyārthaḥ pratīyate | prayoktṛtvaṃ tu na saṃbhavati | kathaṃ
na saṃbhavati ? nityaprāptatvāt kārakagrāmasya | dravyasya hyatra niyamaḥ

[p. b1012]

kriyate na kārakāṇām | na cākārakasya prayoktṛtvaṃ
saṃbhavati | tasmādviniyoga evāyaṃ(niyama) viṣayaviśeṣo’vagamyate
na prayoktṛvyāpāra iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

atrocyate---yadi dravyādanyat kārakatayā’vagamyate
tato yathā bhavānāha | iha tu dravyameva kāraka-
tayā’vagamyate | tasyābhidhānādvibhaktyutpatteḥ |

[p. b1013]

nanu ca kārakaṃ hi kriyāprayuktyaiva prayujyate | na ca kārakaśūnyaṃ
karmāsti | satyaṃ na kārakaśūnyaṃ karma na punaḥ sāmānyaṃ kiñcidasti
kārakaṃ nāma | yadeva hi vibhaktyā dravyaṃ guṇo vā samarpyate
tadeva kārakam | tasmādviniyogāt prayoktṛtvepi niyamaḥ
saṃbhavati | uktaṃ ca viniyogānusārī niyogaḥ sati saṃbhave
iti |

|| 10 || 1tathā dravyeṣu guṇaśrutirutpattisaṃyogāt || 25 ||
yuktaṃ tāvat dravyasyopādānaṃ vibhaktibhistasya kārakatayā-

[p. b1014]

’vagateḥ guṇastu tadviśeṣaṇamātrameva śvetādiṣu | yatra punararuṇayeti
vibhaktyaiva kārakatvenāvagatiḥ tatra pūrvādhikaraṇenaivāvagatam | ato
dravyopādānārthatvāt guṇajātyoravivakṣeti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |
atrocyate---satyaṃ dravyopādānārthamidam | tadeva tu dravyaṃ tadrahitaṃ
na śakyate tadviśiṣṭamupādātumiti guṇajātyorapi dravyavadupādāna
siddhiḥ |

|| 11 || 1saṃskāre ca tatpradhānatvāt || 26 ||
saṃskāre punaḥ ko viśeṣaḥ ? ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ | adhikāraśūnyo
hi saṃskāravidhirbhavati saṃskāryārthatvāt saṃskārasya, nātrādhikārārthaṃ

[p. b1015]

karmāvagamyate | tasmādavivakṣaiveti pūrvābhyāṃ viśeṣaḥ | atrocyate
---satyamadhikāro nāvagamyate | adhikṛtadravyārthatayā tu pratīyate |
tataḥ kim ? idaṃ tato bhavati ? saṃskāraviśiṣṭadravyaṃ adhikāra-
vidhiviśeṣaṇatayā’vagamyate | tataśca guṇavat tadapi adhikāra-
vidhinaivopādīyata iti tadvadityucyate | tatpradarśitamevaṃ satyaṅga-
vidhiranuvāda iti |

|| 12 || 1yajati codanādravyadevatākriyaṃ samudāye kṛtārthatvāt || 27 ||

[p. b1016]

ke punaste’dhikāraviṣayāḥ ? yāgadānahomāḥ | “svargakāmo
yajeta,”
 “āyuṣkāmo hiraṇyaṃ dadyāt”
 saubhāgyakāmo juhuyāt”
ityevamādayaḥ | etaccopalakṣaṇamātram | anyepi ye kāmaśruti-
saṃyuktāḥ | kaḥ punastyāgātmakatve sati yāgadānahomānāṃ parasparaṃ
viśeṣa ityāśaṅkānivṛttyarthaṃ svarūpavarṇanamityuktam |

[p. b1017]

[p. b1018]

|| 14 || vidheḥ karmāpavargitvādarthāntare vidhipradeśaḥ syāt || 29 ||
kaḥ punarasyopādānalakṣaṇena saṃbandhaḥ ? eṣa saṃbandhaḥ | kimu-
pāttasyātithyārthaṃ dhāraṇamātraṃ dvābhyāṃ prayujyate, uta niriṣṭakasya
punarupādānam, uta pṛthagevopādānaṃ, dharmamātrātideśakamidaṃ
vacanaṃ, uta sādhāraṇametadevopādānamiti | anyaśeṣasyānyatra-

[p. b1019]

viniyogo nāsti[yathā] anyatra vyāpṛtasyaivānyatra vyāpāraḥ
ityuktam | viniyogānusārī co(no)pādānamityetadapyuktam |
tasmānnātithyopāttasya upasadagnīṣomābhyāṃ dhāraṇaṃ prayujyata iti
ghaṭate | niriṣṭakopadeśaśca śiṣṭācāravirodhānna bhavati | lakṣaṇā-
śrayaṇaṃ ca nopapadyate | evaṃ dharmātideśo’pi lakṣaṇābhayādeva
tyajyate | tasmātsādhāraṇāvagatireva śrutyopapannā bhavatīti
sādhāraṇamupādānamiti siddham |

iti bṛhatyāṃ caturthādhyāyasya
dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b1020]

caturthādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1dravyāsaṃskārakarmasu parārthatvāt phalaśrutirarthavādaḥ
syāt || 1 ||
uktamadhyāyādau sarvatra niyogavyāpāra eva cintyata iti | ihe-
dānīṃ kiṃ phalasaṃbandhitayā puruṣasya vidhivyāpārassaṃbhavati neti ci-
ntyate | puruṣaphalasaṃbandhāsaṃbhave kathaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ saṃbhavati iti pramāṇa-
lakṣaṇe cintitam | yo’pi tatra pūrvapakṣaḥ asāvapi kriyākāraka-
saṃbandho’vagamyamānaḥ kathaṃ kriyāntaraviṣayaṃ niyogaṃ pratipādayatī

[p. b1021]

tyasyārthasya nirākaraṇāya | iha tu svaviṣayo niyogo na saṃbhavatīti
cintā | kathaṃ ? yasyetyevamādīnāmadhikāriviśeṣaṇatayā’saṃbhavāt |
kathamasaṃbhavaḥ ? tatsaṃbandhasya niyogasyānupapatteḥ kimiti ? siddha-
vadupadeśāt | yathā devadattaḥ odanaṃ pacati iti puruṣopalakṣaṇatā
pratīyate | na puruṣeṇodanaḥ paktavya iti | nāyaṃ śabdārthaḥ yasya
parṇamayī juhūrbhavati na sa pāpaṃ ślokaṃ śṛṇoti iti | pratyayāntarā-

[p. b1022]

vagamyatvādasyārthasya | na ca tatpratyayāntaramasti prathamalakṣaṇokta
evāyamartha iti sthitaṃ evaṃbhūtānāmarthavādatvamiti |

|| 2 || 1naimittike vikāratvāt kratu्pradhānamanyat syāt || 4 ||
kathaṃ punaratra dvayarthaṃ vākyamupanyasyate | yatrāpi tāvadarthadvaye
viśeṣo nāvagamyate tatrāpyekārthenaiva vākyena bhavitavyam | prakaraṇā-
dibhistu viśeṣo’vagantavyaḥ | kiṃ punaratra viśeṣa evāvagamyate |

[p. b1023]

dvayarthatā cābhidhānasya pramāṇalakṣaṇa eva nirākṛtā | tasmāta-
saṃbhavātpūrvapakṣamevedamadhikaraṇaṃ pratibhāti |

atrocyate---evamevaitadekārthatvameva anvitābhidhāne | prayojana-
dvayaṃ tu atra saṃbhavatīti kratvarthatāmapi pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | saṃbandhā-
nusāri copādānamityuktam | nanu ca viniyogānusārītyuktaṃ na saṃba-
ndhānusārī | naivaṃ, viniyogo hi saṃbandhahetutayoktaḥ, na punarviniyoga-

[p. b1024]

taivāśrīyate | tasmāt dvayamupādāpakaṃ, saṃbandhadvayāvagamāt
na kriyāsaṃbandhatyāgena dravyādeḥ puruṣārthatā’vasīyate |
atrābhidhīyate | naitadyuktaṃ yaduktaṃ saṃbandhamātrānusāryu-
pādānamiti śabdopapāditasaṃbandhānusāryupādānaṃ na saṃbandhānu-
sārī śabdācca puruṣārthatayaivānvayo’vagamyate | nanu kriyayāpya-
nvayo’vagamyate | satyam gamyate puruṣārthaupayikatayā |

[p. b1025]

tasmāt tadevopādāpakaṃ yadarthamevānvayāvagamaḥ | yatpunaridaṃ bhāṣya-
kāreṇoktaṃ viśeṣānavagamāt dviṣṭhaṃ vākyamalambusānāṃ 1yāteti
tat prakaraṇādyasaṃbhave anirṇayāyaiva na punaḥ śabdāt dvaya
avagantuṃ śakyate | iha tu viśeṣāvaṇamānnānirṇaya ityarthaḥ |
nanu na kiñcidvihitamastīti paricodya sāmānyavidhānopanyāsaḥ
sāmasādhyastutyapekṣayā nābhivartaparatayetyadoṣaḥ |

[p. b1026]

|| 3 || 1ekasya tūbhayatve saṃyogapṛthaktvam || 5 ||
kaḥ punaḥ pūrvaparkṣiṇa āśaṅkāhetuḥ ? ayamāśaṅkāhetuḥ yathā
‘agnihotraṃ juhoti’
 ‘agnihotraṃ juhuyāt svargakāma’
 iti ca na pṛthak
prayoktṛtvamasti svargakāmapadānvayepi homānvayāvagateḥ | evamatrāpi
‘dadhnā juhoti’
 ‘dadhnendriyakāmasya juhuyāt’
 iti kāmasaṃyogepi
dadhihomāvagateḥ puruṣārthasyaiva prayoktṛtvaṃ manyate[pūrvavādī]evaṃ prāpte-
’bhidhīyate | naitadyuktam kratorapyatra prayoktṛtvamavasīyate kraturapi
hi sādhikāraḥ | na guṇaphalasaṃbandhaḥ āśraye aidamarthyamapekṣata

[p. b1027]

ityuktam | tasmāt ‘dadhnā juhoti’
 iti homārtha evāyaṃ
viniyogaḥ ‘dadhnendriyakāmasya’
 ityeva puruṣārthaḥ | yaduktaṃ ‘agnihotraṃ
juhoti’
 ‘agnihotraṃ juhuyāt svargakāmaḥ’
 ityekaḥ prayokteti yuktaṃ
adhikārāntarāśravaṇāt upādāturevādhikārakalpanā | iha tu
adhikāradvayaṃ nityaṃ naimittikaṃ cāstīti yukto viśeṣaḥ |

|| 4 || 1dravyāṇāṃ tu kiyārthānāṃ saṃskāraḥ kratudharmaḥ syāt || 8 ||
iha tu prāṇadhāraṇārthatvādvratasya puruṣārthatāṃ manyate pūrvapakṣa-
vādī niyamasyaiva prāṅmukho’nnānibhuñjīta iti vat pratigraha-
niyamavacca |

[p. b1028]

atrocyate---nāyaṃ niyamaniyogo’dhikārakalpanāṃ sahate |
sannihitaśca kratvadhikāraḥ | sākṣāt śrutādhikārasannidhau yadaśrūya-
māṇādhikāraṃ tata tadarthamevābhidhānena pratibhāti | tādarthyenaiva ca
niyamasya niyojyasaṃbandhalābhāt nādhikārāntarāpekṣā’sti | yaduktaṃ
pratigrahādiniyamavat bhavitumarhatīti | yuktaṃ tatrādhikārāśravaṇāt
iha punaḥ tadviparītaṃ iti darśitam | ataḥ kratuprayojya eva dravya-
niyama iti sthitam | kratvadhikāraprayojyamityuktaṃ bhavati |

|| 5 || 1codanāyāṃ phalāśruteḥ karmamātraṃ vidhīyeta na hyaśabdaṃ
pratīyeta || 10 ||

[p. b1029]

ihādhikāraśūnyā vidhaya udāharaṇam | kiṃ teṣāmadhikāra-
kalpanā uta tāvatyeva paryavasānamiti saṃśayaḥ | nanu ca ‘saphalaṃ’

‘aphalaṃ’
 ityatra sandeho bhāṣye śrūyate | ucyate---adhikārādṛte
phalaṃ nāstīti svargakāmādhikaraṇe ‘tasmātsiddho’dhikāra’
 iti vadatā
pradarśitam | anyathā hi siddhaṃ svargasya prādhānyamityupasaṃhāraḥ
syāt | tasmāta sādhikāraniradhikāracintaiveyam |

kiṃ tāvatprāptam ? yāvadvacanaṃ vacanamiti kartavya-
tāparyavasāyyeva niyogo nādhikāraparyanto bhavitumarhati |
nanu ca niyoga evādhikārādṛte na nirvartyate |

[p. b1030]

kena vā uktamadhikārādṛte na niyogo niṣpadyata
iti adhikṛtasya niyogaḥ na punaḥ niyogata evādhi-
kāraḥ | nanu cādhikārādṛte’nanuṣṭheyatvāt niyogāvagati-
rbādhyeta | nāvagateḥ kaścidbādhaḥ | anadhikṛtāstu na kurvate
kimāyātamavagateḥ | taduktaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa ‘kṣemyo’yaṃ panthāḥ gacchatu
bhavān anena’
 iti | tasmānnātra kasyacidadhikāraṃ paśyāmaḥ |
adhyāhriyamāṇepyadhikāre kasyādhikāro’dhyāhriyeta yasmānna niyogā-

[p. b1031]

dviśeṣākṣepaḥ saṃbhavati | na cāparipūrṇa vākyamityatra pramāṇamasti |
‘paṭobhavati’
 ityetāvadeva vākyam | ‘raktaḥ paṭobhavati’

ityapi bhavati | tasmāt padāntaraśravaṇanibandhana eva
vākyeṣu arthāntarāvagamaḥ | na punaḥ aśrūyamāṇa evārthāntara-
kalpanāpramāṇamasti | kathaṃ tarhi ‘vasantāya kapiñjalānālabhate’

grīṣmāya kalaviṅkān iti agṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāditi tatro-
ktam | loke tarhi kathaṃ ‘dvāraṃ’
 ‘dvāra’
 mityevamādiṣu |

[p. b1032]

yuktaṃ kārakāṇāṃ kriyāpekṣatvāt na punarniyogo’dhikārama-
pekṣata ityuktam | tasmādyāvadvacanaṃ vacanaṃ iti | atrā-
bhidhīyate---kārakamiva kriyāpekṣaṃ niyogamapyadhikārāpekṣaṃ
vadāmaḥ | kutaḥ ? loke tathā darśanāt devadattagāmabhyāja śuklāṃ iti |
na hi anapekṣitaviśeṣaṃ loke vaktāro bhavanti iti devadattaṃ
yajñadattaṃ vā’dhikṛtya liṅādayaḥ prayojyāḥ | nanu ca devadattādayaḥ
kartṛviśeṣaṇam | yathā na kartṛviśeṣaṇaṃ tathā ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ |

[p. b1033]

adhikārāddhi kartṛtvaṃ sthāsyati na kartṛtvādadhikāra-
tasmāt śrutyekadeśaḥ sa ucyate---yatpunaratra bhāṣyakāreṇoktaṃ
“evaṃ laukiko’dhyāhāraḥ prāpnoti”
 iti parihṛtaṃ ca “vaidikamevādhyāha-
riṣyāma”
 iti, vyavadhānaṃ cāśaṅkya ‘api paśyasi’
 iti dṛṣṭāntā-
bhidhānaṃ sarvamidaṃ asamañjasamiva pratibhāti yanniyogādavagamyate
kathaṃ tadavaidikaṃ ? vyavadhāne ca kathamanuṣaṅga ? yadapyuktaṃ ‘api
paśyasi dhāvantaṃ’
 iti | asaṃbandhipadavyavāyāt naivāyaṃ vyavāyaḥ
yogyapadasaṃbaddhaṃ vyavahitamityucyate | atrocyate---vaidikamevādhyā-
hariṣyāmaḥ iti | etaduktaṃ bhavati | na kārakatayā’dhyāhriyate |

[p. b1034]

kathaṃ tarhi ? adhikārimukhenaiveti | kārakatayā hi phalasaṃbandho
laukiko bhavati pramāṇāntarāpekṣatvāt dṛṣṭānto’pi yathā na
padavyavadhānamātrādvayavahitaṃ bhavati kintu yogyapadasaṃbaddhaṃ vākyā-
ntaravyavahitaṃ vyavahitaṃ bhavati | tasmānnādhikārādhyāhāre vya-
vadhānam | api paśyasīti ca sūktam | ato’śrūyamāṇādhi-
kārāṇyapi sādhikārāṇīti siddham |

|| 6 || 1tatsarvārthamaviśeṣāt sa svargaḥ sarvān pratyaviśiṣṭa-
tvāt || 13, 15 ||
sthitaṃ niyogo nādhikāraśūnya iti | kenādhi-

[p. b1035]

kāreṇeti na jñāyate | ato’gṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvāt sarvārthibhiḥ
saṃbadhyata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | atrocyate---bahubhiḥ saṃbandhe niyatamekena
saṃbandhaḥ bhavati | ekena cedupapanno niyogaḥ nādhikārāntarasaṃbandhe
pramāṇamasti | nanu cāgṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvānnāsau nirūpayituṃ śakyata
ityuktam | satyaṃ yadi na gṛhyate viśeṣaḥ | iha tu sarvān pratyaviśi-
ṣṭatvāt svargakāmasyaivādhikāro’trāvagamyate | sāmānyakalpanāyāṃ hi
viśeṣāvagamahetvabhāvāt | tadidamuktaṃ---“sa svargaḥ syāt sarvān

[p. b1036]

pratyaviśiṣṭatvāt”
 iti |

|| 8 || kratau phalārthavādamaṅgavat kārṣṇājiniḥ || 17 ||
sthitamaśrūyamāṇādhikāreṣu sarvān pratyaviśiṣṭatvāt svargo-
’dhikāravidhiheturiti | yatra punaḥ viśeṣābhidhāyino’rthavādāḥ
śrūyante yatra saṃśayaḥ---kiṃ atrāpi svarga evādhikārahetuḥ | atha
vā’rthavādābhihitaṃ vastviti | pūrvapakṣavādī niyogādyadavagamyate
tadeva yuktamiti manyate | arthavādastvarthavāda eva |

[p. b1037]

atrocyate---arthavādābhihitameva phalaṃ yuktam | kutaḥ ?
evamarthastutyaiva stutyartha upapanno bhavati | nanu ca niyogāt
svargārthataivāvagamyata ityuktam | sandigdhe ca vākyaśeṣānnirṇayaḥ
uktaḥ | atrocyate---satyaṃ saṃśayo nāsti | niścayo’pi naiva
vidyate | ataśca vākyaśeṣādyo’rtho’vagamyate sa eva
vākyārthaḥ | na cāpratipanne vākyārthe kevalānniyogāt
kiñcitpratipadyate | tasmātsūktamarthavādābhihitamevādhikāraheturiti |

[p. b1038]

yatpunarbhāṣyakāreṇoktaṃ---vibhaktimātraṃ kalpyate sa sahadhātvarthena
prayogavacano vidhāsyatīti | pakṣe kārye kāraṇaviśeṣakalpanā[nāya]
na khedanīyaṃ mana ityabhiprāyaḥ |

|| 9 || 1kāmye karmaṇi nityaḥ svargo yathā yajñāṅge kratvarthaḥ || 20 ||
idamidānīṃ cintyate---yadi punarniyogāt svargādhikāraḥ |
padāntarayogāccopādhayaḥ kalpyante śākamapyāharetivat ko doṣa

[p. b1039]

syāt | na khalu kaściddoṣaḥ | kintu aniścitāt sāmānyādhikārāt
viśeṣa upādhiriti tu duśśakaṃ vaktum | kāṣṭhasaṃyuktamiva śākā-
haraṇam |

|| 10 || 1sārvakāmyamaṅgakāmaiḥ prakaraṇāt || 21 ||
sarvebhyaḥ kāmebhyo darśapūrṇamāsāviti kimayamanuvādaḥ | atha-
vā’yamapi vidhiriti prāptārthatvādanuvāda iti manyate | kathaṃ
prāptiḥ ? kiñcidaṅgadvāreṇa kiñcitsvata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. b1040]

nanu pradhānasaṃbandhitayaivaitacchrūyate kathamaṅgāpekṣo’nuvādaḥ | aṅga-
dvāramapi hi pradhānena vyapadiśyate yūpavat |

atrābhidhīyate---pramāṇāntarāvagamyamidaṃ yadutānyasaṃbandhī
anyena vyapadiśyata iti | siddhe cānuvāde pramāṇāntaragamyatā,
pramāṇāntaragamyatāyāṃ cānuvāda itītaretarāśrayatvam | tasmānnira-
pekṣācchabdānniyoga eveti manyāmahe | yūpe punararthāntaravidheyatvāt
pramāṇāntaraprameyatā yuktā |

|| 11 || 1tatra sarve’viśeṣāt || 17 ||

[p. b1041]

idamidānīṃ cintyate---sakṛtsarvakābhāvāptiḥ uta yāvatkāmaṃ
prayoga iti | sarvakāmanimittatayā śravaṇāt na prayoge viśeṣā-
vadhāraṇā śakyā | ekatvāditi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | nanu ca sarvakāmo nāma
naiva kaścidastyadhikārī anadhikārācca phalotpatterasaṃbhavaḥ | ucyate
---yadi kāmanā aṅgaṃ syāt anutpannā satī nāṅgabhāva upapadyata iti
bhavati kāmanānāmanutpādaḥ sakṛtprayoge sarvaphalānutpattau hetuḥ |

[p. b1042]

iha tu sarvakāmairyuktasya kāmino’dhikāra iti na prayoga-
kāle kāmanotpāda upayujyate | tasmānnanimitte sati naimitti-
kasya vyāghāto bhavitu marhati | atrocyate---yāvatkāmaṃ
prayoga iti | evamadhikārasiddhirupapannā bhavati | na hi
akāminaḥ phalādhikāro’stīti ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | nanu
ca prayogakāle kāmanotpādo’naṅgatvānnopayujyata ityuktam |

[p. b1043]

kena voktaṃ prayogakāle utpādo heturiti | adhikārāvagatāveva
sarvakāmitā yugapanna saṃbhavatītyuktamasmābhiḥ | asaṃbhave ca yugapat
sarvaniyogānupapattiḥ | tasmāt pratikāmamadhikāraḥ | yadvā
yathoddiṣṭakāmābhiprāyamiti bhāṣyakāreṇoktam anirdiṣṭānāmava-
gamābhāva ityabhiprāyaḥ | ato nirdiṣṭāpekṣī sarvaśabdaḥ sarvaṃ
kṛtamitivat | ataḥ siddhaṃ pratikāmaṃ prayoga iti | ata eva
ca viruddhānāmapi phalānāmadhikāraḥ siddhobhavati | sakṛtprayoge
hi maraṇāyuṣoranutpattiḥ syāt | tasmādyogasiddhirveti
sūktam |

[p. b1044]

 evaṃ vā ||

asminnadhikaraṇe niyogasya phalaṃ prati kiṃ śarīragrahaṇapūrvakaḥ
prayojakabhāvaḥ uta yāvacchrutaṃ prayoktṛtvamiti | tata pūrvapakṣavādī
śarīragrahaṇasya kāraṇāntarādarśanāt paśvādīnāṃ ca kāraṇāntareṇo-
papatteḥ śarīragrahaṇapūrvakaḥ phalākṣepa iti manyate | paśvādīnāṃ kāraṇaṃ
pratigrahādi pratyakṣaṃ bhaviṣyatīti | rāddhāntavādī tu yāvacchrutaṃ prayo-
ktṛtvaṃ manyate | kasmāt ? kevalaśravaṇāvagamyatvāt prayoktṛbhāvasya |

[p. b1045]

sarvāviśeṣāt | na hi gamyate viśeṣaḥ ihaiva amutraiva veti
kāmaśrutisāmānyāt |

atra bhāṣyakāro’nantarādarśanamupanyasya pratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ
nopalabhāmahe śabdādavagamyata ityuktavān | tadayuktaṃ, śabdādapi
hi nānantaryamupalabhyata ityuktam | ayamabhiprāyaḥ bhāṣyakārasya
---sāmānyāvagamāt ihāmuṣminniti ca viśeṣānavagamāt śabdato
na pramāṇāntarābhāvaḥ ānantaryaṃ vihantīti | liṅgamuktam |

|| 12 || 1samavāye codanāsaṃyogasyārthavattvāt || 19 ||
vājapeyeneṣṭvā bṛhaspatisavena yajeta’ iti lakṣaṇe ktyāpratya-

[p. 1046]

yotpattau kālaśravaṇāt kālārthaḥ saṃyoga iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |
samānakartṛkyordhātvarthayoḥ anvayāvagateḥ dhātusaṃbandha evāyam |
sa ca aṅgāṅgibhāvādṛte na saṃbhavatīti aṅgāṅgitaiveti rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 13 || 1ubhayārthamiti cet || 32 ||
paurṇamāsīṃ saṃsthāpyeti parisamāptivacanatvāt saṃsthāśabdasya
nāṅgāṅgibhāvaḥ paurṇamāsyāṃ saṃbhavatīti prakaraṇāt darśapūrṇamāsāṅgataiva
vaimṛdhasyeti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. 1047]

atrābhidhīyate---yadi samāptivacanatvāt saṃsthāśabdasya
nāṅgitvaṃ paurṇamāsyāḥ prakaraṇādapi naivāṅgitvaṃ saṃbhavati | atha
saṃsthāpyeti pūrvavihitāṅgakalāpaparisamāptyabhiprāyo’yaṃ saṃsthā-
śabdaḥ evaṃ tarhi vākyāt paurṇamāsyaṅgataiva vaimṛdhasya | na ca
niyogaikye kaścidviśeṣaḥ asyāpyadhikārasaṃbandhāt | atassiddhaṃ
paurṇamāsyaṅgaṃ vaimṛdha iti |

|| 14 || anutpattau tu kālaḥ syāt prayojanena sambandhāt || 36 ||

[p. 1048]

nirjñātaparāṅgabhāvayoḥ saṃbandhe niyogaḥ kālavidhānaparyavasāyyeva
karmaṇaḥ pratyabhijñānāt tasmāt kālārtho niyogaḥ |

|| 15 || utpattikālaviśaye kālaḥ syādvākyasya tatpradhāna-
tvāt || 37 ||
darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiṣṭvā somena yajena’ ityatrākarmavidhānāt
kālārtho niyoga ityuktam | tatpunaridamayuktam | vājapeyeneṣṭvā
bṛhaspatisavena yajetetyanenāviśeṣāt tenātrāpi kālārthena niyogena
bhavitavyam | ubhayatra vā karmārthatvaṃ niyogasya | vaktavyo vā

[p. 1049]

viśeṣaḥ | atra kaiścidanārabhya vidhānaṃ viśeṣa uktaḥ | kaḥ punaranāra-
bhyavidhānena viśeṣa upalabhyate ? ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ---karmadvayavidhānaṃ
tāvanna saṃbhavati aneneṣṭvā anena yajetetyevamādiṣu | anārabhya-
vidhāne ca naikamapi karma prakṛtamasti | tasmānnāmataḥ prasiddha-
karmopādānaṃ kālaniyogārtham | uktaṃ ca nāmadheyamapi guṇaphalopabandhe-
nārthavat iti | vājapeye punaḥ prakṛtatvādvājapeyasya karmāntara-
vidhānaṃ śrutyopapadyate | nāma ca taddharmātideśārthaṃ bhaviṣyati
prakaraṇāntare prayojanānyatvamiti nyāyāt | anārabhyavidhāne
punaḥ prakaraṇāntarābhāvāt dvayorapi (akarmārthatvaṃ yuktam)

[p. 1050]

karmāntaratvamayuktam | yeṣāṃ punaḥ darśapūrṇamāsaprakaraṇa evaitatpaṭhyate
tatra kā kathā bhavatu | tatrāṅgāṅgibhāvaḥ kā no bādhā ? atha vā pari-
hṛtamevaitat śākhāntarādhikaraṇe sarvaśākhāpratyayamekaṃ karmeti vadatā |
kathaṃ parihṛtaṃ ? tadevedaṃ śāstramiti yuktam | bhavatvevaṃ tathāpi na
vidmaḥ kiṃ anārabhyavādasya yo’rthaḥ tena śāstraṃ pratyabhijñāyatāṃ uta
prakaraṇe’pi yo’rthaḥ iti | avirodhādanārabhyavādena pratyabhijñānaṃ bheda-
vido manyante | tasmātsūktamiha kalārthaḥ saṃyoga iti | darśana-
muktam |

[p. 1051]

|| 16 || phalasaṃyogastvacodite na syādaśeṣabhūtatvāt || 38 ||
atra saṃśayaḥ kiṃ yasya niyogaḥ tasya phalaṃ uta anyasya
niyogo’nyasya phalamiti | adhikārī ca niyujyate | yadarthaṃ
karma so’dhikārī sa eva niyujyate | yadarthaṃ karma so’dhikārī sa
eva niyujyate | anadhikāre ca pituḥ nāsya niyojyatā syāt |
tasmādbhavati saṃśayaḥ | kiṃ yuktam ? niyojyasya pituḥ phalamiti |
kutaḥ ? adhikārasidhyartho niyogaḥ na saṃbandhamātrāvagamyaḥ
tasmāt pituridaṃ phalaṃ putraphalānvayasya stutyā’nvayo bhavatu |
lakṣaṇayā’dhikṛtaviśeṣaṇam | tathā ca kratusaṃbandhinimitte

[p. 1052]

niyojyasyaiva phalaṃ na kratunā saṃbadhyate | atrocyate
---yasya phalānvayaḥ tasyaiva phalaṃ nimittatayāpyadhikāra-
siddheḥ | itarasya putrajanmanā nimittenāpi puruṣārthatā’stī-
tyadhikārasiddhiḥ | kathaṃ tarhi phalapadaṃ ? na karma viśeṣaṇaṃ, nāpyadhi-
kāropanipāti | ucyate---bhaviṣyatyadhikāropanipāti | katha-
mevaṃvidhasya putrajanmano’dhikāriviśeṣaṇatvena śravaṇāt | tasmādyasya
niyogastasyaiva phalamiti aviruddhametat prītyāpyadhikārasiddheḥ |
yaduktaṃ kratusaṃbandhe niyojyasyaiva phalamiti, yuktamadhikārānu-

[p. 1053]

praveśānnimittasyeti ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ |

evaṃ vā ||

‘putre jāte’
 iti śrūyate | tatra saṃśayaḥ---kiṃ jananānantarameva
naimittikaṃ bhavatu utāṅgopasaṃhāramapekṣate prakṛtivadabhiprāptamiti |
yadyevaṃ kimiti kumārasya prāṇadhāraṇamutkarṣahetutvenopanyasyate |
ekamevedamadhikaraṇamastu | sāṅgasya naimittikatvāt na nimittāna-
ntaraṃ prayogaḥ saṃbhavati iti svakāla eva kartavyaḥ | na cānantarye-
’sati nimittatvavyāghātaḥ bhūtapratyayayogānnimittasya | na ca
pratīkṣaṇe bhūtatvamapaiti | tasmāt pṛthagadhikaraṇaṃ niṣprayojanamiva
pratibhāti |

[p. 1054]

atrābhidhīyate---nimittānantaraṃ naimittikamityautsargiko
nyāyaḥ na kāraṇādṛte kālotkarṣo’vagamyate | nanu ca kāraṇamuktaṃ
sāṅgasya naimittikatvam | etadeva na vijānīmaḥ kiṃ nimittakāle
niyogāvagamāt viniyogāvagamyamaṅgaṃ tyajyatāṃ uta niyogakāla-
syotkarṣa iti | niyogārthatvādviniyogasyāṅgatyāgaṃ viniyoga-
vido manyante | yathā rajasvalādisparśanena pūrvāhṇādikālaḥ vihita
upekṣyate | ata etadbādhanārthaṃ pūrvamadhikaraṇam | yadi hi
niyogakāla evānuṣṭhānaṃ kriyate yadartho niyogastadeva hīyate |

[p. 1055]

tasmānniyogakālāvagatirevotkraṣṭavyā | utkṛṣṭā cet svakāla
eva syānnāṅgatyāge pramāṇamastīti dvitīyamadhikaraṇaṃ
likhitam |

|| 17 || pradhānenābhisaṃyogādaṅgānāṃ mukhyakālatvam || 40 ||
‘vājapeyeneṣṭvā bṛhaspatisavena yajeta’
 ityatra
saṃśayaḥ---kimeko niyogaḥ uta niyogadvayamiti | nanu
niyogadvitve’ṅgāṅgibhāvo nopapadyate | asti cāṅgāṅgitvaṃ,
tasmānna prayogabhedaḥ | bāḍhamupapadyate | viniyogādaṅgāṅgitā

[p. 1056]

prayogānyatvena viniyogānyatvam | nanu ca niyogānyatve śāstra-
bhedaḥ prāpnoti | bhavatu prayogāpekṣayā na kaściddoṣaḥ | viniyogā-
pekṣayā tvekamevedaṃ śāstram | kathaṃ punarekatve śāstrasya prayogabhedaḥ ?
kena vāryate vidhānabhedavat anubandhanimittakam | yadyathaivānu-
badhyate tat tathaiva tantraṃ bhinatti | iha tu prayogānubandhabhedāta
prayogabhedaḥ prāpnoti | bhavatu prayogabhedāpekṣayā na doṣaḥ |
tasmānna śāstrāntarāpattiḥ kevalaṃ prayogabhedaḥ | yathā ca
prayogabhedastathā darśitam |

iti bṛhatyāṃ caturthādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. 1057]

caturthādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1prakaraṇaśabdasāmānyāccodanānāmanaṅgatvam || 1 ||
nanu ca nāmadheyavicāre yajisāmānādhikaraṇyānnāma bhavatī-
tyuktam | kathaṃ punarayajyarthasyāpyetadabhidhānaṃ śaṅkyate ?
ataḥ pūrvapakṣa evāyaṃ nopapadyate | ucyate---iha tu
niyogaikyādyugapadabhidhānaṃ dhātvarthasya | niyogaikyaṃ ca
prakaraṇāyattamiti sarvaprakṛtābhidhāyī dhāturiti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate |

[p. 1058]

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---satyaṃ prakaraṇānniyogaikyaṃ na punaḥ śrutānu-
bandhaparityāge kāraṇamasti | kathaṃ ? itikartavyatāpi hi
niyogaikyaṃ naiva viruṇaddhi | tathābhūtopadeśāditi vakṣyāmaḥ |
ato yāgavyatiriktamaṅgamiti siddham |

|| 2 || 1madhyasthaṃ yasya tanmadhye || 2 ||
nanu cātrāpi kramāt prakaraṇa balīya ityetadevodāhṛtam |

[p. 1059]

sthitaṃ ca sarvārthatvaṃ tṛtīye | ucyate---satyaṃ viniyogāt sthitam |
idānīṃ tu pṛthak prayojyatvādabhiṣecanīyaniyogasya tasya ca
sannidhānāt tenaivaikya yuktamiti manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | na viniyoga-
virodhaḥ ; pratyekasaṃbandhahetutvāta prakaraṇasya | ataḥ sannidhi-
viśeṣasyātikramo nopapadyate | atrocyate---sthitamaikaśabdyamadhi-
kāravidheḥ | tataḥ kiṃ ? idaṃ tato bhavati | yasyaiva prayogānubandha-
bhedaḥ tasyaiva pṛthak prayogo’vasīyate svenetikartavyatā-
kālāpena | yasya punaraṅgasyādhikāravidhireva prayogaṃ jñāpayati
na tasyāvāntaraprayogaḥ śakyate jñāpayitum | tasmāt
kramāt taddeśasyaiva anuṣṭhānamadhikāravidhisamadhigamyameva |

[p. 1060]

prakaraṇāvirodhastvayaivoktaḥ |

|| 3 || 1prakaraṇāvibhāge ca vipratiṣiddhaṃ hyubhayam || 5 ||
purastādupasadāṃ saumyena pracarantīti | kaḥ khalvāgnimārutā-
dūrdhvamanūyājaiścarantītyanenāsya viśeṣaḥ | na khalu kaścidviśeṣaḥ |
kimityadhikaraṇāntaraṃ saumyādīnāṃ anyadadhikaraṇāntaram | kā no
bādhā ? | na khalu kaścidbhavato bādhā | adhikaraṇānarthakyaṃ tu
bhavati | ayaṃ tarhi viśeṣaḥ | prakaraṇāt sarvasya prādhānyaṃ iti-
kartavyatātvaṃ vā rājasūye | asaṃyuktaṃ ca prakaraṇādityuktam |

[p. 1061]

tasmādvākyādupasadaṅgameveti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ | api ca
sannidhānaviśeṣeṇopasadaṅgataiva yuktā, adhikāraniyogāt |

atrābhidhīyate---na tāvadupasadāṃ prakaraṇamasti adhikāra-
nibandhanatvāt prakaraṇasya | nanu ca vākyādupasadaṅgatvamityuktam
nobhayapadasaṃbandhābhāvāt vākyaṃ saṃbhavati | tasmāt
purastādupasadāmityeva saṃbandhaḥ | atha kasmādupasadāṃ saumyenetyeṣa
saṃbandhaḥ na bhavati | purastādityananvayaḥ prāpnoti

[p. 1062]

purastāditi saṃbandhe kālavidhānāt saumyena carantītyaviruddham |
tasmādidamapi āgnimārutādityanenāviśiṣṭam |

|| 4 || 1phalavadvoktahetutvāditarasya pradhāna syāt || 7 ||
sāṅgrahaṇyāṃ tu phalavatsannidhau aphalaṃ tadaṅgamiti siddhepyanu-
ṣaṅganivṛttyarthamidamadhikaraṇam | darśitaṃ ca |

[p. 1063]

|| 5 || 1dadhigraho naimittikaḥ śrutisaṃyogāt || 8 ||
anityadoṣanirharaṇārthasaṅkīrtanāt naimittikaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣa-
vādī | khadiravadubhayārthatāmācāryadeśīyaḥ | nityatve sati jyeṣṭha-
śabdopapattiriti manyate | rāddhāntastu nimittasaṃyogābhāvāt
na doṣanirharaṇārthatayā naimittikatvamasya śakyate kalpayitum |
kimiti na śakyate naimittikadoṣanirharaṇānyathānupapattyā
tasyāpi naimittikatvaṃ bhavitumarhati | satyaṃ yadyanyathā
nopapadyate | arthavādatayā tūpapadyata eveti na kalpanāmarhati |

[p. 1064]

sārūpyādyuktam |

|| 6 || vaiśvānaraśca nityaḥ syānnityaiḥ samānasaṅkhyatvāt || 12 ||
anityadoṣanirharaṇārthavādatayāpyupapadyata iti yadyu-
cyeta vaiśvānare’pi nityatā bhavatīti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhimānaḥ |
rāddhāntastu na brūmo doṣanirharaṇānnaimittikatvam nimitta-
saṃyoga evātra śrūyate | na ca nimittaśrutirarthavādatayā śakyate
vyākhyātum | śrutyarthe saṃbhavati nārthāntarakalpanopapadyate |
darśanamuktam |

[p. 1065]

|| 7 || 1ṣaṭ‌citiḥ pūrvavattvāt || 14 ||
ṣaṣṭhīṃ citimiti pūraṇapratyayāvagamāt nimittaśrutiḥ artha-
vādatayā varṇayituṃ nyāyyeti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ kutaḥ ?
vidheyānurodhāt adhikārakalpanā nyāyyeti manyate |

rāddhāntastu satyamevaṃ yadi virodhaḥ syāt na tvatra virodhaḥ,
upadeśavivakṣayāpi pratyayārthopapatteḥ nāvaśyama(rthā)nuṣṭhānāpekṣayaiva
bhagavataḥ pāṇineḥ pūraṇasūtraṃ śakyate varṇayitum | tasmāda-

[p. 1066]

virodhaḥ | avirodhe ca na nimittaśratiḥ tyaktuṃ pāryate |
tasmānnaimittikatvaṃ siddham | darśanopanyāsaparihārāvuktau |

|| 8 || 1pitṛyajñaḥ svakālatvādanaṅgaṃ syāt || 19 ||
phalakalpanāto varaṃ lakṣaṇeti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ |
darśādhikārānupraveśe sati nādhikārāntaraṃ śakyate kalpayitu-
mityuktaṃ bhavati | tasmādaṅgataivāmāvāsyāyāḥ pitṛyajñasya |
atrocyate-naikaniyogatā saṃbhavati amāvāsyāyāmiva kālasaṃyogā-

[p. 1067]

viśeṣāt piṇḍapitṛyajñasya | nāmāvāsyāyāṃ śrautaḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñe
ca lakṣaṇeti viśeṣo’vagamyate | ubhayatra kālasaṃyogasya saṃbhavāt |
tasmātpūrvāparaśrutistatkālalakṣaṇetyavirodhaḥ | prayojanamuktam |

|| 9 || paśvaṅgaṃ raśanā syāt tadāgame vidhānāt || 21 ||

[p. 1068]

atrāpi ktvāpratyayasya kriyāntarāpekṣitvāt ekaniyoga-
tāṃ pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | tatra ca paśvarthataiva prāpnoti yūpasyāpi
tadarthatvāt | rāddhāntavādī tu samānakartṛkatāmātraṃ ktvāpratyayāda-
vagamyate na niyogaikyamiti yūpasyaḥ punaḥ dvitīyātaḥ prādhānyameva
yuktamiti rāddhāntitavān |

nanu caikaniyogādṛte samānakartṛkataiva na saṃbhavati | satyaṃ
na saṃbhavati kintu adhikāraikyāt siddhaṃ samānakartṛkatvam |

[p. 1069]

nanu ca kālalakṣaṇā naivātra saṃbhavati | uccāraṇakālavibhāgo hi
ktvāpratyayotpattau heturityuktam | satyamuktaṃ, sa evoccāraṇakālo-
’nuṣṭhānepi lakṣaṇayocyate | sarvatra ca kālavidhāviyameva prati-
pattirloke vede ca | samānakartṛkatāpi naiva tatrādriyate kāla-
niṣṭhatvānniyogasya | ata eva ca darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiṣṭvā somena
yajeteti samānakartṛkatācodyaṃ parihṛtaṃ bhavati | prayojanamuktam |

|| 10 || 1svaruścāpyekadeśatvāt || 15 ||
yūpasya svarupamiti svarumān yūpa iti gomānitivat
tādarthyaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | dṛṣṭo hi tādarthye sati

[p. 1070]

matvarthaḥ | evamatrāpītyabhiprāyaḥ | rāddhāntavādī tu yavamatītye-
vamādiṣu anaikāntyāt paśumanaktītī dvitīyānirdeśāt dṛṣṭārthaḥ-
tvācca paśvarthataiveti | ṣaṣṭhī tūtpattisaṃyogepyupapannā cetyadoṣaḥ |
prayojanamuktam |

|| 11 || darśapūrṇamāsayorijyāḥ prādhānyaviśeṣāt || 29 ||

[p. 1071]

sthitaṃ rājasūyādhikaraṇe anijyā vyudasyante | yajiśabda-
saṃyogenādhikāraśravaṇāt | yatraiva ca dhāturvartate tatraivaupapadaṃ
nāmatayā vartata iti nāmadheyavicāre sthitam | ataḥ pūrvapakṣavādī
yāgamātra’vacano’yaṃ yajiḥ yatra yajiḥ tatraivopapadaṃ vartata iti
manvāno dvivacanāntasarūpamevedamabhidhānaṃ manyate | yatpunaḥ
‘prayāje prayāje kṛṣṇalaṃ juhoti’
 iti vikṛtiṣu darśanaṃ tat katha-

[p. 1072]

masmin pakṣe bhaviṣyati ? pramāṇāntaraṃ mṛgyaṃ yatreti vā,
vyāmoho vā, karmāntaraṃ vā nāmataśca dharmagrahaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati | yatpunaḥ
ya evaṃ vidvānamāvāsyāṃ yajata iti tat prakṛtasamudāyadvayaṃ kimiti
na parigṛhyate dvivacanayogāt | uktaṃ ca dhātvarthānusāryupapadamiti |
darśaśabdāccānyatvāvagateḥ | tasmādrājasūyavadanijyaivātrāṅgamiti
sthitam | atrocyate---satyamidaṃ dhātvarthānusāryupapadamiti

[p. 1073]

tathāpi samudāyadvayasya prakṛtatvāt dvivacanasya cārthāntarānupapatteḥ |
saṃbhavati ca svārthe sarūpakalpanāyā asaṃvāt | dhātvarthānusāri-
tāyāṃ cāvirodha upapadasya | kathaṃ ? naivedamupapadaṃ adhātvarthe pravṛttaṃ,
dhātvarthapravṛttau satyāṃ na nāmārthaparityāga upapadyate | kaścāsyārthaḥ
prakṛtasamudāyadvayavacanatā | evaṃ ca tadanusāritve upapanne
nārthāntarakalpanāyāṃ pramāṇamasti | nanu ca prakṛtayāgaparityāge
nāsti pramāṇaṃ ghātoḥ; kena vā uktaṃ aparityāge pramāṇamastīti
kintūpapadārthavirodhābhidhāne nāsti pramāṇamityuktamasmābhiḥ |

[p. 1074]

yacca darśaśabdaśravaṇāt na prakṛtapratyabhijñānamastīti parihṛtaṃ
tadvākyaśeṣeṣvekārthatvadarśanāditi | śakyatvācca darśaśabde-
nāpi tadarthābhidhānasya | tasmātsūktaṃ phalavatsannidhāva-
phalaṃ tadaṅgamiti | yatpunaridamuktaṃ kasmānnānuṣaṅgo bhavatīti codyaṃ
parihṛtaṃ tadanuṣaṅgato vara[mitikartavyatā]kāṅkṣāparipūraṇamiti |
anyathā tvanavasthaivākāṅkṣāyā iti | yadyavaṃ kimiti vyavā-
yānnānuṣajyete iti sāṅgrahaṇyāṃ darśitaṃ tatrāpyayameva nyāyo yuktaḥ
vaktavyo vā viśeṣaḥ |

ayamabhidhīyate---yuktaṃ tatra prakṛtivaditikartavya-
tāyāṃ sthitāyāṃ nirākāṅkṣatvāt vyavāyādevānuṣaṅga-
niṣedhaḥ | iha punaraparyavasitatvāditikartavyatārthitāyāṃ sthitāyāṃ

[p. 1075]

pūraṇenaivaikaniyogatvaṃ yuktam | na punaradhikārāntarakalpaneti
sūktam | darśanāni coktāni | aṅgavatstutayaḥ |

|| 12 || jyotiṣṭome tulyānyaviśiṣṭaṃ hi kāraṇam || 39 ||
iha tu dvivacanādyabhāvāt na yāgamātrapratipattiḥ
śakyate vārayitum | [jyotiṣṭomaśabdasya ca nāmadheyatvāt
pravṛttau nimittaṃ abhidheyaṃ nānvayītyuktaṃ nāmadheyavicāre] |

[p. 1076]

nanu ca nāmataivātra hīyate viśeṣaṇamevaitattadā bhavati kiṃ na
śchinnam | tathāpi rāddhāntaḥ siddha eva | kintu viśeṣaṇa-
padamapi etannāmaiva arthāntarānupapatteḥ yajyarthasya viśeṣaṇa-
mātrāvagamahetutvāt | (tadetad‌dvitvamapi) | ato’nvitamapi
viśeṣaṇaniṣṭhamiti nāmaiva, arthāntarānupādānāt | stutidarśanaṃ
coktam |

iti bṛhatyāṃ caturthādhyāyasya
caturthaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b1077]

[p. b1078]

[p. 1079]

atha bṛhatyāṃ

pañcamādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1śrutilakṣaṇamānupūrvyaṃ tatpradhānatvāt || 1 ||
nanu kratvarthapuruṣārthacintāpi tatra vṛttā kimidaṃ prayojakā-
prayojakalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttamiti | ucyate---kramacintāyāmasyaivopayogāt
vakṣyamāṇaupayikatayā vṛttaṃ saṅkīrtyate na vṛttatayā | ekaprayogaprayuktanāṃ
hi kramo bhavati nāprayuktānāmiti prayojakāprayojakasaṅkīrtanam |

[p. 1080]

yadi(prayojakāprayojaka)prayuktānāṃ kramaścintyate | avidheyastarhi
kramaḥ vihitaṃ hi prayujyate nāprayuktasya vidhānam |
kena voktaṃ kramovidhīyata iti | vihitavitānamevātra
cintyate kiṃ yathāpratipannavihitavitānamanuṣṭheyaṃ utā-
niyameneti | kathaṃ punarayathāpratipannānuṣṭhānamāśaṅkyate |

[p. 1081]

viniyogaśūnyatvādvihitavitānāvagamasya | nāntarīyako hi vidheyā-
vagatau vitānapratyayaḥ | ato yaścārthādartho na sa codanārtha iti
manvānaḥ pūrvapakṣavādī aniyamaṃ manyate | nanu ca ‘adhvaryurgṛhapatiṃ
dīkṣayitvā’
 ityatra kramavidhānamevopanyastaṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa
vidheyāntarābhāvāditi vadatā | yadyevamayuktabhidam | astyevātra
vidheyāntaraṃ anenāmī dīkṣitavyā iti kartṛviśeṣavidhānam |

[p. 1082]

adhvaryuvidhāvapi niyamaḥ eva | kiṃ heyamevedaṃ bhāṣyam ?
neti vadāmaḥ | yadi ktveti paurvāparyamabhidhāne
gamyate kartṛvidhau, tadanuṣṭhānaupayikatayaiva kartṛvidhāvupa-
yujyate na saṃbandhividhāviti tanniṣṭhā śrutirityuktam |

[p. 1083]

aprayuktasyāvidheyatvameva heturuktaḥ | evaṃ tarhi hṛdayasyāgre’vadyati
atha jihvāyā atha vakṣasa ityetadapyudāharaṇaṃ atra syāt |

[p. 1084]

neti brūmaḥ | pāṭhāvagatasyāyamathaśabdo’nuvādaḥ | kartṛviśeṣavidhau
punarnānyataḥ kramo’vagantuṃ śakyate ktvāpratyayāt iti
sūktam | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ nāntarīyako’yaṃ niyamaḥ ādriyate |
na hyacodanālakṣaṇasya codanālakṣaṇanirvṛttāvaṅgatopapadyate | satyaṃ
nopapadyate; ata eva ca nānuṣṭhīyate kintvarthaprāptameva
kramāntaraṃ niruṇaddhi tasyānavagamādityucyate | ato’napekṣatva-
mivā’vidhīyamānamapyupakārāyaiva bhavatīti na doṣaḥ |

[p. 1085]

punaruktyādyāśaṅkā tūktā |

|| 2 || 1arthācca || 2 ||
evaṃ tāvat ananyaparākṣaratayā niyamitaḥ kramaḥ | idānīṃ
kāryānurodhena kramo’pi niyamyate | tatparatvādanuṣṭhānasya | tatra prada-
rśitānyudāharaṇāni | kiṃ pāthakramasya bādho’yaṃ neti cintyate | neti
brūmaḥ | idamapi kramaniyamahetukāraṇaṃ na kasyacidvirodhitayā pradarśyate

[p. 1086]

balābalacintāyāṃ tu virodhe balīyastvaṃ ca | iha punaḥ
svarūpapratipattyarthamevedamupanyastam |

|| 3 || 1aniyamo’nyatra || 3 ||
yatra kramaniyamapramāṇaṃ nāsti uktaṃ vakṣmāṇaṃ vā tatrāniyama
eva | yathā śākhāntarīyāṅgavidhānaṃ śākhāntare upasaṃhriya-
māṇam |

|| 4 || 2krameṇa vā niyamyeta kratvekatve tadguṇatvāt || 4 ||
yatra punastātparyamakṣarāṇāṃ nāsti yathā adhvaryurityādau
na ca kāryato virodhaḥ | tatra kā kathā ? tatrāpi

[p. 1087]

pāthaniyamaḥ | nanu ca pāthakramāvagatirnirmūlā | na tasyāḥ
kiñcidavagatinimittamasti padānāṃ vākyārthāvagatihetutvāt |
nirmūlā cāvagatirna pramāṇam | pūrvo dhāvatītyatra parānvaya iva |
tasmādyatheṣṭamatra kramaparigrahaṇaṃ manyāmahe | ucyate---maivaṃ vocaḥ |
vākyakrama iva vākyārthe kramo’pi śabdanibandhana eveti |
yathā na śabdādṛte śabdakramo’vagamyate nārthādṛte’rthakramaḥ | yasmiṃśca
satyeva yadavagamyate’vyabhicārāt tat tasya nimittamiti
vadāmaḥ | tasmānnaidaṃ nimittam | ata eva ca niyamakramamadhyayanaṃ

[p. 1088]

dṛṣṭārthameva bhavati | anyatra kāryaviśeṣāt | ata eva ca
laukikāḥ pāthakrameṇa dhūpādiṣu vyutkramābhidhāne pratiṣedhaṃ kurvate |
adṛṣṭārtheṣvapi darśitam | tasmādatrāpi na kramāntarakalpanā
yukteti yuktam | ata eva ca ‘adhvaryurgṛhapatiṃ dīkṣayitvā’

ityevamādiṣu tātparyamātrāt kramasiddhiḥ | nimittatvamātraṃ
hi atra parigṛhyate | tasmāt sanimittā pratipattiḥ pramāṇamityuktaṃ
pramāṇaparīkṣāyām | ato yāvatī sanimittā pratipattiḥ sā sarvaiva
kramāvadhāraṇe anyatra vā vedārthānuṣṭhānopakāriṇī pramāṇamiti
mantavyam | darśanamuktam |

[p. 1089]

|| 5 || 1pravṛttyā tulyakālānāṃ guṇānāṃ tadupakramāt || 8 ||
kimaparaṃ kāraṇaṃ kramaniyame ? pravṛttiriti brūmaḥ |
pravṛttyāpi hi kramabuddhirupajāyate | kathaṃ punavaidikatvam ?
tadarthāśrayāt | tasmāt pravṛttirapi niyamahetuḥ | yatpunaridaṃ
bhāṣyakāreṇoktaṃ “evaṃ pradhānapratyāsattirbhavati”
 iti tathā
sauryādibhiśca sāmyamāśaṅkya punarvaiṣamyamupanyastaṃ kastasyo-
payogaḥ ? nātīva kramāvadhāraṇe upayujyate | astyatropayogaḥ
pravṛttirnāma kramanirapekṣaṃ kvacidanuṣṭhānaṃ nirapekṣaṃ cedanuṣṭhānaṃ pravṛttaṃ na

[p. 1090]

punarapekṣāyāṃ (pramāṇaṃ)prayojanaṃ astīti manvānasyedamuttaraṃ
---pradhānapratyāsattiḥ sahacodanāto’vagatā vaiṣamyāt pīḍitā
bhavati | sāmye punaḥ yathābhyanujñātaiva bhavati | tatsiddhayarthaṃ
niyamo’bhyupagantavyaḥ | sa ca prathamapravṛttipūrvaka
iti tayaiva vyākhyāyate | sauryādidarśanamidānīṃ kimartham ?

[p. 1091]

yadi kramāvagatimātratayaiva krama ādriyate sauryādiṣu kaḥ
pradveṣaḥ | na khalu kaścit | anuṣṭhānānaupayikatayā tu pratītamapi
tyajyate varṇasvarūpamiva padeṣu (vākyeṣu) ekatvamiva graheṣu
ubhayatvamiva haviṣvu naitāvatā aupayikasyāpi tyāga upapadyate |

[p. 1092]

ato’pekṣitaṃ pratipannamādriyate | pratipannamapyanapekṣitamu-
pekṣaṇīyameva | nanu caivaṃ sati pratipattiranarthikā prāpnoti | kena
voktaṃ pratipattera(na) rthavattvaṃ arthavatpratipattirevārthavattve hetuḥ na
pratipattimātraṃ, nāpratipattiḥ pratipattiparyantaiveti sūktam |

|| 6 || 1sthānāccotpattisaṃyogāt || 13 ||
atra bhāṣyakāreṇa triṇavādiṣvadarśanāt samāmnāya[krama]sya
vidyamānasyāpyaviṣayatāṃ manvānaiḥ idamudāhṛtamityaviṣayatāṃ matveda-
mudāhṛtaṃ saha paśūnālabhata iti |

[p. 1093]

atra ca adhikaraṇāntarasiddhamarthamupādāya sahapaśūnā-
labhata iti savanīyasthāne sahatvamiti sthite kaḥ kramaniyama
iti cintyate kimatrāpi pravṛttireva nimittaṃ uta
nimittāntaramastīti | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī avaśyaṃbhāvini
krame yadi pravṛttirneṣyate pūrvāvagatapāṭhādeva niyama iti
manyate | rāddhāntavādī tu sahavacanena sthānasyābādhi-
tatvāt savanīyasyaiva pūrvamanuṣṭhānaṃ nyāyyamityuktavān |

[p. 1094]

yadyevamagnīṣomīyānubandhyayoḥ kā vārtā ? kathaṃ paurvāparyaṃ atra |
kimanayā’nupanyastayā cintayā ? yadvā sthānavat kālabādho’pya-
yukta iti agnīṣomīya eva pūrvam | yadyevaṃ savanīyādapi tarhi
pūrvatvaṃ bhavitumarhati | na | vihitatvāt savanīyakālasya pracayā-
vagamyatvādagnīṣomīyakālasya | vidhānārthatvācca pracayasya vihitaṃ
balīyaḥ | evaṃ tarhi balābalacinteyam (na pramāṇasyārthānniyamo
yuktaḥ) | naivaṃ, sthite pramāṇāntaratve balavadabalatvamiti sūktam |

|| 7 || 1mukhyakrameṇa cāṅgānāṃ tadarthatvāt || 14 ||

[p. 1095]

yatra yaugapadyena codanā sārasvatau bhavati iti tatra kiṃ
pramāṇāntaramasti kramaniyamasya aṅgānāṃ uta yatheṣṭā pravṛttiriti |
tantreṇoccāraṇācchāstrasya na kramaḥ pratīyate ityabhiprāyaḥ |

evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---mukhye kramadarśanāt aṅgeṣvapi kramo
yuktaḥ | evaṃ ca sati yāvadanujñātaṃ vyavadhānaṃ bhaviṣyati |
aniyame hi anujñātātikramaḥ | kramabādho na yukta iti |

|| 8 || 1 prakṛtau tu svaśabdatvādyathākramaṃ pratīyeta || 15 ||
idānīṃ pāthamukhyayorvirodhe cintyate | tatra mukhyā-

[p. b1096]

rthatvādaṅgānāṃ mukhyakramo balīyāniti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | evaṃ ca
prayogacodanānugrahaḥ pratyāsatteḥ | atrābhidhīyate---na pratītasya
bādho yuktaḥ pāṭhācca nirapekṣāt kramo’vagato’ṅgānāṃ nānyāpekṣayā
bādhituṃ yuktaḥ | nanu ca pratyāsatteḥ prādhānyācca balavattoktā |
satyamuktā---ayuktā tu sā | yathāpratītaṃ hi prayogavacano gṛhṇāti |
na prayogavacanādaṅagāvagatiḥ | aṅgaṃ sat prayogavacanena gṛhyata
ityuktam | tasmāt yathāvagamaṃ pratyāsattiḥ | na pratyāsātterava-
gamo’ṅgānāṃ, ataḥ pāṭho balīyāniti yuktam | uktaṃ ca
svaśabdatvāditi |

[p. b1097]

|| 9 || 1mantratastu virādhe syāt prayogarūpasāmarthyāt tasmā-
dutpattideśaḥ saḥ || 16 ||
mantrabrāhmaṇapāthavirodhe kiṃ balīya iti | aniyama iti prāpte
ucyate---mantrakramo balīyāniti | kutaḥ ? pāthasyānanyaparatvāt |
prayojyatvānmantrāṇām | nanu brāhmaṇepyananyapara eva pāthakramaḥ |
yatpunaḥ (kramotpattiḥ)karmotpattiḥ prayojanamityuktaṃ, vākyasya
tatprayojanaṃ na pāthakramasya | ucyate---utpattyarthaḥ sa pāthakramaḥ,
na prayogārtha ityuktaṃ bhavati | mantre punaḥ prayogārthaḥ kramo bhavatīti
balavattvam |

[p. b1098]

|| 10 || 1tadvacanādvikṛtau yathā pradhānaṃ syāt || 17 ||
kiṃ mukhyakramādvikṛtau kramaḥ uta pāthakramāt prakṛtau yathā
nirjñāto’ṅgakramaḥ vikṛtāvapi tathaiva prayoktavya iti | kiṃ yuktaṃ ?
prayogārthatvāt kramasya vikṛtau prayogāvagamāt yathāvikṛti
prayoga iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

rāddhāntastu na nirjñātakramaṃ prayogavacano bādhitumarhati |
nirjñātasya hi prayogo aṅgānāmiva kramasya(āha) | anirjñāte hi
hetvantarakalpanā na niścite | tasmādyathā prakṛtau tathā vikṛtā-
vapyaṅgānāṃ prayoga iti |

[p. b1099]

|| 11 || vikṛtiḥ prakṛtidharmatvāt tatkālā syāt yathā-
śiṣṭam || 18 ||
sadyaskāladvayahakālacintā | keyaṃ kramacintāyāṃ ? krama-
sidhyartheti brūmaḥ | sadyaskālatāyāmanyathā kramo bhavati | dvayaha-
kālatāyāṃ cānyathā | tasmād‌yukto vicāraḥ | tatra prakṛtivaditi
dvayahakālatāṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | aharantarepi savanaśrutera-
viśeṣāt | rāddhāntī tu kālāntaraśravaṇāt na prākṛtaṃ kālamapekṣata
iti sadyaskālatāṃ manyate | savanāni caikāhaḥ saṃbandhānyeva śrutyā’va-
gamyamānāni na kāraṇādṛte’harantarāpekṣāṇi yuktāni kalpayitum |

[p. b1100]

darśanamuktam |

|| 12 || aṅgānāṃ mukhyakālatvādyathoktamutkarṣe syāt || 23 ||
aśrūyamāṇānāmutkarṣavākye’pakarṣavākye ca kimityutkarṣāpa-
karṣau | satyaṃ śrutasya padārthasya utkarṣo’pakarṣo vā śrūyate na
punaruparitanamādhastyaṃ vā’kṛtveti | na cedidamavagamyate kramāda-
vagatamanuṣṭhānaṃ kena vāryate | ataḥ kimatrotkarṣavākye’śrutatvaṃ
kariṣyati | anyataḥ siddheḥ | tasmāt yuktamuktaṃ---tadādi
vā’bhisaṃbandhāt tadantamapakarṣe syāditi |

[p. b1101]

|| 13 || 1pravṛttyā kṛtakālānām || 25 ||
kimalaṅkaraṇādayo dharmāḥ praiṣottarakālaṃ kartavyā uta pāṭhādevā-
vagatāḥ saumikā iti | tatra vacanabalāt nirvāpamātrameva madhye
kartavyaṃ, ato’niyama iti prāpte abhidhīyate---prokṣaṇādayaḥ kartavyāḥ
anirjñātakartavyatā hi pāṭhānniyamyate | na punaḥ nirjñātakartavyatāṃ
niruṇaddhi pāthaḥ | praiṣāt praiṣārthakartavyatā vijñāyate | ataḥ paraṃ tu
pāṭhānniyamaḥ |

[p. b1102]

|| 14 || 1asaṃyogāttu tadeva pratikṛṣyeta || 27 ||
dīkṣāsu yūpaṃ chinattītyapakarṣaḥ śrūyate tatra tadantāśaṅkā-
nivṛttyarthamidamadhikaraṇam | abhisambandhābhāvāt | yāvadvacana-
mapakarṣa iti |

|| 15 || 2prāsaṅgikaṃ ca notkarṣedasaṃyogāt || 28 ||
prāsaṅgikepyayaṃ tulyo heturiti prāsaṅgikaṃ ca notkarṣedityuktam |

|| 16 || 3tathā’pūrvam || 29 ||

[p. b1103]

paurṇamāsyāṃ pāthakramāt kramasaṃbandho nirjñāta iti
tadapakarṣa iti tadantāpakarṣaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | rāddhāntavādī
tu na paurṇamāsīprakṛtikamāmāvāsyaṃ karmeti manvānaḥ apakarṣottara-
kālameva kramo niyamyate śeṣāṇāmiti yāvadvacanamapakarṣamuktavāt |
kāryavirodhaścābhyuccayaḥ |

|| 17 || 1sāntapanīyāṃ tūtkarṣedagnihotraṃ savanadvaiguṇyāt || 30 ||
parārthamuddhṛte’gnau prasaṅgādṛte nāgnihotraṃ hotavyaṃ
iti manvānaḥ pūrvapakṣavādī utkarṣamuktavān |

[p. b1104]

rāddhāntavādī tu nimittatvātkālasyānutkarṣaṃ manyate | kathaṃ punaḥ
kālo nimittaṃ ? tasmin sati prayogāt na punaḥ adhikāri-
viśeṣaṇatvāt |

[p. b1105]

ato nimitte sati naimittikena bhavitavyamityarthāt
prasaṅgasiddhiḥ | ato’nutkarṣaḥ |

|| 18 || ṣoḍaśī cokthyasaṃyogāt || 35 ||
ṣoḍaśinyapyaṅgakālavirodhāta kādācitkatvāccotkarṣasya anu-
tkarṣaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | rāddhāntavādī tu avaśyaṃbhāvitvā-
cchāstrātikramasya varaṃ aṅgaśāstrātikrama ityuktavān |

iti bṛhatyāṃ pañcamādhyāyasya
prathamaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b1106]

bṛhatī

pañcamādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1sannipāte pradhānānāmekaikasya guṇānāṃ sarvakarma
syāt || 1 ||
nanu ca pravṛttyā kramo niyataḥ | kimidamekaikasya kṛtsnamiti |
ucyate---sthita etasminnadhikaraṇe tat | iha punaḥ pūrvapakṣavādī
manyate sakalapadārthānuṣṭhānenāpi sahatvamupapadyata eva | kimiti
kālavyavadhānaṃ kalpyate | evamevānujñātamupapannaṃ bhavati |

[p. b1107]

rāddhāntavādī tu sahatvacodanādekaikasya padārthasya sahānuṣṭhāna-
mavagamyamānaṃ na śakyate tyaktum | atastadanurodhena vyavadhānānu-
jñānaṃ na tatparityāgeneti padārthānusamayamuktavān | adhikaraṇāntaraṃ
veti kāryavirodhāt sahatvāvagamatyāgaḥ pāthavat |

|| 3 || 1muṣṭikapālāvadānāñjanābhyañjanavapanapāvaneṣu caikena || 4 ||
asminnadhikaraṇe anāvṛttenāpi muṣṭyādinā kṛtaṃ
śāstrārthaṃ manyamānaḥ pūrvapakṣavādī padārthānusamayamuktavān |

[p. b1108]

rāddhāntavādī tu codanātaḥ padārthādhyavasānaṃ vaidikeṣu ca na lokavat
pramāṇāntarāvaseyam | ekaṃ cedaṃ śāstraṃ caturo muṣṭhīnnirvapatīti
dvirhaviṣo’vadyatīti ca | tasmācchāstrārthānusamayo nyāyya iti
manyate |

|| 4 || 1saṃyukte tu prakramādaṅgaṃ syāditarasya tadarthatvāt || 6 ||
yadi śāstrādanusamayo dvirhaviṣo’vadyatīti ekatvā-
cchāstrasya etāvataivānusamaya eva nyāyyaḥ iti pūrvapakṣaḥ |

[p. b1109]

tasmāt pradānāntamevānusametavyamiti siddham |

|| 5 || 1vacanāttu parivyāṇāntamañjanādi syāt || 7 ||
arthādyūpadhāraṇamiti pūrvapakṣavādī padārthānusamayaṃ manyate |
śāstrabhedāt śāstrārthānāṃ saha codanāt | rāddhānta-
vādī tu vihitopapattyarthatvātkramasya kāṇḍānusamayaṃ manyate |
nanu ca dṛṣṭārthatvādanyenāpi tat kriyata ityuktam |

[p. b1110]

satyaṃ yadyarthād dṛṣṭārthatā syāt | vidhau tu sati dṛṣṭepyarthe na
śāstrārtho vyatikramituṃ śakyate | darśitametat |

|| 6 || 1paśugaṇe tasya tasyāpavarjayeta paśvekatvāt || 10 ||
atrāpi śāstrabādhaṃ manvānaḥ kāṇḍānusamayaṃ manyate
pūrvapakṣavādī | rāddhāntavādī tu padārthānusamayepyabādhaṃ manvānaḥ
sahacodanānugrahāya padārthānusamaya eva nyāyya ityuktavān |

[p. b1111]

mantroccāraṇatantratā ca sahatve liṅgam |

|| 7 || 1nānābījeṣvekamulūkhalaṃ vibhavāt || 13 ||
kaḥ punaratra viśeṣo’vadānādihomaparyantānusamaya ityanenādhi-
karaṇena | na khalu kaścidviśeṣaḥ | kimarthaṃ tarhīdamadhikaraṇaṃ ?
vidhānāt padārthāvacchedo bhavatīti pūrvapakṣavādino’bhimānaḥ |

rāddhāntavādī tu vidheyānyatvena vidhānopapattau na padārthā-
vacchedāya prabhavo’stīti pūrvokta eva nyāyyaḥ | anena ca

[p. b1112]

viśeṣeṇa tadapyatrodāharaṇam |

|| 8 || 1vikāre tvanūyājānāṃ pātrabhedā’rthabhedāt syāt || 16 ||
kaḥ punarayaṃ kramacintāyāṃ pātrabhedopanyāsaḥ | kramasiddhayarthaṃ
iti brūmaḥ | bhedābhede kramānyatvadarśanāt | tatra pūrvaḥ pakṣo-
dravyavikāraśaṅkyā pātravikāraśaṅkyā ca | kālānyatvena
copapatteḥ |

[p. b1113]

rāddhāntastu dravyavikārāpatteḥ kālānyatve ca grahaṇayoḥ
sahāvagamabādhāt tadanuguṇaḥ pātrabhedo nyāyya iti siddham |

|| 9 || 1prakṛteḥ pūrvoktatvādapūrvamante syāt na hyacoditasya
śeṣāmnānam || 17 ||
vaikṛtānāṃ pāthakramaḥ prākṛtairvyavadhīyate neti cintā | vyavadhī-
yata iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | padārthānuṣṭhānena vyavadhānāvagamāt |

rāddhāntastu vaikṛtaprayogavacanāt prākṛtapadārthāvagamāt (a)
vyavadhānam | atassūktaṃ ante tu bādarāyaṇa iti |

[p. b1114]

|| 10 || 1kṛtadeśāttu pūrveṣām sadeśaḥ syāt tena pratyakṣa-
saṃyogāt nyāyamātramitarat || 21 ||
rājasūyepi videvanādayo vaikṛtena pāṭhena viruddhayamānā
nānte’nuṣṭhānamarhanti | nyāyamātramitaraditi |

etaduktaṃ bhavati---nānte’nuṣṭhānaṃ kenacitkrameṇāvagamyate |
kiṃ tarhi ? pūrvāvagamavirodhāt ante’nuṣṭhānamiti nyāyamātra-
mityucyate |

[p. b1115]

|| 11 || 1prākṛtācca purastāt || 22 ||
atrāpi prākṛtakramāvagamāt prayogacodanāta eva ante’nu-
ṣṭhānaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | punaḥ śrutistu ananyārthā kramamevā-
vagamayatīti rāddhāntaḥ |

|| 12 || 2sannipātaścedyathoktamante syāt || 23 ||

[p. b1116]

atrāpi dīkṣaṇīyāsaṅkīrtanaṃ pūrvavat kramārthaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣa-
vādī | tattu pūrvapadārthakramaparatayaivoktamiti(vopayuktamiti) |
rāddhāntavādī tu parastādyathoktaṃ nyāyyaṃ manyate | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ
pūrvasminnevopayujyate na punaḥ parasminnapīti | atrocyate---parato’na-
nyatvāvagamāt pūrvasmiṃścānyatvāvagateḥ yuktamuktam |

iti bṛhatyāṃ pañcamasya
dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b1117]

bṛhatyāṃ

pañcamādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1vivṛddhiḥ karmabhedāt pṛṣadājyavattasya tasyopadiśyeta || 1 ||
kramārtha eva pratyekasamudāyasambandhaścintyate | pratyekasambandhe
hi vyavadhānamahatvaṃ samudāyasambandhe tvalpatvam | tasmādyuktā
cintā | kiṃ punaryuktaṃ ? pratyekamabhisaṃbandha iti | pratipradhānaṃ guṇā
āvartanta iti nyāyāt | nanu ca pṛthaktvaniveśinī saṅkhyetyuktam |
satyamuktaṃ vidhānavaśāttu yathopapadyate tathopakalpanīyam |

[p. b1118]

ata eva ‘pañcadaśānyājyāni’
 iti ca pratyekamabhisaṃbandhaḥ |
atrocyate---na vidhānaṃ padārthamanyathā kartumutsahate |
padārthānurodhī hi vidhiḥ na vidhyanurodhinaḥ padārthāḥ | ‘agninā
siñcet’
 ityevamādiṣu darśitam | tasmāt yāvatpṛthaktvaṃ
tāvadabhyāsakalpanā na sambhavatīti samudāyābhisambandhaḥ |
pṛṣadājye tvetadviparītaṃ iti pratyekamabhisaṃbandhaḥ | yadapyuktaṃ
pañcadaśānyājyānīti āvṛttau ḍapratyayaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ |

|| 2 || 1svasthānāttu vivṛddhayeran kṛtānupūrvyatvāt || 3 ||

[p. b1119]

saṅkhyāsampattyarthamabhyase prāpte idaṃ cintyate---kiṃ daṇḍakalita-
vadabhyāsaḥ uta svasthāna iti | samudāyābhisambandhāt daṇḍakalita-
vadabhyāsammanyate pūrvapakṣavādī | pūrvapratītakramānurodhāt svasthāne
yukta iti rāddhāntaḥ | samudāyasaṃbandhasya tūbhayathāpyavirodhaḥ |

|| 3 || 1samidhyamānavatīṃ samiddhavatīṃ ka cāntareṇa dhāyyāḥ syuḥ
dyāvāpṛthivyorantarāle samarhaṇāt || 4 ||
nanu vacane sati pūrvapakṣa eva na sambhavati | arthavādapūrvakaṃ
tadvacanamiti pūrvapakṣavādī nyāyānusāreṇāpi gacchatyeveti manyate |

[p. b1120]

rāddhāntastu nāyathārthagrahaṇe pramāṇamastyarthavādasya iti na śabdārthaṃ
nyāyo niruṇaddhi iti nyāyamātraṃ tadityuktam | ato yathārtha-
vādamevedaṃ vidhānaṃ nyāyyaṃ, dhāyyāśabdanirūpaṇaṃ tu prāsaṅgikam |
pradarśitaṃ ca tat |

|| 4 || 1stomavivṛddhau bahiṣpavamāne purastāt paryāsādāgantavaḥ
syuḥ tathāhi dṛṣṭaṃ dvādaśāhe || 7 ||
nyāyamātraṃ vacanaṃ bādhituṃ na kṣamata ityuktam | vacanaṃ
cedaṃ vṛṣaṇvantastṛcā bhavanti tṛca uttamaḥ paryāsa iti |
nanu dvādaśāhe etadvacanaṃ, atadvikāreṣu keyamāśaṅkā |

[p. b1121]

uttamaśabdaśravaṇāt1 | kimidamuttamaśabdaśravaṇāditi ? uttamaḥ
paryāso bhavatīti padārthāṅgatāṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | nanu ca
padārthāṅgatve ānarthakyamuktam | na tat paryāse bhavitumarhati |
sarvasya paryāsasyārthavattvāt kratusaṃbandhinī hi paryāsasaṃjñā
udgīthavata | tasmādyuktamuktam | dvādaśāhavikṛtiṣvapi nirā-
karaṇīya evāyamartha ityupanyāsaḥ |

[p. b1122]

atrābhidhīyate-nāyaṃ padārthadharmaḥ | kastarhi ? kratudharmaḥ | kathaṃ ?
prakaraṇāt dvādaśāhaniyogaikyamavagamyate | na kratvantaraniyogai-
kyam | nanu ca padārthānusārī niyogaḥ | padārthānusārepyatrā-
virodhāt | tasmānna kratvantarasaṃbandhakalpanāyāṃ pramāṇamasti |
dvādaśāhavikāreṣvevaitat syāt | na vā tatrāpi | atha vā
vṛṣaṇvatāṃ dharmaḥ | nanu ca padārthadharmo na bhavatītyuktam, satyamuktaṃ,

[p. b1123]

na punaḥ kratvarthāparityāgeneti | tasmātsūktaṃ tadvikāreṣvapi na
bhavatīti |

|| 5 || 1ante tūttarayordadhyāt || 13 ||
kathamatra pūrvapakṣo yuktaḥ, vacanamadṛṣ‌ṭvetyayuktamidam | adhīta-
vedasya hi dharmajijñāsā | ayaṃ tarhi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | idamapi dvādaśāha
eva vacanam atra viśeṣāgrahaṇāt tadvikāreṣu bhavatu ata-
dvikāreṣu mābhūditi pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

[p. b1124]

atrocyate---āvāpanibandhanametadvacanaṃ yāvadāvāpaṃ bhavitu-
marhati | kathaṃ tarhi prakṛtaniyogaikyaṃ ? na, kāryāśritatvādasya
vidheḥ vṛṣaṇvatsūpadeśāśraya ityuktam |

|| 6 || 1graheṣṭakamaupānuvākyaṃ savanacitiśeṣaḥ syāt || 15 ||
atrāpyaṅgāṅgicintā kramārthaiva | kathamatra pūrvapakṣaḥ ?

[p. b1125]

atrocyate---kāryānupraveśitvānniyogārthasya niyogasya | citi-
savananiṣpattyā cānupraveśasya | tasmāccitisavanaśeṣo’yamiti
pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ |

atrocyate---upadeśasiddhayarthaṃ hi kāryaṃ na kārya-
siddhayartha upadeśaḥ yāgāgrisaṃbandhaścāyaṃ upadeśaḥ | nanu
ca nyāyapaunaruktyamidaṃ aruṇayā krīṇātīti darśitam |

[p. b1126]

tatrāsambhavādapi bhavantīti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | tannirā-
karaṇārthamidam | prayojanamuktam |

|| 7 || 1ante syuravyavāyāta || 17 ||
nanu vacane sati atra pūrvapakṣo nopapadyate | na
nopapadyate | sarvā brāhmaṇavatya ityanuvādaṃ manyate | evaṃ
sati anyā anyā iti darśanamupapannaṃ bhavati

[p. b1127]

iti | evaṃ prāpte abhidhīyate | brāhmaṇavatyaḥ śrutiviniyuktāḥ pari-
gṛhyante | kimiti---mantraviniyojyatvāccheṣāṇām | samākhyā
punarlaukikyevetyuktam | tasmānmadhyamāyāṃ citāvupadhātavya
iti | nanu ca mantreṇāpi viniyujyamānāḥ brāhmaṇenaiva viniyuktā
na svatantraliṅgapramāṇamityuktam | viśeṣābhiprāyeṇedamuktam
vacanasyārthavattvāyetyadoṣaḥ | darśane tu mantrābhiprāyeṇānyatvaṃ
varṇanīyam |

|| 8 || 1prāglokaṃ pṛṇāyāstasyāssampūraṇārthatvāt || 20 ||
nanu cānte vādarāyaṇa iti nopapadyate, lokaṃpṛṇāpi
brāhmaṇavatyeva |

[p. b1128]

nanu ca mantravidhānārthaṃ tadvacanam | nanu ca sarvā eva
mantradvāṃreṇa codyante | satyaṃ; tathāpīṣṭakābhidhānapradhāno’yaṃ nirdeśaḥ
‘citriṇīrupadadhāti’
 ityevamādiṣu | iha punaḥ ‘lokaṃ pṛṇa chidraṃ
pṛṇa’
 iti mantrapradhāno’yaṃ nirdeśa iti laiṅgikyeveyam | ante tu
bādarāyaṇa iti yuktam | rāddhāntastu mantrārthavādānyathānupapatteḥ
laiṅgikā api prāgeva brāhmaṇavatya iti yuktam |

[p. b1129]

|| 9 || saṃskṛte karma saṃskārāṇāṃ tadarthatvāt || 21 ||
āhitāgneradhikāraśravaṇāta ādhānena cāgnīnāmutpatteḥ
bhavatyanantaramadhikāra iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | ādhānānantarakramasiddhiścā-
nena prakāreṇoktā | yathā ‘āhitāgnirnaklinnaṃ dārvabhyādadhyāta’

ityevamādīnām | evaṃ prāpte’bhidhīyate---ubhayamapyagnyarthamityuktam
āhavanīyādimukhenāhitāgneradhikāraḥ | na ceṣṭibhirvinā’’havanī-
yo bhavati iti na karmasvadhikāraḥ | darśanaṃ punaḥ agnyarthasyaiva
homasya |

[p. b1130]

|| 10 || 1sañcite tvagnicidyuktaṃ prāpaṇānnimittasya || 26 ||
ihāpi cayanānantaraṃ vrataprāptiṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī |
atrocyate---nedamāhitāgninā tulyam | tatra hi agnyarthamādhānam
iha punaḥ agnikāryārthaṃ cayanaṃ, prokṣaṇamiva vrīhikāryārtham | kāryaṃ
ca kṛtsnakratuprayogasādhyamiti kratusamāptāvevāgniciditi | na
cāgnicayananimittā hyete niyamāḥ kratoravagamyante bhedanahomavat

[p. b1131]

kṛtaprayogasyāgnicidabhidhānāt | tasmātkratvante niyamā iti
siddham |

|| 11 || 1pareṇāvedanāddīkṣitaḥ syāt sarvairdīṃkṣābhisambandhāt || 29 ||
atrāpi dīkṣitapadārthakramasiddhayarthamidaṃ cintyate | kiyadbhi-
rdīkṣito bhavati iti | yāvatā dīkṣābhisambandhaḥ tāvadbhirdīkṣito
bhavatīti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | sarveṣāmutpattyarthatvāt yathā’’dhānena
pavamāneṣṭibhiścāgnayaḥ | anyathā hi vikalpaḥ syāt
pratyekaṃ dīkṣābhinirvṛtteḥ | ata evābhidhānamapyuttarakāla-
meva | atrocyate---dīkṣiṣyamāṇa ityadhikāripadamidam |

[p. b1132]

na cādhikārāniṣpatterviṣayasiddhiriti ṣaṣṭhe vakṣyāmaḥ | tasmādiṣṭereva
dīkṣitatvasiddhiḥ | samuccayaścaivamupapanno bhavati | utpattisaṃskāra-
bhedāt | sarveṣāmutpasyarthatvāta anapekṣaśravaṇācca | kathaṃ tarhi
ādhānapavamāneṣṭīnāṃ samuccayaḥ ? prayojanāntarābhāvāt na tatra
tayorgrāhakaṃ kiñcidasti | iha punarjyotiṣṭomavidheḥ vikalpaḥ
tulyarthānām | tasmādasmatpakṣa eva samuccayasiddhiḥ | na
cābhidhānābhāvādarthābhāvaḥ śakyate vaktumiti darśitam |

[p. b1133]

|| 12 || 1aṅgavatkratūnāmānupūrvyama || 32 ||
nanu cāvaśyaṃbhāvini krame pāṭhānniyama uktaḥ | kratvantaravaditi
ca vyatireko varṇitaḥ bhinnaprayogavacanatvānnaiṣāṃ krama upapadyata
iti | kathamatra pūrvapakṣaḥ ? ucyate---anekānuṣṭhānanibandhano’yaṃ
kramaḥ | anekaṃ ca kāmyaṃ kurvataḥ kāminaḥ yuktaḥ kramaniyama iti
pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | kathaṃ tarhi sarvasvāre pāthakramaḥ ? arthā-
dbādho bhaviṣyatītyadoṣaḥ | atrābhidhīyate---vidhivitāna-
cinteyam na puruṣecchāvitānacintā, acodanālakṣaṇatvāttasya

[p. b1134]

ekasya cādhikāravidheraṅgavidhivitānasaṃbandho nādhikāramapekṣate
saṃbandhābhāvāt | asaṃbandhasya ca śābdataiva na śakyate vaktum |
na ca sarvakāmasyādhikāraḥ yogasiddhayadhikaraṇe pratyuktatvāt |
arthasya sambandhaviṣayatvāta | tasmāta kāmyeṣvaniyamaḥ |

[p. b1135]

|| 13 || ya etenetyagniṣṭomaḥ prakaraṇāt || 37 ||
uparitanasyādhikaraṇasya viṣayapradarśanārthamidamadhikaṇam |
agniṣṭomapradarśanādityuktam |

|| 14 || 1athānyeneti saṃsthānāṃ sannidhānāt || 39 ||
ya etenetyagniṣṭomasaṃsthasyāvamarśāt anyeneti saṃsthāntarāva-
marśaṃ manyate pūrvapakṣavādī | rāddhāntavādī tu eṣa vāva prathamo
yajño yajñānāmiti yajñaśabdaśruteḥ yajñāntaramevānyaśabdena

[p. b1136]

pratīyata ityuktavān | agniṣṭomasaṃsthasya nityatvāt ya etene-
tyucyate | tathā cāpattivihārāvupapannau bhavataḥ | āpattyākṣepa-
samādhānaṃ ca darśitam |

|| 15 || 1ekastobhe vā kratusaṃyogāta || 43 ||
ekastomānāṃ vākyaśeṣādanyeneti grahaṇam | na cānyenetya-
nyatvamātrāvagateḥ | nanu ca vākyaśeṣādviśeṣāvagatirityuktam |

[p. b1137]

sandigdheṣu vākyaśeṣānnirṇaya ityuktam | iha tu asandigdhaiva
jyotiṣṭomādanyeṣvavagatiriti arthavādamātrametata |

iti bṛhatyāṃ pañcamādhyāyasya
tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ

[p. b1138]

bṛhatyāṃ

pañcamādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||

|| 1 || 1kramakopo’rthaśabdābhyāṃ śrutiviśeṣādarthaparatvācca || 1 ||
atra balābalacintā | pūrvapakṣavādino’bhiprāyaḥ avidheya-
tvātkramasya avagateśca tulyatvādvikalpa iti | rāddhāntastu
vidheyavirodhādavagaterevātulyatvamiti śrutyarthayoreva tu balavattvam |
atra śrutyarthayorvirodhe kathaṃ ? naivodāharaṇamasti | artha eva

[p. b1139]

balīyān | kāryaṃ hyatrārthaśabdenocyate | kāryārthaśca kramaḥ | ataḥ
śrutimapabādhate kāryaviṣayatvāttasyāḥ |

|| 2 || avadānābhidhāraṇāsādaneṣvānupūrvyaṃ pravṛttyā syāt || 2 ||
atrāpi balābalacintaiva | pravṛtteḥ pūrvapravṛttatvāta pravṛtti-
rbalīyasīti pūrvapakṣavādī manyate | kāryārthatvātkramasya avadānasya ca
pradānopakramatvāt mukhyakramo balīyāniti rāddhāntaḥ | darśana-
maktam |

|| 3 || 1vacanādiṣṭipūrvatvam || 5 ||

[p. b1140]

atrāpyakṣaraparaṃ kramamaṅgīkṛtya cintā | tatra pūrvapakṣavādī
somena yakṣyamāṇa’ iti nākṣaraparatā kramasya sambhavatīti manvānaḥ,
iṣṭipūrvatāmevāha | kathaṃ punarna saṃbhavati ? ādhānavidhānaparatvā-
dvākyasya | darśapūrṇamāsāviṣ‌ṭveti tu kālavidhānātsiddhiḥ | atrā-
bhidhīyate---yeyamadyatanī vibhaktiḥ sā nādhānānantaryādṛte
sambhavatītyarthāt atrāpi kramasiddhiḥ somādhānayoḥ | kva
punariyaṃ na sambhavati ? adhikāre | adhikārāyattañca vidhānaṃ

[p. b1141]

ityādhānavidhānepyarthāta kramākṣepaḥ | abhyuccayo darśitaḥ |

|| 4 || 1utkarṣādvā brāhmaṇasya somaḥ syāt || 10 ||
asminnadhikaraṇe pūrvādhikaraṇārthasya viṣayaviśeṣe avasthā-
panam | yadanyattatprāsaṅgikameva | yaḥ punarayamantarāgarbhiṇyupanyāsaḥ
sa kimarthaḥ ? evamartho’sau | yadyanantaraṃ somādhānaṃ tadā
brāhmaṇasya kalpadvayaṃ sambhavati | anyasya vā svakāle
punariṣṭipūrvakatvamevati niyamyate |

[p. b1142]

kathaṃ punaridamavagamyate brāhmaṇasyaiva kalpadvayamiti
rāddhāntastu iṣṭipūrvatve haviṣa utkarṣāt tasmāt somā-
dhānaṃ tasyaiva | anyathā hi brāhmaṇasyaivotkarṣe’napekṣitārtha-
vidhānaṃ syāt | śeṣamuktam |

[p. b1143]

|| 6 || 1puroḍāśastvanirdeśe tad‌yukte devatābhāvāt || 19 ||
ekasya haviṣa utkarṣa iti sthite tadviśeṣāvadhāraṇāyedama-
dhikaraṇam | uktaṃ ca |

|| 7 || vikṛteḥ prakṛtikālatvāt sadyaskālottarā vikṛtiḥ
tayoḥ pratyakṣaśiṣṭatvāt || 12 ||
pūrvaṃ nyāyato’sambhavamuktvā tasminnevāhani samāptyupakramau
darśitavān sambhavārtham |

[p. 1144]

sadyaskālataiva vikṛtīnāmityuktavān |

|| 8 || sānnāyyāgnīṣomīyavikārāḥ ūrdhvaṃ somāt prakṛtivat || 25 ||
|| 9 || tathā somavikārāḥ darśapūrṇamāsābhyās || 26 ||
ihādhikaraṇadvayepyadhikārādṛte kālalakṣaṇā na sambhavatīti
kṛtādhikārasyaiva kramasyādhikāra iti tadvikārāṇāmapyevameveti
yuktam | kimarthaṃ tarhi adhikaraṇadvayam ? pūrvatra kartṛtānirdeśāt
nāsomayājīti somayāgasya kartā sānnāyye kartṛtayā’bhidhīyate

[p. 1145]

dvitīyādhikaraṇe tu darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiṣṭveti punarāśaṅkitavān
kartṛnirdeśābhāvāt | kathaṃ tarhi rāddhānte nyāyatulyatā ? nādhi-
kārādṛte kālalakṣaṇā sambhavatīti tulyatoktā |

iti bṛhatyāṃ pañcamādhyāyasya
caturthaḥ pādaḥ
